// FILE: README.md
======================================================================
# NICH Employee Attendance 3 (Android)

Enterprise biometric employee attendance terminal built with **Kotlin**, **Jetpack Compose (Material 3)**, **CameraX + ML Kit Face Detection**, **Offline-First Local Persistence**, and **Firebase Cloud Firestore** (`attendanceapp-f6a0e`).

## Core Features

- **Strict Terminal Mode (Zero Admin/Logs/Login UI):** Launches directly into the dedicated VIP Biometric Attendance terminal with zero Google Sign-In, zero Admin DB tabs, zero Logs tabs, and zero manual enrollment screens. All existing employee directory data in local storage (`nich_offline_attendance`) remains intact and active.
- **Enforced Full-Face Biometric Scanner:** Tapping **VIP ATTENDANCE** forces open the CameraX + ML Kit scanner (`PreviewView.ImplementationMode.COMPATIBLE`, `PERFORMANCE_MODE_ACCURATE` + full landmarks, contours, and classification) to analyze eyes, nose bridge, philtrum/mustache region, mouth, and beard/jawline structure across an 8-dimensional biometric signature.
- **Conditional Fallback Only:** There is zero bypass to skip the camera. The backup employee code keypad (`147` / `369`) unlocks **only** after CameraX runs complete 2-pass processing and the facial recognition engine explicitly rejects the scan (or if hardware camera initialization fails).
- **Pre-Registered Employee Profile:**
  - **Name:** محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar) (`NICH-0147`)
  - **Check-In Code:** `147` (forces `CHECK_IN`)
  - **Check-Out Code:** `369` (forces `CHECK_OUT`)
  - **Reference Portrait:** `app/src/main/res/drawable/emp_muhammad_ansar.jpg`
- **Automated Time-Window Rules:** Standard passcodes and face scans automatically classify morning hours (`< 12:00`) as `CHECK_IN` and `12:00+` as `CHECK_OUT`.
- **Automatic PDF Attendance Email Report:** On every verified Check-In or Check-Out (via Face Scan or conditional backup code `147`/`369`), the app generates a structured A4 PDF report and dispatches it asynchronously in the background via SMTP TLS to `Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com` without interrupting the terminal flow. Configure `SMTP_EMAIL` and `SMTP_APP_PASSWORD` (Gmail App Password) in the **Secrets panel in AI Studio** (or `.env` locally).

## Firebase Configuration (`attendanceapp-f6a0e`)

- `app/google-services.json` is configured for Firebase project **`attendanceapp-f6a0e`** (`applicationId = "com.aistudio.nichattendance.vtxkqm"`).
- `app/src/main/res/values/firebase_applet_config.xml` specifies the named Firestore database ID.
- `firestore.rules` enforces authenticated per-operator data isolation and strict schema validation for background cloud sync.

## Building the Debug APK Locally

1. **Prerequisites:** JDK 17+, Android SDK (API 36), and **Gradle 9.3.1**.
2. **Prepare the debug signing keystore** (required by `signingConfigs.debugConfig` in `app/build.gradle.kts`). If `debug.keystore.base64` is present, decode it; otherwise generate a standard debug keystore with `keytool`:
   ```bash
   if [ -f debug.keystore.base64 ]; then
     base64 -d debug.keystore.base64 > debug.keystore
   elif [ ! -f debug.keystore ]; then
     keytool -genkeypair -v -keystore debug.keystore -storepass android -alias androiddebugkey -keypass android -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 -dname "CN=Android Debug,O=Android,C=US"
   fi
   ```
3. **Run unit tests and compile the Debug APK:**
   ```bash
   gradle :app:testDebugUnitTest \
     --tests 'com.example.ExampleUnitTest' \
     --tests 'com.example.ExampleRobolectricTest' \
     --tests 'com.example.data.model.AttendanceLogicTest' \
     --tests 'com.example.data.local.OfflineAttendanceStoreTest' \
     --tests 'com.example.data.report.AttendanceReportTest' \
     --tests 'com.example.ui.viewmodel.AttendanceViewModelStrictTest'
   gradle :app:assembleDebug
   ```
4. The installable APK is generated at:
   `app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk`

## GitHub Actions CI

`.github/workflows/android-build.yml` automatically prepares `debug.keystore` (from `secrets.DEBUG_KEYSTORE_BASE64`, `debug.keystore.base64`, or `keytool`), restores `app/google-services.json` from `secrets.GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON` if provided, runs unit and Robolectric tests, builds `app-debug.apk` with Gradle 9.3.1, and publishes `nich-attendance-3-debug-apk` as a workflow artifact on every push and pull request.

> **Important GitHub Push Note:** Automated GitHub exports may exclude `app/google-services.json`. The build succeeds even without it, and if you want CI-generated APKs to include live Firebase (`attendanceapp-f6a0e`) sync, either upload `app/google-services.json` into your GitHub repository or add its contents as a GitHub Repository Secret named `GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON`.

  alias(libs.plugins.google.services) apply false

// FILE: firestore.rules
======================================================================
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    // Global Safety Net: default-deny catch-all
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isValidEmployee(data, userId) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId == userId &&
             data.employeeCode is string && data.employeeCode.size() >= 2 && data.employeeCode.size() <= 32 &&
             data.name is string && data.name.size() >= 1 && data.name.size() <= 80 &&
             data.department is string && data.department.size() >= 1 && data.department.size() <= 80 &&
             data.roleTitle is string && data.roleTitle.size() >= 1 && data.roleTitle.size() <= 80 &&
             data.passcode is string && data.passcode.size() >= 3 && data.passcode.size() <= 12 &&
             data.checkInCode is string && data.checkInCode.size() <= 12 &&
             data.checkOutCode is string && data.checkOutCode.size() <= 12 &&
             data.avatarKey is string && data.avatarKey.size() >= 1 && data.avatarKey.size() <= 64 &&
             data.faceSignature is string && data.faceSignature.size() <= 512 &&
             data.isActive is bool &&
             data.createdAt is timestamp && data.createdAt <= request.time &&
             data.updatedAt is timestamp && data.updatedAt <= request.time;
    }

    function isValidAttendanceLog(data, userId) {
      return data.userId is string && data.userId == userId &&
             isValidId(data.employeeId) &&
             data.employeeCode is string && data.employeeCode.size() >= 2 && data.employeeCode.size() <= 32 &&
             data.employeeName is string && data.employeeName.size() >= 1 && data.employeeName.size() <= 80 &&
             data.department is string && data.department.size() >= 1 && data.department.size() <= 80 &&
             data.avatarKey is string && data.avatarKey.size() >= 1 && data.avatarKey.size() <= 64 &&
             (data.eventType == 'CHECK_IN' || data.eventType == 'CHECK_OUT') &&
             (data.verificationMethod == 'FACE_SCAN_1' || data.verificationMethod == 'FACE_SCAN_2' || data.verificationMethod == 'PASSCODE') &&
             data.confidenceScore is int && data.confidenceScore >= 0 && data.confidenceScore <= 100 &&
             data.loggedTimeLabel is string && data.loggedTimeLabel.size() >= 1 && data.loggedTimeLabel.size() <= 32 &&
             data.loggedDateKey is string && data.loggedDateKey.size() >= 8 && data.loggedDateKey.size() <= 16 &&
             data.createdAt is timestamp && data.createdAt <= request.time;
    }

    match /users/{userId}/employees/{employeeId} {
      allow get: if isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId && isValidId(employeeId);
      allow list: if isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;

      allow create: if isSignedIn() &&
                       request.auth.uid == userId &&
                       isValidId(employeeId) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasAll([
                         'userId', 'employeeCode', 'name', 'department', 'roleTitle',
                         'passcode', 'checkInCode', 'checkOutCode', 'avatarKey',
                         'faceSignature', 'isActive', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                         'userId', 'employeeCode', 'name', 'department', 'roleTitle',
                         'passcode', 'checkInCode', 'checkOutCode', 'avatarKey',
                         'faceSignature', 'isActive', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
                       ]) &&
                       isValidEmployee(incoming(), userId);

      allow update: if isSignedIn() &&
                       request.auth.uid == userId &&
                       isValidId(employeeId) &&
                       isValidEmployee(incoming(), userId) &&
                       incoming().userId == existing().userId &&
                       incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt &&
                       incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
                         'employeeCode', 'name', 'department', 'roleTitle',
                         'passcode', 'checkInCode', 'checkOutCode', 'avatarKey',
                         'faceSignature', 'isActive', 'updatedAt'
                       ]);

      allow delete: if isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId && isValidId(employeeId);
    }

    match /users/{userId}/attendanceLogs/{logId} {
      allow get: if isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId && isValidId(logId);
      allow list: if isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;

      allow create: if isSignedIn() &&
                       request.auth.uid == userId &&
                       isValidId(logId) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasAll([
                         'userId', 'employeeId', 'employeeCode', 'employeeName',
                         'department', 'avatarKey', 'eventType', 'verificationMethod',
                         'confidenceScore', 'loggedTimeLabel', 'loggedDateKey', 'createdAt'
                       ]) &&
                       incoming().keys().hasOnly([
                         'userId', 'employeeId', 'employeeCode', 'employeeName',
                         'department', 'avatarKey', 'eventType', 'verificationMethod',
                         'confidenceScore', 'loggedTimeLabel', 'loggedDateKey', 'createdAt'
                       ]) &&
                       isValidAttendanceLog(incoming(), userId) &&
                       exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId)/employees/$(incoming().employeeId));

      allow update, delete: if false;
    }
  }
}


======================================================================
// FILE: firestore.test.js
======================================================================
const {
  initializeTestEnvironment,
  assertFails,
  assertSucceeds,
} = require("@firebase/rules-unit-testing");
const { test, before, after, beforeEach } = require("node:test");
const fs = require("node:fs");

let testEnv;
const PROJECT_ID = process.env.GCP_PROJECT || "demo-no-project";
const ALICE_UID = "alice_admin_123";
const BOB_UID = "bob_admin_456";

const [emulatorHost, emulatorPortStr] = (
  process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST || "127.0.0.1:8085"
).split(":");
const emulatorPort = parseInt(emulatorPortStr, 10);

function validEmployee(userId) {
  const now = new Date();
  return {
    userId,
    employeeCode: "NICH-0147",
    name: "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
    department: "Executive Operations",
    roleTitle: "Authorized Staff",
    passcode: "147",
    checkInCode: "147",
    checkOutCode: "369",
    avatarKey: "avatar_ansar",
    faceSignature: "0.42:0.51:0.38:0.29",
    isActive: true,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  };
}

function validAttendanceLog(userId, employeeId) {
  return {
    userId,
    employeeId,
    employeeCode: "NICH-1042",
    employeeName: "Dr. Ananya Rao",
    department: "Biometric Systems",
    avatarKey: "avatar_1",
    eventType: "CHECK_IN",
    verificationMethod: "FACE_SCAN_1",
    confidenceScore: 98,
    loggedTimeLabel: "09:15:00 AM",
    loggedDateKey: "2026-11-13",
    createdAt: new Date(),
  };
}

before(async () => {
  const rules = fs.readFileSync("./firestore.rules", "utf8");
  testEnv = await initializeTestEnvironment({
    projectId: PROJECT_ID,
    firestore: {
      rules,
      host: emulatorHost,
      port: emulatorPort,
    },
  });
});

after(async () => {
  if (testEnv) {
    await testEnv.cleanup();
  }
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  if (testEnv) {
    await testEnv.clearFirestore();
  }
});

test("Unauthenticated user: cannot list or write employees or attendanceLogs", async () => {
  const unauthDb = testEnv.unauthenticatedContext().firestore();
  await assertFails(unauthDb.collection(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees`).get());
  await assertFails(unauthDb.collection(`users/${ALICE_UID}/attendanceLogs`).get());
  await assertFails(
    unauthDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1`).set(validEmployee(ALICE_UID))
  );
});

test("Owner: can create, read, update, and delete employee with valid schema", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();
  const empRef = aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1042`);

  await assertSucceeds(empRef.set(validEmployee(ALICE_UID)));
  await assertSucceeds(empRef.get());
  await assertSucceeds(aliceDb.collection(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees`).get());
  await assertSucceeds(
    empRef.update({
      department: "Quantum Security",
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
  await assertSucceeds(empRef.delete());
});

test("Cross-user isolation: Bob cannot read or write Alice employees or logs", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();
  const bobDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(BOB_UID).firestore();

  await assertSucceeds(
    aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1042`).set(validEmployee(ALICE_UID))
  );

  await assertFails(bobDb.collection(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees`).get());
  await assertFails(bobDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1042`).get());
  await assertFails(
    bobDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_9999`).set(validEmployee(BOB_UID))
  );
  await assertFails(
    bobDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1042`).delete()
  );
});

test("Dirty Dozen: rejects ghost field, short passcode, and immutable field mutation", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();
  const empRef = aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1042`);

  await assertFails(
    aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_ghost`).set({
      ...validEmployee(ALICE_UID),
      isAdmin: true,
    })
  );

  await assertFails(
    aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_short`).set({
      ...validEmployee(ALICE_UID),
      passcode: "12",
    })
  );

  await assertSucceeds(empRef.set(validEmployee(ALICE_UID)));
  await assertFails(
    empRef.update({
      userId: BOB_UID,
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
  );
});

test("AttendanceLog: requires existing employee, validates enums, and blocks updates/deletes", async () => {
  const aliceDb = testEnv.authenticatedContext(ALICE_UID).firestore();

  // Orphaned log without existing employee must fail
  await assertFails(
    aliceDb
      .doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/attendanceLogs/log_orphan`)
      .set(validAttendanceLog(ALICE_UID, "emp_missing"))
  );

  // Create employee first
  await assertSucceeds(
    aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/employees/emp_1042`).set(validEmployee(ALICE_UID))
  );

  // Invalid enum eventType must fail
  await assertFails(
    aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/attendanceLogs/log_bad_enum`).set({
      ...validAttendanceLog(ALICE_UID, "emp_1042"),
      eventType: "LUNCH_BREAK",
    })
  );

  // Valid attendance log create succeeds
  const logRef = aliceDb.doc(`users/${ALICE_UID}/attendanceLogs/log_001`);
  await assertSucceeds(logRef.set(validAttendanceLog(ALICE_UID, "emp_1042")));
  await assertSucceeds(aliceDb.collection(`users/${ALICE_UID}/attendanceLogs`).get());

  // Append-only: update and delete must fail
  await assertFails(logRef.update({ eventType: "CHECK_OUT" }));
  await assertFails(logRef.delete());
});

# When configured, Gradle will run in incubating parallel mode.
# This option should only be used with decoupled projects. For more details, visit
# https://developer.android.com/r/tools/gradle-multi-project-decoupled-projects
org.gradle.parallel=true
# Kotlin code style for this project: "official" or "obsolete":
kotlin.code.style=official
# Enables namespacing of each library's R class so that its R class includes only the
# resources declared in the library itself and none from the library's dependencies,
# thereby reducing the size of the R class for that library
android.nonTransitiveRClass=true
org.gradle.caching=true
org.gradle.configuration-cache=true
# Set the maximum number of workers to 4 to avoid overloading the machine.
org.gradle.workers.max=4
# Set the Kotlin compiler execution strategy to in-process to avoid "Could not
# connect to Kotlin compile daemon" error.
kotlin.compiler.execution.strategy=in-process
# Allow the google-services plugin to work even if the google-services.json is not present.

      <h1><a href="https://ansargold286-hash.github.io/-NICH-Employee-Attendance-3/">-NICH-Employee-Attendance-3</a></h1>
      

      <h1 id="nich-employee-attendance-3-android">NICH Employee Attendance 3 (Android)</h1>

<p>Enterprise biometric employee attendance terminal built with <strong>Kotlin</strong>, <strong>Jetpack Compose (Material 3)</strong>, <strong>CameraX + ML Kit Face Detection</strong>, <strong>Offline-First Local Persistence</strong>, and <strong>Firebase Cloud Firestore</strong> (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">attendanceapp-f6a0e</code>).</p>

<h2 id="core-features">Core Features</h2>

<ul>
  <li><strong>Strict Terminal Mode (Zero Admin/Logs/Login UI):</strong> Launches directly into the dedicated VIP Biometric Attendance terminal with zero Google Sign-In, zero Admin DB tabs, zero Logs tabs, and zero manual enrollment screens. All existing employee directory data in local storage (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">nich_offline_attendance</code>) remains intact and active.</li>
  <li><strong>Enforced Full-Face Biometric Scanner:</strong> Tapping <strong>VIP ATTENDANCE</strong> forces open the CameraX + ML Kit scanner (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">PreviewView.ImplementationMode.COMPATIBLE</code>, <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">PERFORMANCE_MODE_ACCURATE</code> + full landmarks, contours, and classification) to analyze eyes, nose bridge, philtrum/mustache region, mouth, and beard/jawline structure across an 8-dimensional biometric signature.</li>
  <li><strong>Conditional Fallback Only:</strong> There is zero bypass to skip the camera. The backup employee code keypad (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">147</code> / <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">369</code>) unlocks <strong>only</strong> after CameraX runs complete 2-pass processing and the facial recognition engine explicitly rejects the scan (or if hardware camera initialization fails).</li>
  <li><strong>Pre-Registered Employee Profile:</strong>
    <ul>
      <li><strong>Name:</strong> محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar) (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">NICH-0147</code>)</li>
      <li><strong>Check-In Code:</strong> <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">147</code> (forces <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">CHECK_IN</code>)</li>
      <li><strong>Check-Out Code:</strong> <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">369</code> (forces <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">CHECK_OUT</code>)</li>
      <li><strong>Reference Portrait:</strong> <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/src/main/res/drawable/emp_muhammad_ansar.jpg</code></li>
    </ul>
  </li>
  <li><strong>Automated Time-Window Rules:</strong> Standard passcodes and face scans automatically classify morning hours (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">&lt; 12:00</code>) as <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">CHECK_IN</code> and <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">12:00+</code> as <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">CHECK_OUT</code>.</li>
  <li><strong>Automatic PDF Attendance Email Report:</strong> On every verified Check-In or Check-Out (via Face Scan or conditional backup code <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">147</code>/<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">369</code>), the app generates a structured A4 PDF report and dispatches it asynchronously in the background via SMTP TLS to <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com</code> without interrupting the terminal flow. Configure <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">SMTP_EMAIL</code> and <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">SMTP_APP_PASSWORD</code> (Gmail App Password) in the <strong>Secrets panel in AI Studio</strong> (or <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">.env</code> locally).</li>
</ul>

<h2 id="firebase-configuration-attendanceapp-f6a0e">Firebase Configuration (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">attendanceapp-f6a0e</code>)</h2>

<ul>
  <li><code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/google-services.json</code> is configured for Firebase project <strong><code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">attendanceapp-f6a0e</code></strong> (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">applicationId = "com.aistudio.nichattendance.vtxkqm"</code>).</li>
  <li><code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/src/main/res/values/firebase_applet_config.xml</code> specifies the named Firestore database ID.</li>
  <li><code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">firestore.rules</code> enforces authenticated per-operator data isolation and strict schema validation for background cloud sync.</li>
</ul>

<h2 id="building-the-debug-apk-locally">Building the Debug APK Locally</h2>

<ol>
  <li><strong>Prerequisites:</strong> JDK 17+, Android SDK (API 36), and <strong>Gradle 9.3.1</strong>.</li>
  <li><strong>Prepare the debug signing keystore</strong> (required by <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">signingConfigs.debugConfig</code> in <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/build.gradle.kts</code>). If <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">debug.keystore.base64</code> is present, decode it; otherwise generate a standard debug keystore with <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">keytool</code>:
    <div class="language-bash highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code><span class="k">if</span> <span class="o">[</span> <span class="nt">-f</span> debug.keystore.base64 <span class="o">]</span><span class="p">;</span> <span class="k">then
  </span><span class="nb">base64</span> <span class="nt">-d</span> debug.keystore.base64 <span class="o">&gt;</span> debug.keystore
<span class="k">elif</span> <span class="o">[</span> <span class="o">!</span> <span class="nt">-f</span> debug.keystore <span class="o">]</span><span class="p">;</span> <span class="k">then
  </span>keytool <span class="nt">-genkeypair</span> <span class="nt">-v</span> <span class="nt">-keystore</span> debug.keystore <span class="nt">-storepass</span> android <span class="nt">-alias</span> androiddebugkey <span class="nt">-keypass</span> android <span class="nt">-keyalg</span> RSA <span class="nt">-keysize</span> 2048 <span class="nt">-validity</span> 10000 <span class="nt">-dname</span> <span class="s2">"CN=Android Debug,O=Android,C=US"</span>
<span class="k">fi</span>
</code></pre></div>    </div>
  </li>
  <li><strong>Run unit tests and compile the Debug APK:</strong>
    <div class="language-bash highlighter-rouge"><div class="highlight"><pre class="highlight"><code>gradle :app:testDebugUnitTest <span class="se">\</span>
  <span class="nt">--tests</span> <span class="s1">'com.example.ExampleUnitTest'</span> <span class="se">\</span>
  <span class="nt">--tests</span> <span class="s1">'com.example.ExampleRobolectricTest'</span> <span class="se">\</span>
  <span class="nt">--tests</span> <span class="s1">'com.example.data.model.AttendanceLogicTest'</span> <span class="se">\</span>
  <span class="nt">--tests</span> <span class="s1">'com.example.data.local.OfflineAttendanceStoreTest'</span> <span class="se">\</span>
  <span class="nt">--tests</span> <span class="s1">'com.example.data.report.AttendanceReportTest'</span> <span class="se">\</span>
  <span class="nt">--tests</span> <span class="s1">'com.example.ui.viewmodel.AttendanceViewModelStrictTest'</span>
gradle :app:assembleDebug
</code></pre></div>    </div>
  </li>
  <li>The installable APK is generated at:
<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk</code></li>
</ol>

<h2 id="github-actions-ci">GitHub Actions CI</h2>

<p><code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">.github/workflows/android-build.yml</code> automatically prepares <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">debug.keystore</code> (from <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">secrets.DEBUG_KEYSTORE_BASE64</code>, <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">debug.keystore.base64</code>, or <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">keytool</code>), restores <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/google-services.json</code> from <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">secrets.GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON</code> if provided, runs unit and Robolectric tests, builds <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app-debug.apk</code> with Gradle 9.3.1, and publishes <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">nich-attendance-3-debug-apk</code> as a workflow artifact on every push and pull request.</p>

<blockquote>
  <p><strong>Important GitHub Push Note:</strong> Automated GitHub exports may exclude <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/google-services.json</code>. The build succeeds even without it, and if you want CI-generated APKs to include live Firebase (<code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">attendanceapp-f6a0e</code>) sync, either upload <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">app/google-services.json</code> into your GitHub repository or add its contents as a GitHub Repository Secret named <code class="language-plaintext highlighter-rouge">GOOGLE_SERVICES_JSON</code>.</p>
</blockquote>

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                AppNavigation()
            }
        }
    }
}

private fun FirebaseAuth.authStateFlow(): Flow<FirebaseUser?> = callbackFlow {
    val listener = FirebaseAuth.AuthStateListener { auth ->
        trySend(auth.currentUser)
    }
    addAuthStateListener(listener)
    awaitClose { removeAuthStateListener(listener) }
}

@Composable
fun AppNavigation() {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    var permissionStatus by remember {
        mutableStateOf(readStartupPermissionStatus(context))
    }

    if (!permissionStatus.allGranted) {
        val gateVoiceAssistant = remember(context) { AndroidVoiceAssistant(context) }
        DisposableEffect(gateVoiceAssistant) {
            onDispose { gateVoiceAssistant.shutdown() }
        }
        StartupPermissionGateScreen(
            status = permissionStatus,
            onRefreshPermissions = {
                permissionStatus = readStartupPermissionStatus(context)
            },
            onSpeakPermissionWarning = {
                gateVoiceAssistant.speak(
                    VoicePromptScript.REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_WARNING_PROMPT,
                    flush = true
                )
            },
            onOpenSystemSettings = {
                openAppSystemSettings(context)
            }
        )
        return
    }

    val firebaseAuth: FirebaseAuth? = remember(context) {
        val ready = FirebaseApp.getApps(context).isNotEmpty() || FirebaseApp.initializeApp(context) != null
        if (ready) Firebase.auth else null
    }

    if (firebaseAuth != null) {
        val currentUser by firebaseAuth.authStateFlow()
            .collectAsStateWithLifecycle(initialValue = firebaseAuth.currentUser)
        val user = currentUser

        key(user?.uid ?: "offline") {
            StrictBiometricTerminalShell(currentUserId = user?.uid)
        }
    } else {
        key("offline_no_firebase") {
            StrictBiometricTerminalShell(currentUserId = null)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun StrictBiometricTerminalShell(
    currentUserId: String?
) {
    val viewModel: AttendanceViewModel = viewModel(
        key = currentUserId ?: "offline",
        factory = viewModelFactory {
            initializer {
                val app = checkNotNull(this[APPLICATION_KEY]) {
                    "APPLICATION_KEY missing from CreationExtras"
                }
                val offlineStore = OfflineAttendanceStore(app)
                val emailReporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(app)
                val cloudRepository = if (!currentUserId.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    val databaseId = app.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
                    val db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(databaseId)
                    AttendanceRepository(db)
                } else {
                    null
                }
                val voiceAssistant = AndroidVoiceAssistant(app)
                val networkTimeProvider = SntpNetworkTimeProvider()
                val locationProvider = AndroidLocationAddressProvider(app)
                AttendanceViewModel(
                    offlineStore = offlineStore,
                    repository = cloudRepository,
                    currentUserId = currentUserId,
                    emailReporter = emailReporter,
                    voiceSpeaker = voiceAssistant,
                    networkTimeProvider = networkTimeProvider,
                    locationProvider = locationProvider
                )
            }
        }
    )

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val terminalPhase by viewModel.terminalPhase.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val showRegistration by viewModel.showRegistrationScreen.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val networkTimeStatus by viewModel.networkTimeStatus.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val employeesUi by viewModel.employeesState.collectAsStateWithLifecycle()
    val employeesList = (employeesUi as? DataUiState.Success)?.data.orEmpty()

    Scaffold(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(ObsidianVoid),
        containerColor = ObsidianVoid,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0, 0, 0, 0)
    ) { innerPadding ->
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(innerPadding)
        ) {
            if (showRegistration) {
                EmployeeRegistrationScreen(
                    savedEmployees = employeesList,
                    onRegisterBatch = viewModel::registerEmployeesBatch,
                    onDeleteEmployee = viewModel::deleteRegisteredEmployee,
                    onNavigateToScanner = viewModel::closeRegistrationScreen
                )
            } else {
                TerminalDashboardScreen(
                    terminalPhase = terminalPhase,
                    onStartVipScan = viewModel::startVipBiometricScan,
                    onCameraInitialized = viewModel::onCameraInitialized,
                    onCameraInitFailed = viewModel::onCameraInitFailed,
                    onFrameAnalyzed = viewModel::onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed,
                    onSendCheckOutEmail = {
                        viewModel.sendEmailAndFinish { intent ->
                            runCatching { context.startActivity(intent) }
                        }
                    },
                    onResetTerminal = viewModel::resetToReady,
                    onAttemptSkipCheckOutEmail = viewModel::attemptSkipOrDismissCheckOut,
                    networkTimeLabel = networkTimeStatus.sourceLabel
                )
            }
        }
    }
}


======================================================================
// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/local/OfflineAttendanceStore.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.local

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.content.edit
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceTimeRules
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.FaceBiometricMatcher
import com.example.data.model.PasscodeMatchResult
import com.example.data.model.PasscodeResolver
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import com.google.firebase.Timestamp
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.io.File
import java.util.UUID

data class LocalRegistrationInput(
    val name: String,
    val department: String = "Authorized Staff",
    val photoPath: String,
    val faceSignature: String
)

data class PendingCheckOutLock(
    val logId: String,
    val employeeId: String,
    val employeeCode: String,
    val employeeName: String,
    val department: String,
    val roleTitle: String = "Authorized Staff",
    val avatarKey: String = "avatar_1",
    val localEmployeePhotoPath: String = "",
    val verificationMethod: String,
    val confidenceScore: Int,
    val loggedTimeLabel: String,
    val loggedDateKey: String,
    val gpsCoordinates: String = "",
    val locationAddress: String = "",
    val timeSource: String = "",
    val liveSelfiePath: String = "",
    val pdfReportPath: String = "",
    val lockedAtMillis: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
)

class OfflineAttendanceStore(context: Context) {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private val prefs = appContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private val _employees = MutableStateFlow(loadOrSeedEmployees())
    val employees: StateFlow<List<Employee>> = _employees.asStateFlow()

    private val _logs = MutableStateFlow(loadLogs())
    val logs: StateFlow<List<AttendanceLog>> = _logs.asStateFlow()

    private val _pendingSyncLogIds = MutableStateFlow(loadStringSet(KEY_PENDING_SYNC_IDS))
    val pendingSyncLogIds: StateFlow<Set<String>> = _pendingSyncLogIds.asStateFlow()

    private val _pendingSyncEmployeeIds = MutableStateFlow(loadStringSet(KEY_PENDING_EMPLOYEE_IDS))
    val pendingSyncEmployeeIds: StateFlow<Set<String>> = _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.asStateFlow()

    private val _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds = MutableStateFlow(loadStringSet(KEY_PENDING_DELETE_EMPLOYEE_IDS))
    val pendingDeleteEmployeeIds: StateFlow<Set<String>> = _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.asStateFlow()

    private val _pendingEmailLogIds = MutableStateFlow(loadStringSet(KEY_PENDING_EMAIL_LOG_IDS))
    val pendingEmailLogIds: StateFlow<Set<String>> = _pendingEmailLogIds.asStateFlow()

    private val _pendingCheckOutLock = MutableStateFlow(loadPendingCheckOutLock())
    val pendingCheckOutLock: StateFlow<PendingCheckOutLock?> = _pendingCheckOutLock.asStateFlow()

    val isCheckOutEmailLocked: Boolean
        get() = _pendingCheckOutLock.value != null

    fun hasRegisteredFaceProfiles(): Boolean {
        return _employees.value.any { emp ->
            emp.isActive &&
                emp.faceSignature.isNotBlank() &&
                FaceBiometricMatcher.parseSignature(emp.faceSignature) != null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Previously calibrated a static bundled drawable for Muhammad Ansar, which could
     * cause mismatches against other employees. Clear any legacy unverified synthetic
     * vector unless backed by a live captured local photo.
     */
    suspend fun calibrateRegisteredReferenceFaces() {
        val current = _employees.value
        var changed = false
        val cleaned = current.map { emp ->
            val hasRealPhoto = emp.localPhotoPath.isNotBlank() && File(emp.localPhotoPath).exists()
            val isLegacySynthetic = emp.faceSignature == DEFAULT_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE ||
                emp.faceSignature == LEGACY_ANSAR_FACE_SIGNATURE
            if (!hasRealPhoto && isLegacySynthetic) {
                changed = true
                emp.copy(faceSignature = "")
            } else {
                emp
            }
        }
        if (changed) {
            _employees.value = cleaned
            persistEmployees(cleaned)
        }
    }

    fun verifyCode(rawCode: String, defaultEventType: AttendanceEventType): PasscodeMatchResult? {
        return PasscodeResolver.resolve(rawCode, _employees.value, defaultEventType)
    }

    /**
     * Registers a batch of 1 or more locally captured employee profiles (Name + durable
     * Internal Storage portrait photo + extracted ML Kit facial embedding) at once.
     */
    fun registerEmployeesBatch(inputs: List<LocalRegistrationInput>): List<Employee> {
        if (inputs.isEmpty()) return emptyList()

        val createdList = mutableListOf<Employee>()
        var updatedList = _employees.value.toMutableList()
        var nextNum = computeNextEmployeeNumber(updatedList)

        for (input in inputs) {
            val cleanName = input.name.trim()
            if (cleanName.isBlank()) continue

            val empId = "emp_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").take(16)}"
            val empCode = String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "NICH-%04d", nextNum)
            val passcode = String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%04d", nextNum)
            nextNum++

            val durablePhotoPath = persistRegisteredEmployeePhoto(empId, input.photoPath)

            val newEmployee = Employee(
                id = empId,
                employeeCode = empCode,
                name = cleanName,
                department = input.department.trim().ifEmpty { "Authorized Staff" },
                roleTitle = "Registered Staff",
                passcode = passcode,
                avatarKey = "avatar_local",
                faceSignature = input.faceSignature.trim(),
                isActive = true,
                createdAt = Timestamp.now(),
                updatedAt = Timestamp.now(),
                localPhotoPath = durablePhotoPath
            )
            // Replace if same name already existed cleanly or append new
            updatedList.add(newEmployee)
            createdList.add(newEmployee)
            markEmployeePendingSync(empId)
        }

        val sorted = updatedList.sortedBy { it.employeeCode }
        _employees.value = sorted
        persistEmployees(sorted)
        prefs.edit { putBoolean(KEY_REGISTRATION_COMPLETED, true) }
        return createdList
    }

    private fun computeNextEmployeeNumber(current: List<Employee>): Int {
        val existingMax = current.mapNotNull { emp ->
            emp.employeeCode.substringAfter("-", "").toIntOrNull()
        }.maxOrNull() ?: 4000
        return maxOf(4001, existingMax + 1)
    }

    private fun persistRegisteredEmployeePhoto(employeeId: String, sourcePath: String): String {
        if (sourcePath.isBlank()) return ""
        val srcFile = File(sourcePath)
        if (!srcFile.exists() || srcFile.length() <= 0L) return ""

        return runCatching {
            val dir = File(appContext.filesDir, "employee_photos").apply {
                if (!exists()) mkdirs()
            }
            val safeId = employeeId.replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]"), "_")
            val destFile = File(dir, "$safeId.jpg")
            if (srcFile.absolutePath != destFile.absolutePath) {
                srcFile.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)
            }
            destFile.absolutePath
        }.getOrElse {
            Log.w(TAG, "Failed to persist registered photo: ${it.message}")
            sourcePath
        }
    }

    fun addEmployee(
        employeeCode: String,
        name: String,
        department: String,
        roleTitle: String,
        passcode: String,
        checkInCode: String = "",
        checkOutCode: String = "",
        avatarKey: String,
        faceSignature: String = "",
        localPhotoPath: String = ""
    ): Employee {
        val empId = "emp_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").take(16)}"
        val durablePhotoPath = persistRegisteredEmployeePhoto(empId, localPhotoPath)
        val newEmployee = Employee(
            id = empId,
            employeeCode = employeeCode.trim(),
            name = name.trim(),
            department = department.trim(),
            roleTitle = roleTitle.trim(),
            passcode = passcode.trim(),
            checkInCode = checkInCode.trim(),
            checkOutCode = checkOutCode.trim(),
            avatarKey = avatarKey,
            faceSignature = faceSignature.trim(),
            isActive = true,
            createdAt = Timestamp.now(),
            updatedAt = Timestamp.now(),
            localPhotoPath = durablePhotoPath
        )
        val updated = (_employees.value + newEmployee).sortedBy { it.employeeCode }
        _employees.value = updated
        persistEmployees(updated)
        markEmployeePendingSync(newEmployee.id)
        return newEmployee
    }

    fun updateEmployeeFaceSignature(employee: Employee, signature: String) {
        val updated = _employees.value.map {
            if (it.id == employee.id) it.copy(faceSignature = signature.trim(), updatedAt = Timestamp.now()) else it
        }
        _employees.value = updated
        persistEmployees(updated)
        markEmployeePendingSync(employee.id)
    }

    fun deleteEmployee(employeeId: String, queueCloudDelete: Boolean = true) {
        val target = _employees.value.firstOrNull { it.id == employeeId }
        if (target != null && target.localPhotoPath.isNotBlank()) {
            runCatching { File(target.localPhotoPath).delete() }
        }
        val updated = _employees.value.filterNot { it.id == employeeId }
        _employees.value = updated
        persistEmployees(updated)
        markEmployeeSynced(employeeId)
        if (queueCloudDelete) {
            val pendingDeletes = _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value + employeeId
            _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value = pendingDeletes
            persistStringSet(KEY_PENDING_DELETE_EMPLOYEE_IDS, pendingDeletes)
        }
    }

    fun recordLocalAttendance(
        employee: Employee,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        loggedTimeLabel: String,
        loggedDateKey: String,
        synced: Boolean,
        explicitLogId: String? = null,
        gpsCoordinates: String = "",
        locationAddress: String = "",
        timeSource: String = ""
    ): AttendanceLog {
        val logId = explicitLogId ?: "log_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").take(20)}"
        val entry = AttendanceLog(
            id = logId,
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            department = employee.department,
            avatarKey = employee.avatarKey,
            eventType = eventType.wireValue,
            verificationMethod = verificationMethod.wireValue,
            confidenceScore = confidenceScore.toLong().coerceIn(0L, 100L),
            loggedTimeLabel = loggedTimeLabel,
            loggedDateKey = loggedDateKey,
            gpsCoordinates = gpsCoordinates,
            locationAddress = locationAddress,
            timeSource = timeSource,
            createdAt = Timestamp.now()
        )
        val updatedLogs = (listOf(entry) + _logs.value.filterNot { it.id == logId }).take(MAX_LOCAL_LOGS)
        _logs.value = updatedLogs
        persistLogs(updatedLogs)

        if (!synced) {
            val updatedPending = _pendingSyncLogIds.value + logId
            _pendingSyncLogIds.value = updatedPending
            persistStringSet(KEY_PENDING_SYNC_IDS, updatedPending)
        }
        return entry
    }

    fun persistSelfieForLog(logId: String, sourcePath: String?): String? {
        if (logId.isBlank() || sourcePath.isNullOrBlank()) return null
        val srcFile = File(sourcePath)
        if (!srcFile.exists() || srcFile.length() <= 0L) return null

        val storedPath = runCatching {
            val dir = File(appContext.filesDir, "weekly_selfies").apply {
                if (!exists()) mkdirs()
            }
            val safeId = logId.replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]"), "_")
            val destFile = File(dir, "$safeId.jpg")
            if (srcFile.absolutePath != destFile.absolutePath) {
                srcFile.copyTo(destFile, overwrite = true)
            }
            dir.listFiles()?.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }?.drop(MAX_STORED_SELFIES)?.forEach { old ->
                runCatching { old.delete() }
            }
            destFile.absolutePath
        }.getOrElse { srcFile.absolutePath }

        val map = getSelfiePathsByLogId().toMutableMap()
        map[logId] = storedPath
        persistSelfiePathsMap(map)
        return storedPath
    }

    fun getSelfiePathsByLogId(): Map<String, String> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_LOG_SELFIES_JSON, null) ?: return emptyMap()
        return try {
            val obj = JSONObject(raw)
            val result = LinkedHashMap<String, String>()
            val keys = obj.keys()
            while (keys.hasNext()) {
                val k = keys.next()
                val v = obj.optString(k)
                if (v.isNotBlank()) {
                    result[k] = v
                }
            }
            result
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyMap()
        }
    }

    private fun persistSelfiePathsMap(map: Map<String, String>) {
        val trimmedEntries = map.entries
            .filter { File(it.value).exists() }
            .toList()
            .takeLast(MAX_STORED_SELFIES)
        val obj = JSONObject()
        trimmedEntries.forEach { (k, v) -> obj.put(k, v) }
        prefs.edit { putString(KEY_LOG_SELFIES_JSON, obj.toString()) }
    }

    fun markLogSynced(logId: String) {
        if (logId !in _pendingSyncLogIds.value) return
        val updated = _pendingSyncLogIds.value - logId
        _pendingSyncLogIds.value = updated
        persistStringSet(KEY_PENDING_SYNC_IDS, updated)
    }

    fun markEmployeePendingSync(employeeId: String) {
        if (employeeId in _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value) return
        val updated = _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value + employeeId
        _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value = updated
        persistStringSet(KEY_PENDING_EMPLOYEE_IDS, updated)
    }

    fun markEmployeeSynced(employeeId: String) {
        if (employeeId !in _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value) return
        val updated = _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value - employeeId
        _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value = updated
        persistStringSet(KEY_PENDING_EMPLOYEE_IDS, updated)
    }

    fun markEmployeeDeleteSynced(employeeId: String) {
        if (employeeId !in _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value) return
        val updated = _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value - employeeId
        _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value = updated
        persistStringSet(KEY_PENDING_DELETE_EMPLOYEE_IDS, updated)
    }

    fun markEmailPending(logId: String) {
        if (logId in _pendingEmailLogIds.value) return
        val updated = _pendingEmailLogIds.value + logId
        _pendingEmailLogIds.value = updated
        persistStringSetSync(KEY_PENDING_EMAIL_LOG_IDS, updated)
    }

    fun markEmailSent(logId: String) {
        if (logId in _pendingEmailLogIds.value) {
            val updated = _pendingEmailLogIds.value - logId
            _pendingEmailLogIds.value = updated
            persistStringSetSync(KEY_PENDING_EMAIL_LOG_IDS, updated)
        }
        if (_pendingCheckOutLock.value?.logId == logId) {
            clearPendingCheckOutLock(logId)
        }
    }

    fun savePendingCheckOutLock(lock: PendingCheckOutLock) {
        _pendingCheckOutLock.value = lock
        persistPendingCheckOutLock(lock)
        markEmailPending(lock.logId)
    }

    fun updatePendingCheckOutPdfPath(logId: String, pdfReportPath: String) {
        val current = _pendingCheckOutLock.value ?: return
        if (current.logId != logId || pdfReportPath.isBlank()) return
        val updated = current.copy(pdfReportPath = pdfReportPath)
        _pendingCheckOutLock.value = updated
        persistPendingCheckOutLock(updated)
    }

    fun clearPendingCheckOutLock(logId: String? = null) {
        val current = _pendingCheckOutLock.value
        if (current != null && (logId == null || current.logId == logId)) {
            _pendingCheckOutLock.value = null
            prefs.edit(commit = true) { remove(KEY_PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_JSON) }
        }
        val idToClear = logId ?: current?.logId
        if (!idToClear.isNullOrBlank() && idToClear in _pendingEmailLogIds.value) {
            val updated = _pendingEmailLogIds.value - idToClear
            _pendingEmailLogIds.value = updated
            persistStringSetSync(KEY_PENDING_EMAIL_LOG_IDS, updated)
        }
    }

    fun mergeRemoteEmployees(remoteEmployees: List<Employee>) {
        if (remoteEmployees.isEmpty()) return
        val pendingEdits = _pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value
        val pendingDeletes = _pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value
        val byId = _employees.value.associateBy { it.id }.toMutableMap()
        remoteEmployees.forEach { remote ->
            if (remote.id !in pendingDeletes && remote.id !in pendingEdits) {
                // Preserve localPhotoPath stored on this device
                val localPhoto = byId[remote.id]?.localPhotoPath.orEmpty()
                byId[remote.id] = remote.copy(localPhotoPath = localPhoto)
            }
        }
        val merged = byId.values.filterNot { it.id in pendingDeletes }.sortedBy { it.employeeCode }
        _employees.value = merged
        persistEmployees(merged)
    }

    fun mergeRemoteLogs(remoteLogs: List<AttendanceLog>) {
        val pendingIds = _pendingSyncLogIds.value
        val mergedById = LinkedHashMap<String, AttendanceLog>()
        _logs.value.forEach { mergedById[it.id] = it }
        remoteLogs.forEach { remote ->
            if (remote.id !in pendingIds) {
                mergedById[remote.id] = remote
            }
        }
        val sorted = mergedById.values
            .sortedWith(
                compareByDescending<AttendanceLog> { it.createdAt?.seconds ?: 0L }
                    .thenByDescending { it.loggedDateKey }
            )
            .take(MAX_LOCAL_LOGS)
        _logs.value = sorted
        persistLogs(sorted)
    }

    private fun loadOrSeedEmployees(): List<Employee> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_EMPLOYEES, null)
        val parsed = if (!raw.isNullOrBlank()) parseEmployees(raw) else emptyList()
        val hasAnsar = parsed.any { it.id == MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID }
        if (parsed.isNotEmpty() && hasAnsar) return parsed

        val seeded = (defaultPreRegisteredEmployees() + parsed.filterNot { it.id == MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID })
            .distinctBy { it.id }
        persistEmployees(seeded)
        return seeded
    }

    private fun parseEmployees(raw: String): List<Employee> {
        return try {
            val arr = JSONArray(raw)
            (0 until arr.length()).map { idx ->
                val obj = arr.getJSONObject(idx)
                Employee(
                    id = obj.optString("id"),
                    userId = obj.optString("userId"),
                    employeeCode = obj.optString("employeeCode"),
                    name = obj.optString("name"),
                    department = obj.optString("department"),
                    roleTitle = obj.optString("roleTitle"),
                    passcode = obj.optString("passcode"),
                    checkInCode = obj.optString("checkInCode"),
                    checkOutCode = obj.optString("checkOutCode"),
                    avatarKey = obj.optString("avatarKey", "avatar_1"),
                    faceSignature = obj.optString("faceSignature"),
                    isActive = obj.optBoolean("isActive", true),
                    localPhotoPath = obj.optString("localPhotoPath")
                )
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    private fun persistEmployees(list: List<Employee>) {
        val arr = JSONArray()
        list.forEach { emp ->
            arr.put(
                JSONObject().apply {
                    put("id", emp.id)
                    put("userId", emp.userId)
                    put("employeeCode", emp.employeeCode)
                    put("name", emp.name)
                    put("department", emp.department)
                    put("roleTitle", emp.roleTitle)
                    put("passcode", emp.passcode)
                    put("checkInCode", emp.checkInCode)
                    put("checkOutCode", emp.checkOutCode)
                    put("avatarKey", emp.avatarKey)
                    put("faceSignature", emp.faceSignature)
                    put("isActive", emp.isActive)
                    put("localPhotoPath", emp.localPhotoPath)
                }
            )
        }
        prefs.edit { putString(KEY_EMPLOYEES, arr.toString()) }
    }

    private fun loadLogs(): List<AttendanceLog> {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_LOGS, null) ?: return emptyList()
        return try {
            val arr = JSONArray(raw)
            (0 until arr.length()).map { idx ->
                val obj = arr.getJSONObject(idx)
                val epochSec = obj.optLong("createdAtSec", 0L)
                AttendanceLog(
                    id = obj.optString("id"),
                    userId = obj.optString("userId"),
                    employeeId = obj.optString("employeeId"),
                    employeeCode = obj.optString("employeeCode"),
                    employeeName = obj.optString("employeeName"),
                    department = obj.optString("department"),
                    avatarKey = obj.optString("avatarKey", "avatar_1"),
                    eventType = obj.optString("eventType", AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue),
                    verificationMethod = obj.optString("verificationMethod", VerificationMethod.PASSCODE.wireValue),
                    confidenceScore = obj.optLong("confidenceScore", 100L),
                    loggedTimeLabel = obj.optString("loggedTimeLabel", AttendanceTimeRules.formatTimeLabel()),
                    loggedDateKey = obj.optString("loggedDateKey", AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey()),
                    gpsCoordinates = obj.optString("gpsCoordinates"),
                    locationAddress = obj.optString("locationAddress"),
                    timeSource = obj.optString("timeSource"),
                    createdAt = if (epochSec > 0L) Timestamp(epochSec, 0) else null
                )
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            emptyList()
        }
    }

    private fun persistLogs(list: List<AttendanceLog>) {
        val arr = JSONArray()
        list.forEach { log ->
            arr.put(
                JSONObject().apply {
                    put("id", log.id)
                    put("userId", log.userId)
                    put("employeeId", log.employeeId)
                    put("employeeCode", log.employeeCode)
                    put("employeeName", log.employeeName)
                    put("department", log.department)
                    put("avatarKey", log.avatarKey)
                    put("eventType", log.eventType)
                    put("verificationMethod", log.verificationMethod)
                    put("confidenceScore", log.confidenceScore)
                    put("loggedTimeLabel", log.loggedTimeLabel)
                    put("loggedDateKey", log.loggedDateKey)
                    put("gpsCoordinates", log.gpsCoordinates)
                    put("locationAddress", log.locationAddress)
                    put("timeSource", log.timeSource)
                    put("createdAtSec", log.createdAt?.seconds ?: (System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000L))
                }
            )
        }
        prefs.edit { putString(KEY_LOGS, arr.toString()) }
    }

    private fun loadPendingCheckOutLock(): PendingCheckOutLock? {
        val raw = prefs.getString(KEY_PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_JSON, null) ?: return null
        return try {
            val obj = JSONObject(raw)
            val logId = obj.optString("logId")
            val empName = obj.optString("employeeName")
            if (logId.isBlank() || empName.isBlank()) return null
            PendingCheckOutLock(
                logId = logId,
                employeeId = obj.optString("employeeId"),
                employeeCode = obj.optString("employeeCode"),
                employeeName = empName,
                department = obj.optString("department", "Authorized Staff"),
                roleTitle = obj.optString("roleTitle", "Authorized Staff"),
                avatarKey = obj.optString("avatarKey", "avatar_1"),
                localEmployeePhotoPath = obj.optString("localEmployeePhotoPath"),
                verificationMethod = obj.optString("verificationMethod", VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1.wireValue),
                confidenceScore = obj.optInt("confidenceScore", 90),
                loggedTimeLabel = obj.optString("loggedTimeLabel"),
                loggedDateKey = obj.optString("loggedDateKey"),
                gpsCoordinates = obj.optString("gpsCoordinates"),
                locationAddress = obj.optString("locationAddress"),
                timeSource = obj.optString("timeSource"),
                liveSelfiePath = obj.optString("liveSelfiePath"),
                pdfReportPath = obj.optString("pdfReportPath"),
                lockedAtMillis = obj.optLong("lockedAtMillis", System.currentTimeMillis())
            )
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    private fun persistPendingCheckOutLock(lock: PendingCheckOutLock) {
        val obj = JSONObject().apply {
            put("logId", lock.logId)
            put("employeeId", lock.employeeId)
            put("employeeCode", lock.employeeCode)
            put("employeeName", lock.employeeName)
            put("department", lock.department)
            put("roleTitle", lock.roleTitle)
            put("avatarKey", lock.avatarKey)
            put("localEmployeePhotoPath", lock.localEmployeePhotoPath)
            put("verificationMethod", lock.verificationMethod)
            put("confidenceScore", lock.confidenceScore)
            put("loggedTimeLabel", lock.loggedTimeLabel)
            put("loggedDateKey", lock.loggedDateKey)
            put("gpsCoordinates", lock.gpsCoordinates)
            put("locationAddress", lock.locationAddress)
            put("timeSource", lock.timeSource)
            put("liveSelfiePath", lock.liveSelfiePath)
            put("pdfReportPath", lock.pdfReportPath)
            put("lockedAtMillis", lock.lockedAtMillis)
        }
        prefs.edit(commit = true) { putString(KEY_PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_JSON, obj.toString()) }
    }

    private fun loadStringSet(key: String): Set<String> {
        return prefs.getStringSet(key, emptySet())?.toSet() ?: emptySet()
    }

    private fun persistStringSet(key: String, ids: Set<String>) {
        prefs.edit { putStringSet(key, ids) }
    }

    private fun persistStringSetSync(key: String, ids: Set<String>) {
        prefs.edit(commit = true) { putStringSet(key, ids) }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "OfflineStore"
        private const val PREFS_NAME = "nich_offline_attendance"
        private const val KEY_EMPLOYEES = "employees_json"
        private const val KEY_LOGS = "logs_json"

        private const val KEY_PENDING_EMPLOYEE_IDS = "pending_employee_ids"
        private const val KEY_PENDING_DELETE_EMPLOYEE_IDS = "pending_delete_employee_ids"
        private const val KEY_PENDING_EMAIL_LOG_IDS = "pending_email_log_ids"
        private const val KEY_PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_JSON = "pending_checkout_lock_json"
        private const val KEY_LOG_SELFIES_JSON = "log_selfies_json"
        private const val KEY_REGISTRATION_COMPLETED = "local_registration_completed"
        private const val MAX_LOCAL_LOGS = 400
        private const val MAX_STORED_SELFIES = 80

        const val MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID = "emp_muhammad_ansar"

// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/location/LocationAddressProvider.kt

package com.example.data.location

import android.Manifest
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.location.Address
import android.location.Geocoder
import android.location.Location
import android.location.LocationListener
import android.location.LocationManager
import android.os.Build
import android.os.CancellationSignal
import android.os.Looper
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeoutOrNull
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.coroutines.resume

data class VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
    val latitude: Double? = null,
    val longitude: Double? = null,
    val coordinatesLabel: String = "",
    val readableAddress: String = "",
    val accuracyMeters: Float? = null,
    val providerName: String = ""
) {
    val hasFix: Boolean
        get() = latitude != null && longitude != null && coordinatesLabel.isNotBlank()
}

interface LocationAddressProvider {
    suspend fun captureCurrentLocation(): VerifiedLocationSnapshot
}

object NoOpLocationAddressProvider : LocationAddressProvider {
    override suspend fun captureCurrentLocation(): VerifiedLocationSnapshot =
        VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
            coordinatesLabel = "Unavailable (Offline/Test)",
            readableAddress = "Location unavailable"
        )
}

class AndroidLocationAddressProvider(
    context: Context
) : LocationAddressProvider {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext

    override suspend fun captureCurrentLocation(): VerifiedLocationSnapshot = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val hasFine = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
            appContext,
            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION
        ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        val hasCoarse = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
            appContext,
            Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION
        ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

        if (!hasFine && !hasCoarse) {
            return@withContext VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
                coordinatesLabel = "Permission Missing",
                readableAddress = "GPS Location permission not granted"
            )
        }

        val locationManager = appContext.getSystemService(Context.LOCATION_SERVICE) as? LocationManager
            ?: return@withContext VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
                coordinatesLabel = "GPS Hardware Unavailable",
                readableAddress = "Location service unavailable"
            )

        val freshOrCached = obtainBestLocation(locationManager, hasFine)
            ?: return@withContext VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
                coordinatesLabel = "Acquiring GPS Signal",
                readableAddress = "GPS signal unavailable indoors"
            )

        val lat = freshOrCached.latitude
        val lon = freshOrCached.longitude
        val coordsLabel = formatCoordinates(lat, lon, freshOrCached.accuracy.takeIf { it > 0f })
        val resolvedAddress = reverseGeocodeAddress(lat, lon) ?: "GPS Fix ($coordsLabel)"

        VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
            latitude = lat,
            longitude = lon,
            coordinatesLabel = coordsLabel,
            readableAddress = resolvedAddress,
            accuracyMeters = freshOrCached.accuracy.takeIf { it > 0f },
            providerName = freshOrCached.provider ?: "gps"
        )
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private suspend fun obtainBestLocation(
        locationManager: LocationManager,
        hasFine: Boolean
    ): Location? {
        val preferredProviders = buildList {
            if (hasFine && isProviderEnabled(locationManager, LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER)) {
                add(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER)
            }
            if (isProviderEnabled(locationManager, LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)) {
                add(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)
            }
        }

        for (provider in preferredProviders) {
            val activeFix = withTimeoutOrNull(LOCATION_FIX_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                requestSingleLocation(locationManager, provider)
            }
            if (activeFix != null) {
                return activeFix
            }
        }

        val lastCandidates = listOf(
            LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER,
            LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER,
            LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER
        ).mapNotNull { provider ->
            runCatching { locationManager.getLastKnownLocation(provider) }.getOrNull()
        }

        return lastCandidates.maxByOrNull { loc ->
            val recencyBonus = loc.time / 1000L
            val accuracyPenalty = loc.accuracy.toLong().coerceAtMost(500L)
            recencyBonus - accuracyPenalty
        }
    }

    private fun isProviderEnabled(locationManager: LocationManager, provider: String): Boolean {
        return runCatching { locationManager.isProviderEnabled(provider) }.getOrDefault(false)
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private suspend fun requestSingleLocation(
        locationManager: LocationManager,
        provider: String
    ): Location? = suspendCancellableCoroutine { continuation ->
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
            val signal = CancellationSignal()
            continuation.invokeOnCancellation { signal.cancel() }
            runCatching {
                locationManager.getCurrentLocation(
                    provider,
                    signal,
                    ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(appContext)
                ) { location ->
                    if (continuation.isActive) {
                        continuation.resume(location)
                    }
                }
            }.onFailure {
                if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(null)
            }
        } else {
            val listener = object : LocationListener {
                override fun onLocationChanged(location: Location) {
                    runCatching { locationManager.removeUpdates(this) }
                    if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(location)
                }

                override fun onProviderDisabled(providerName: String) = Unit
                override fun onProviderEnabled(providerName: String) = Unit
            }
            continuation.invokeOnCancellation {
                runCatching { locationManager.removeUpdates(listener) }
            }
            runCatching {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                locationManager.requestSingleUpdate(provider, listener, Looper.getMainLooper())
            }.onFailure {
                if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(null)
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun reverseGeocodeAddress(lat: Double, lon: Double): String? {
        if (!Geocoder.isPresent()) return null
        val geocoder = Geocoder(appContext, Locale.getDefault())
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            withTimeoutOrNull(GEOCODE_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                suspendCancellableCoroutine { continuation ->
                    runCatching {
                        geocoder.getFromLocation(lat, lon, 1) { addresses ->
                            if (continuation.isActive) {
                                continuation.resume(addresses.firstOrNull()?.let(::formatAddress))
                            }
                        }
                    }.onFailure {
                        if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(null)
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            runCatching {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                geocoder.getFromLocation(lat, lon, 1)?.firstOrNull()?.let(::formatAddress)
            }.getOrNull()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val LOCATION_FIX_TIMEOUT_MS = 2200L
        private const val GEOCODE_TIMEOUT_MS = 2200L

        internal fun formatCoordinates(lat: Double, lon: Double, accuracyMeters: Float? = null): String {
            val base = String.format(Locale.US, "%.6f, %.6f", lat, lon)
            return if (accuracyMeters != null && accuracyMeters > 0f) {
                "$base (±${accuracyMeters.toInt()}m)"
            } else {
                base
            }
        }

        internal fun formatAddress(address: Address): String? {
            val line0 = runCatching { address.getAddressLine(0) }.getOrNull()?.trim()
            if (!line0.isNullOrBlank()) return line0
            val parts = listOfNotNull(
                address.featureName,
                address.thoroughfare,
                address.subLocality,
                address.locality,
                address.adminArea,
                address.countryName
            ).map { it.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.distinct()
            return if (parts.isNotEmpty()) parts.joinToString(", ") else null
        }
    }
}
