package com.example.data.model

import com.google.firebase.Timestamp
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentId
import com.google.firebase.firestore.Exclude
import com.google.firebase.firestore.IgnoreExtraProperties
import com.google.firebase.firestore.ServerTimestamp
import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min
import kotlin.math.sqrt

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class Employee(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "",
    val userId: String = "",
    val employeeCode: String = "",
    val name: String = "",
    val department: String = "",
    val roleTitle: String = "",
    val passcode: String = "",
    val checkInCode: String = "",
    val checkOutCode: String = "",
    val avatarKey: String = "avatar_1",
    val faceSignature: String = "",
    val isActive: Boolean = true,
    @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null,
    @ServerTimestamp val updatedAt: Timestamp? = null,
    @get:Exclude val localPhotoPath: String = ""
)

data class PasscodeMatchResult(
    val employee: Employee,
    val eventType: AttendanceEventType
)

@IgnoreExtraProperties
data class AttendanceLog(
    @DocumentId val id: String = "",
    val userId: String = "",
    val employeeId: String = "",
    val employeeCode: String = "",
    val employeeName: String = "",
    val department: String = "",
    val avatarKey: String = "avatar_1",
    val eventType: String = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue,
    val verificationMethod: String = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1.wireValue,
    val confidenceScore: Long = 0L,
    val loggedTimeLabel: String = "",
    val loggedDateKey: String = "",
    val gpsCoordinates: String = "",
    val locationAddress: String = "",
    val timeSource: String = "",
    @ServerTimestamp val createdAt: Timestamp? = null
)

enum class AttendanceEventType(val wireValue: String, val displayLabel: String) {
    CHECK_IN("CHECK_IN", "CHECK-IN"),
    CHECK_OUT("CHECK_OUT", "CHECK-OUT");

    companion object {
        fun fromWire(value: String): AttendanceEventType =
            entries.firstOrNull { it.wireValue == value } ?: CHECK_IN
    }
}

enum class VerificationMethod(val wireValue: String, val displayLabel: String) {
    FACE_SCAN_1("FACE_SCAN_1", "Biometric Face Scan (50%+ Verified)"),
    FACE_SCAN_2("FACE_SCAN_2", "Biometric Face Scan (Pass 2)"),
    PASSCODE("PASSCODE", "Employee Passcode Fallback");

    companion object {
        fun fromWire(value: String): VerificationMethod =
            entries.firstOrNull { it.wireValue == value } ?: PASSCODE
    }
}

object PasscodeResolver {
    fun resolve(
        rawCode: String,
        employees: List<Employee>,
        fallbackTimeEventType: AttendanceEventType
    ): PasscodeMatchResult? {
        val code = rawCode.trim()
        if (code.isEmpty()) return null
        val active = employees.filter { it.isActive }
        active.firstOrNull { it.checkInCode.isNotBlank() && it.checkInCode == code }?.let {
            return PasscodeMatchResult(employee = it, eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN)
        }
        active.firstOrNull { it.checkOutCode.isNotBlank() && it.checkOutCode == code }?.let {
            return PasscodeMatchResult(employee = it, eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        }
        active.firstOrNull { it.passcode == code }?.let {
            return PasscodeMatchResult(employee = it, eventType = fallbackTimeEventType)
        }
        return null
    }
}

object AttendanceTimeRules {
    /**
     * Morning (before 12:00 PM): CHECK_IN.
     * Afternoon (12:00 PM onwards): CHECK_OUT.
     */
    fun determineEventType(hourOfDay24: Int): AttendanceEventType {
        return if (hourOfDay24 < 12) {
            AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN
        } else {
            AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT
        }
    }

    fun currentEventType(calendar: Calendar = Calendar.getInstance()): AttendanceEventType {
        return determineEventType(calendar.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY))
    }

    fun windowDescription(hourOfDay24: Int): String {
        return when {
            hourOfDay24 in 8..11 -> "Morning Window (08:00–12:00) • Auto Check-In"
            hourOfDay24 < 8 -> "Early Morning (<08:00) • Auto Check-In"
            else -> "Afternoon Window (12:00+) • Auto Check-Out"
        }
    }

    fun formatTimeLabel(calendar: Calendar = Calendar.getInstance()): String {
        val hour24 = calendar.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY)
        val minute = calendar.get(Calendar.MINUTE)
        val second = calendar.get(Calendar.SECOND)
        val amPm = if (hour24 < 12) "AM" else "PM"
        val hour12 = when (val h = hour24 % 12) {
            0 -> 12
            else -> h
        }
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d:%02d %s", hour12, minute, second, amPm)
    }

    fun formatDateKey(calendar: Calendar = Calendar.getInstance()): String {
        val year = calendar.get(Calendar.YEAR)
        val month = calendar.get(Calendar.MONTH) + 1
        val day = calendar.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH)
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%04d-%02d-%02d", year, month, day)
    }
}

data class FaceMatchResult(
    val employee: Employee,
    val confidencePercent: Int
)

data class FaceEvaluationOutcome(
    val match: FaceMatchResult?,
    val bestConfidencePercent: Int,
    val closestEmployeeName: String? = null
)

enum class LivenessDirection(
    val instruction: String,
    val shortLabel: String
) {
    TURN_LEFT(
        instruction = "TURN FACE SLIGHTLY LEFT",
        shortLabel = "LOOK LEFT"
    ),
    TURN_RIGHT(
        instruction = "TURN FACE SLIGHTLY RIGHT",
        shortLabel = "LOOK RIGHT"
    ),
    LOOK_UP(
        instruction = "TILT FACE SLIGHTLY UP",
        shortLabel = "LOOK UP"
    ),
    LOOK_DOWN(
        instruction = "TILT FACE SLIGHTLY DOWN",
        shortLabel = "LOOK DOWN"
    ),
    CENTER_VERIFY(
        instruction = "LOOK STRAIGHT • VERIFYING FACE",
        shortLabel = "CENTER MATCH"
    );

    companion object {
        fun buildChallengeSequence(seed: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): List<LivenessDirection> {
            val directionalPool = listOf(TURN_LEFT, TURN_RIGHT, LOOK_UP, LOOK_DOWN)
            val firstIdx = ((seed and 0x7FFFFFFFL) % directionalPool.size).toInt()
            val secondIdx = (firstIdx + 1 + ((seed ushr 3 and 0x7FFFFFFFL) % 3).toInt()) % directionalPool.size
            return listOf(directionalPool[firstIdx], directionalPool[secondIdx], CENTER_VERIFY)
        }
    }
}

object LivenessEvaluator {
    private const val YAW_THRESHOLD_DEG = 8.0f
    private const val PITCH_THRESHOLD_DEG = 6.5f

    fun isDirectionSatisfied(
        direction: LivenessDirection,
        headYawDeg: Float,
        headPitchDeg: Float,
        isFrontCamera: Boolean = true
    ): Boolean {
        val mirroredYaw = if (isFrontCamera) headYawDeg else -headYawDeg
        return when (direction) {
            LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT -> mirroredYaw >= YAW_THRESHOLD_DEG
            LivenessDirection.TURN_RIGHT -> mirroredYaw <= -YAW_THRESHOLD_DEG
            LivenessDirection.LOOK_UP -> headPitchDeg >= PITCH_THRESHOLD_DEG
            LivenessDirection.LOOK_DOWN -> headPitchDeg <= -PITCH_THRESHOLD_DEG
            LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY ->
                abs(headYawDeg) <= 25f && abs(headPitchDeg) <= 25f
        }
    }
}

data class FaceAnalysisFrame(
    val faceDetected: Boolean,
    val eyesLocked: Boolean = false,
    val noseMustacheLocked: Boolean = false,
    val jawBeardLocked: Boolean = false,
    val signature: String? = null,
    val livenessOpenEyes: Boolean = true,
    val headPoseAligned: Boolean = true,
    val headYawDeg: Float = 0f,
    val headPitchDeg: Float = 0f,
    val headRollDeg: Float = 0f,
    val leftEyeOpenProbability: Float? = null,
    val rightEyeOpenProbability: Float? = null,
    val smilingProbability: Float? = null,
    val faceCenterXNorm: Float? = null,
    val faceCenterYNorm: Float? = null,
    val isFrontCamera: Boolean = true,
    val selfieImagePath: String? = null,
    val qualityIssue: String? = null
)

data class AntiSpoofingCheckResult(
    val livenessVerified: Boolean,
    val spoofSuspected: Boolean,
    val statusPrompt: String
)

/**
 * Multi-frame anti-spoofing liveness tracker that defeats printed photos and mobile screenshots
 * by requiring live biological signals from ML Kit:
 * 1) Natural eye blink (open -> blink dip -> reopen), OR
 * 2) Dynamic smile / expression transition (smile with expression shift or micro-motion), OR
 * 3) Live 3D head pose motion accompanied by subtle eye/smile classification variation.
 */
class AntiSpoofingLivenessTracker {
    private var frameCount = 0
    private var sawOpenEyesBeforeBlink = false
    private var sawBlinkDip = false
    private var blinkCompleted = false
    private var dynamicSmileCompleted = false
    private var livenessLockedVerified = false

    private var minEyeProb = 1f
    private var maxEyeProb = 0f
    private var minSmileProb = 1f
    private var maxSmileProb = 0f

    private var minYaw = Float.MAX_VALUE
    private var maxYaw = -Float.MAX_VALUE
    private var minPitch = Float.MAX_VALUE
    private var maxPitch = -Float.MAX_VALUE
    private var minCenterX = Float.MAX_VALUE
    private var maxCenterX = -Float.MAX_VALUE

    fun reset() {
        frameCount = 0
        sawOpenEyesBeforeBlink = false
        sawBlinkDip = false
        blinkCompleted = false
        dynamicSmileCompleted = false
        livenessLockedVerified = false
        minEyeProb = 1f
        maxEyeProb = 0f
        minSmileProb = 1f
        maxSmileProb = 0f
        minYaw = Float.MAX_VALUE
        maxYaw = -Float.MAX_VALUE
        minPitch = Float.MAX_VALUE
        maxPitch = -Float.MAX_VALUE
        minCenterX = Float.MAX_VALUE
        maxCenterX = -Float.MAX_VALUE
    }

    fun evaluate(frame: FaceAnalysisFrame): AntiSpoofingCheckResult {
        if (!frame.faceDetected) {
            if (frameCount > 0) reset()
            return AntiSpoofingCheckResult(
                livenessVerified = false,
                spoofSuspected = false,
                statusPrompt = "Position your live face inside the scanner frame"
            )
        }

        frameCount++

        if (frame.headYawDeg < minYaw) minYaw = frame.headYawDeg
        if (frame.headYawDeg > maxYaw) maxYaw = frame.headYawDeg
        if (frame.headPitchDeg < minPitch) minPitch = frame.headPitchDeg
        if (frame.headPitchDeg > maxPitch) maxPitch = frame.headPitchDeg

        val cx = frame.faceCenterXNorm
        if (cx != null) {
            if (cx < minCenterX) minCenterX = cx
            if (cx > maxCenterX) maxCenterX = cx
        }

        val eyeProbs = listOfNotNull(frame.leftEyeOpenProbability, frame.rightEyeOpenProbability)
        val avgEyeOpen = if (eyeProbs.isNotEmpty()) {
            eyeProbs.average().toFloat()
        } else if (!frame.livenessOpenEyes) {
            0.15f
        } else {
            null
        }

        if (avgEyeOpen != null) {
            if (avgEyeOpen < minEyeProb) minEyeProb = avgEyeOpen
            if (avgEyeOpen > maxEyeProb) maxEyeProb = avgEyeOpen

            if (!sawOpenEyesBeforeBlink && avgEyeOpen >= EYE_OPEN_THRESHOLD) {
                sawOpenEyesBeforeBlink = true
            } else if (sawOpenEyesBeforeBlink && !sawBlinkDip && avgEyeOpen <= EYE_BLINK_THRESHOLD) {
                sawBlinkDip = true
            } else if (sawBlinkDip && avgEyeOpen >= EYE_OPEN_THRESHOLD) {
                blinkCompleted = true
            }
        }

        val smile = frame.smilingProbability
        if (smile != null) {
            if (smile < minSmileProb) minSmileProb = smile
            if (smile > maxSmileProb) maxSmileProb = smile
        }

        val yawSpan = (maxYaw - minYaw).coerceAtLeast(0f)
        val pitchSpan = (maxPitch - minPitch).coerceAtLeast(0f)
        val poseSpan = yawSpan + pitchSpan
        val eyeSpan = (maxEyeProb - minEyeProb).coerceAtLeast(0f)
        val smileSpan = (maxSmileProb - minSmileProb).coerceAtLeast(0f)
        val centerShift = if (minCenterX <= maxCenterX) (maxCenterX - minCenterX) else 0f

        if (smile != null && smile >= SMILE_ACTIVE_THRESHOLD &&
            (smileSpan >= SMILE_DELTA_THRESHOLD || poseSpan >= 1.5f || centerShift >= 0.015f)
        ) {
            dynamicSmileCompleted = true
        }

        val natural3DMotionVerified = frameCount >= 2 &&
            poseSpan >= MOTION_POSE_SPAN_THRESHOLD &&
            (eyeSpan >= 0.04f || smileSpan >= 0.06f)

        if (blinkCompleted || dynamicSmileCompleted || natural3DMotionVerified) {
            livenessLockedVerified = true
        }

        if (livenessLockedVerified) {
            return AntiSpoofingCheckResult(
                livenessVerified = true,
                spoofSuspected = false,
                statusPrompt = "Liveness Verified • Face Scan Active"
            )
        }

        val staticPhotoSuspected = frameCount >= STATIC_SPOOF_FRAME_LIMIT &&
            poseSpan < 1.2f &&
            centerShift < 0.01f &&
            eyeSpan < 0.05f &&
            smileSpan < 0.05f &&
            !sawBlinkDip

        val prompt = when {
            staticPhotoSuspected ->
                "Spoof Alert: Static photo or screen detected — Blink eyes or smile naturally"
            sawBlinkDip ->
                "Blink detected — Open eyes and look straight"
            else ->
                "Liveness Check: Blink your eyes or smile naturally"
        }

        return AntiSpoofingCheckResult(
            livenessVerified = false,
            spoofSuspected = staticPhotoSuspected,
            statusPrompt = prompt
        )
    }

    companion object {
        private const val EYE_OPEN_THRESHOLD = 0.50f
        private const val EYE_BLINK_THRESHOLD = 0.42f
        private const val SMILE_ACTIVE_THRESHOLD = 0.55f
        private const val SMILE_DELTA_THRESHOLD = 0.16f
        private const val MOTION_POSE_SPAN_THRESHOLD = 7.0f
        private const val STATIC_SPOOF_FRAME_LIMIT = 8
    }
}

object FaceBiometricMatcher {
    const val MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT = 50
    const val HIGH_PRECISION_DIMENSION = 16
    const val FULL_FEATURE_DIMENSION = 8
    const val LEGACY_FEATURE_DIMENSION = 4

    fun encodeSignature(
        eyeToBoxRatio: Float,
        noseToEyeRatio: Float,
        mouthToEyeRatio: Float,
        boxAspectRatio: Float
    ): String {
        return String.format(
            Locale.US,
            "%.4f:%.4f:%.4f:%.4f",
            eyeToBoxRatio,
            noseToEyeRatio,
            mouthToEyeRatio,
            boxAspectRatio
        )
    }

    fun encodeFullSignature(
        eyeToBoxRatio: Float,
        noseToEyeRatio: Float,
        mouthToEyeRatio: Float,
        boxAspectRatio: Float,
        philtrumToEyeRatio: Float,
        jawChinDropRatio: Float,
        jawToCheekWidthRatio: Float,
        symmetryBalanceRatio: Float
    ): String {
        return String.format(
            Locale.US,
            "%.4f:%.4f:%.4f:%.4f:%.4f:%.4f:%.4f:%.4f",
            eyeToBoxRatio,
            noseToEyeRatio,
            mouthToEyeRatio,
            boxAspectRatio,
            philtrumToEyeRatio,
            jawChinDropRatio,
            jawToCheekWidthRatio,
            symmetryBalanceRatio
        )
    }

    fun encodeVectorSignature(features: List<Float>): String {
        return features.joinToString(":") { value ->
            String.format(Locale.US, "%.4f", value)
        }
    }

    fun parseSignature(raw: String): List<Float>? {
        if (raw.isBlank()) return null
        val parts = raw.split(":").mapNotNull { it.toFloatOrNull() }
        return if (parts.size in LEGACY_FEATURE_DIMENSION..32) {
            parts
        } else {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Compares the live facial feature vector against ALL active local-storage
     * employee profiles that have enrolled biometric embeddings.
     * - Eliminates false fallback to any default employee when confidence < 50%.
     * - Scores using weighted RMS distance + L-infinity peak feature difference so two
     *   distinct faces separate cleanly while same-person scans pass reliably at 50%+
     *   even under lighting or angle variation.
     */
    fun evaluateFrame(
        liveSignature: String,
        employees: List<Employee>
    ): FaceEvaluationOutcome {
        val enrolledEmployees = employees.filter {
            it.isActive && parseSignature(it.faceSignature) != null
        }
        if (enrolledEmployees.isEmpty()) {
            return FaceEvaluationOutcome(match = null, bestConfidencePercent = 0)
        }

        val liveVec = parseSignature(liveSignature)
            ?: return FaceEvaluationOutcome(match = null, bestConfidencePercent = 0)

        var bestEmployee: Employee? = null
        var bestScore = 0
        var closestName: String? = null

        for (employee in enrolledEmployees) {
            val enrolledVec = parseSignature(employee.faceSignature) ?: continue
            val comparedDims = min(liveVec.size, enrolledVec.size)
            if (comparedDims < LEGACY_FEATURE_DIMENSION) continue

            val score = computeSimilarityScorePercent(
                liveVec = liveVec,
                enrolledVec = enrolledVec,
                comparedDims = comparedDims
            )
            if (score > bestScore) {
                bestScore = score
                bestEmployee = employee
                closestName = employee.name
            }
        }

        val matched = if (bestEmployee != null && bestScore >= MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT) {
            FaceMatchResult(employee = bestEmployee, confidencePercent = bestScore)
        } else {
            null
        }

        return FaceEvaluationOutcome(
            match = matched,
            bestConfidencePercent = bestScore,
            closestEmployeeName = closestName
        )
    }

    internal fun computeSimilarityScorePercent(
        liveVec: List<Float>,
        enrolledVec: List<Float>,
        comparedDims: Int
    ): Int {
        var sumSq = 0.0
        var maxAbsDiff = 0.0
        for (i in 0 until comparedDims) {
            val diff = abs(liveVec[i].toDouble() - enrolledVec[i].toDouble())
            sumSq += diff * diff
            maxAbsDiff = max(maxAbsDiff, diff)
        }
        val rmsDistance = sqrt(sumSq / comparedDims.toDouble())
        // Penalize both overall Euclidean drift and any single-feature mismatch
        val penalty = (rmsDistance * 165.0) + (maxAbsDiff * 42.0)
        return (100.0 - penalty).toInt().coerceIn(0, 100)
    }

    fun matchBestEmployee(
        liveSignature: String,
        employees: List<Employee>
    ): FaceMatchResult? = evaluateFrame(liveSignature, employees).match
}

object BidiTextHelper {
    private const val LRI = "\u2066"
    private const val RLI = "\u2067"
    private const val PDI = "\u2069"
    private const val LRM = "\u200E"

    private val arabicUrduRegex = Regex("[\\u0600-\\u06FF\\u0750-\\u077F\\u08A0-\\u08FF\\uFB50-\\uFDFF\\uFE70-\\uFEFF]+(?:\\s+[\\u0600-\\u06FF\\u0750-\\u077F\\u08A0-\\u08FF\\uFB50-\\uFDFF\\uFE70-\\uFEFF]+)*")
    private val parensLatinRegex = Regex("\\(([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 .,'_-]*)\\)")
    private val parensUrduRegex = Regex("\\(([\\u0600-\\u06FF\\u0750-\\u077F\\u08A0-\\u08FF\\uFB50-\\uFDFF\\uFE70-\\uFEFF\\s]+)\\)")

    fun isolateMixedText(raw: String): String {
        if (raw.isBlank()) return ""
        val isolated = arabicUrduRegex.replace(raw) { match ->
            "$RLI${match.value}$PDI"
        }
        return "$LRI$isolated$PDI"
    }

    fun formatEmployeeHeaderTitle(rawName: String, employeeCode: String): String {
        val cleanName = rawName.trim()
        val cleanCode = employeeCode.trim()
        val containsArabic = arabicUrduRegex.containsMatchIn(cleanName)
        val displayTitle = if (containsArabic) {
            val latinInParens = parensLatinRegex.find(cleanName)?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)?.trim()
            val urduWithoutLatinParens = parensLatinRegex.replace(cleanName, "").trim()
            val urduInParens = parensUrduRegex.find(cleanName)?.groupValues?.getOrNull(1)?.trim()
            val latinWithoutUrduParens = parensUrduRegex.replace(cleanName, "").trim()

            when {
                !latinInParens.isNullOrBlank() && urduWithoutLatinParens.isNotBlank() ->
                    "$LRI$latinInParens / $RLI$urduWithoutLatinParens$PDI${if (cleanCode.isNotBlank()) " - $cleanCode" else ""}$PDI"
                !urduInParens.isNullOrBlank() && latinWithoutUrduParens.isNotBlank() ->
                    "$LRI$latinWithoutUrduParens / $RLI$urduInParens$PDI${if (cleanCode.isNotBlank()) " - $cleanCode" else ""}$PDI"
                else ->
                    "$LRI${isolateMixedText(cleanName)}${if (cleanCode.isNotBlank()) "$LRM - $cleanCode" else ""}$PDI"
            }
        } else {
            val combined = if (cleanCode.isNotBlank()) "$cleanName - $cleanCode" else cleanName
            "$LRI$combined$PDI"
        }
        return displayTitle
    }

    fun isolateLtrToken(token: String): String {
        return if (token.isBlank()) "" else "$LRI${token.trim()}$PDI"
    }

    fun stripBidiControls(input: String): String {
        return buildString(input.length) {
            for (ch in input) {
                when (ch) {
                    '\u200E', '\u200F', '\u202A', '\u202B', '\u202C', '\u202D', '\u202E',
                    '\u2066', '\u2067', '\u2068', '\u2069' -> Unit
                    else -> append(ch)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/remote/FirestoreErrorHandler.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.remote

import android.util.Log
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject

enum class OperationType(val value: String) {
    CREATE("create"),
    UPDATE("update"),
    DELETE("delete"),
    LIST("list"),
    GET("get"),
    WRITE("write")
}

fun handleFirestoreError(
    exception: Throwable,
    operationType: OperationType,
    path: String?
): String {
    val causes = generateSequence(exception) { it.cause }
    val rootError = causes.firstOrNull { it is FirebaseFirestoreException }
        ?: causes.firstOrNull { it !is CancellationException }
        ?: exception

    val currentUser = try {
        FirebaseAuth.getInstance().currentUser
    } catch (_: Exception) {
        null
    }

    val providerInfoList = currentUser?.providerData?.map { provider ->
        JSONObject().apply {
            put("providerId", provider.providerId)
            put("email", provider.email)
        }
    } ?: emptyList()

    val authInfoJson = JSONObject().apply {
        put("userId", currentUser?.uid)
        put("email", currentUser?.email)
        put("emailVerified", currentUser?.isEmailVerified)
        put("tenantId", currentUser?.tenantId)
        put("providerInfo", JSONArray(providerInfoList))
    }

    val errorInfoJson = JSONObject().apply {
        put("error", rootError.message ?: rootError.toString())
        put("operationType", operationType.value)
        put("path", path)
        put("authInfo", authInfoJson)
    }

    val jsonString = errorInfoJson.toString()
    Log.e("FirestoreError", "Firestore Error: $jsonString")
    return jsonString
}

package com.example.data.report

import android.accounts.AccountManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.util.Log
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import com.example.BuildConfig
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File

enum class EmailReportStatus(val label: String) {
    DISPATCHING("Generating & Sending PDF..."),
    SENT("Sent PDF to Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com"),
    QUEUED_OFFLINE("PDF Ready • Sends via Device Gmail"),
    NEEDS_CONFIG("PDF Ready • Tap Send Email & Finish")
}

object DeviceAccountResolver {
    fun resolveLoggedInGoogleEmail(context: Context): String? {
        return runCatching {
            val manager = AccountManager.get(context)
            val googleAccounts = manager.getAccountsByType("com.google")
            val primaryGoogle = googleAccounts
                .map { it.name.trim() }
                .firstOrNull { it.contains("@") }
            if (!primaryGoogle.isNullOrBlank()) {
                return@runCatching primaryGoogle
            }
            manager.accounts
                .map { it.name.trim() }
                .firstOrNull { it.contains("@") }
        }.getOrNull()
    }
}

class AttendanceEmailReporter(
    private val context: Context,
    private val smtpSender: SmtpEmailSender = SmtpEmailSender(),
    private val senderEmailProvider: () -> String = { BuildConfig.SMTP_EMAIL },
    private val appPasswordProvider: () -> String = { BuildConfig.SMTP_APP_PASSWORD }
) {
    fun hasConfiguredSmtpCredentials(): Boolean {
        val appPassword = appPasswordProvider().trim()
        return appPassword.isNotEmpty() && appPassword != PLACEHOLDER_PASSWORD
    }

    fun resolveSenderAccountEmail(): String {
        return DeviceAccountResolver.resolveLoggedInGoogleEmail(context)
            ?: senderEmailProvider().trim().ifEmpty { TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL }
    }

    fun resolveRecipientEmail(): String = TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL

    suspend fun generateReportPdfFile(
        employee: Employee,
        log: AttendanceLog,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        syncedToCloud: Boolean,
        recentDailyLogs: List<AttendanceLog>,
        liveSelfiePath: String? = null
    ): File = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val recipient = resolveRecipientEmail()
        val pdfFile = AttendancePdfGenerator.generateReportPdf(
            context = context,
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = eventType,
            verificationMethod = verificationMethod,
            confidenceScore = confidenceScore,
            syncedToCloud = syncedToCloud,
            recentDailyLogs = recentDailyLogs,
            recipientEmail = recipient,
            liveSelfiePath = liveSelfiePath
        )
        pruneOldCachedReports(pdfFile)
        pdfFile
    }

    suspend fun generateAndSendReport(
        employee: Employee,
        log: AttendanceLog,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        syncedToCloud: Boolean,
        recentDailyLogs: List<AttendanceLog>,
        liveSelfiePath: String? = null
    ): EmailReportStatus = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val pdfFile = try {
            generateReportPdfFile(
                employee = employee,
                log = log,
                eventType = eventType,
                verificationMethod = verificationMethod,
                confidenceScore = confidenceScore,
                syncedToCloud = syncedToCloud,
                recentDailyLogs = recentDailyLogs,
                liveSelfiePath = liveSelfiePath
            )
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to generate attendance PDF report", e)
            return@withContext EmailReportStatus.QUEUED_OFFLINE
        }

        val senderEmail = resolveSenderAccountEmail()
        val recipientEmail = resolveRecipientEmail()
        val appPassword = appPasswordProvider().trim()
        if (appPassword.isEmpty() || appPassword == PLACEHOLDER_PASSWORD) {
            Log.i(TAG, "PDF generated for device Gmail dispatch (${pdfFile.name}, sender=$senderEmail)")
            return@withContext EmailReportStatus.NEEDS_CONFIG
        }

        val subject = buildSubject(eventType, employee)
        val body = buildPlainTextBody(
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = eventType,
            verificationMethod = verificationMethod,
            confidenceScore = confidenceScore,
            syncedToCloud = syncedToCloud,
            deviceAccountEmail = senderEmail
        )

        val livePhotoFile = liveSelfiePath
            ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            ?.let { File(it) }
            ?.takeIf { it.exists() && it.length() > 0L }

        return@withContext try {
            smtpSender.sendWithAttachment(
                config = SmtpConfig(
                    username = senderEmail,
                    appPassword = appPassword
                ),
                toEmail = recipientEmail,
                subject = subject,
                plainTextBody = body,
                attachmentFile = pdfFile,
                extraAttachmentFile = livePhotoFile
            )
            EmailReportStatus.SENT
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Background SMTP email deferred; PDF preserved for device Gmail: ${e.message}")
            EmailReportStatus.QUEUED_OFFLINE
        }
    }

    fun buildDeviceGmailShareIntent(
        pdfFile: File,
        employee: Employee,
        log: AttendanceLog,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        syncedToCloud: Boolean
    ): Intent {
        val recipientEmail = resolveRecipientEmail()
        val deviceSenderEmail = DeviceAccountResolver.resolveLoggedInGoogleEmail(context)
        val pdfUri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
            context,
            "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
            pdfFile
        )

        val subject = buildSubject(eventType, employee)
        val body = buildPlainTextBody(
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = eventType,
            verificationMethod = verificationMethod,
            confidenceScore = confidenceScore,
            syncedToCloud = syncedToCloud,
            deviceAccountEmail = deviceSenderEmail
        )

        val baseIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
            type = "application/pdf"
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_EMAIL, arrayOf(recipientEmail))
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, subject)
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, body)
            putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, pdfUri)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
        }

        val gmailTargeted = Intent(baseIntent).setPackage(GMAIL_PACKAGE_NAME)
        val canLaunchGmailDirectly = runCatching {
            context.packageManager.resolveActivity(gmailTargeted, 0) != null
        }.getOrDefault(false)

        return if (canLaunchGmailDirectly) {
            gmailTargeted
        } else {
            Intent.createChooser(baseIntent, "Send Attendance PDF Report").apply {
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun buildSubject(eventType: AttendanceEventType, employee: Employee): String {
        return "NICH Employee Attendance 3 • ${eventType.displayLabel} • ${employee.name} (${employee.employeeCode})"
    }

    private fun buildPlainTextBody(
        employee: Employee,
        log: AttendanceLog,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        syncedToCloud: Boolean,
        deviceAccountEmail: String?
    ): String {
        return buildString {
            appendLine("NICH Employee Attendance 3 — Automated Attendance Report")
            appendLine("==========================================================")
            appendLine("Employee Name: ${employee.name}")
            appendLine("Employee ID: ${employee.employeeCode}")
            appendLine("Department: ${employee.department}")
            appendLine("Role: ${employee.roleTitle}")
            appendLine("Event Type: ${eventType.displayLabel} (${eventType.wireValue})")
            appendLine("Verification Method: ${verificationMethod.displayLabel}")
            appendLine("Match Confidence: ${confidenceScore.coerceIn(0, 100)}%")
            appendLine("Timestamp: ${log.loggedTimeLabel} (${log.loggedDateKey})")
            appendLine("Time Authority: ${log.timeSource.ifBlank { "Network Time (NTP)" }}")
            appendLine("GPS Coordinates: ${log.gpsCoordinates.ifBlank { "Unavailable" }}")
            appendLine("Location Address: ${log.locationAddress.ifBlank { "Unavailable" }}")
            appendLine("Cloud Sync: ${if (syncedToCloud) "Cloud Firestore Live" else "Saved Offline (Queued)"}")
            if (!deviceAccountEmail.isNullOrBlank()) {
                appendLine("Dispatched From Device Account: $deviceAccountEmail")
            }
            appendLine()
            appendLine("The structured PDF attendance report (with live capture photo, GPS address, and weekly summary) is attached.")
        }
    }

    private fun pruneOldCachedReports(latestFile: File) {
        runCatching {
            val dir = latestFile.parentFile ?: return
            dir.listFiles()
                ?.filter { it.absolutePath != latestFile.absolutePath && it.extension.equals("pdf", ignoreCase = true) }
                ?.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }
                ?.drop(4)
                ?.forEach { it.delete() }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AttendanceEmail"
        private const val GMAIL_PACKAGE_NAME = "com.google.android.gm"
        const val TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL = "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com"
        private const val PLACEHOLDER_PASSWORD = "MY_GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD"
    }
}

package com.example.data.report

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument
import android.text.Layout
import android.text.StaticLayout
import android.text.TextDirectionHeuristics
import android.text.TextPaint
import android.text.TextUtils
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.BidiTextHelper
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream

import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.max

data class WeeklyDayRow(
    val dateKey: String,
    val dayDateLabel: String,
    val checkInLabel: String,
    val checkOutLabel: String,
    val dutyHoursLabel: String,
    val isPresent: Boolean,
    val dutyMinutes: Int
)

data class WeeklyReportSummary(
    val weekRangeLabel: String,
    val rows: List<WeeklyDayRow>,
    val totalWorkingDays: Int,
    val totalDutyHoursLabel: String,
    val overallStatusLabel: String,
    val generatedOnLabel: String
)

object AttendancePdfGenerator {

    // A4 Landscape to match the Weekly Attendance Report sample layout
    private const val PAGE_WIDTH = 842
    private const val PAGE_HEIGHT = 595
    private const val MARGIN_X = 28f

    fun generateReportPdf(
        context: Context,
        employee: Employee,
        log: AttendanceLog,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        syncedToCloud: Boolean,
        recentDailyLogs: List<AttendanceLog>,
        recipientEmail: String,
        liveSelfiePath: String? = null
    ): File {
        val reportsDir = File(context.cacheDir, "attendance_reports").apply {
            if (!exists()) mkdirs()
        }
        val safeCode = employee.employeeCode.replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]"), "_")
        val safeLogId = log.id.replace(Regex("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]"), "_")
        val outFile = File(reportsDir, "NICH_${safeCode}_${eventType.wireValue}_${safeLogId}.pdf")

        val weeklySummary = buildWeeklySummary(
            employee = employee,
            currentLog = log,
            recentDailyLogs = recentDailyLogs
        )

        val registeredPhotoPath = employee.localPhotoPath
            .takeIf { it.isNotBlank() && File(it).let { f -> f.exists() && f.length() > 0L } }
        val liveCapturePath = liveSelfiePath
            ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() && File(it).let { f -> f.exists() && f.length() > 0L } }

        val nativePdfSuccess = tryWriteNativeWeeklyPdf(
            outFile = outFile,
            employee = employee,
            currentLog = log,
            weeklySummary = weeklySummary,
            registeredPhotoPath = registeredPhotoPath ?: liveCapturePath,
            liveCapturePath = liveCapturePath ?: registeredPhotoPath,
            hasDistinctLiveCapture = liveCapturePath != null
        )

        if (!nativePdfSuccess || !outFile.exists() || outFile.length() == 0L) {
            writeFallbackPdf14(
                outFile = outFile,
                employee = employee,
                currentLog = log,
                weeklySummary = weeklySummary,
                recipientEmail = recipientEmail,
                portraitPath = liveCapturePath ?: registeredPhotoPath
            )
        }

        return outFile
    }

    internal fun buildWeeklySummary(
        employee: Employee,
        currentLog: AttendanceLog,
        recentDailyLogs: List<AttendanceLog>,
        generatedAt: Date = Date()
    ): WeeklyReportSummary {
        val anchorCal = parseDateKeyCalendar(currentLog.loggedDateKey) ?: Calendar.getInstance()

        // Snap anchor calendar to Monday of its ISO/standard Monday–Sunday week
        val mondayCal = (anchorCal.clone() as Calendar).apply {
            val dow = get(Calendar.DAY_OF_WEEK)
            val daysFromMonday = when (dow) {
                Calendar.MONDAY -> 0
                Calendar.TUESDAY -> 1
                Calendar.WEDNESDAY -> 2
                Calendar.THURSDAY -> 3
                Calendar.FRIDAY -> 4
                Calendar.SATURDAY -> 5
                Calendar.SUNDAY -> 6
                else -> 0
            }
            add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, -daysFromMonday)
        }
        val sundayCal = (mondayCal.clone() as Calendar).apply {
            add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, 6)
        }

        val dateKeyFormat = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US)
        val weekHeaderDateFormat = SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM yyyy", Locale.US)
        val rowDayDateFormat = SimpleDateFormat("EEEE, dd MMM yyyy", Locale.US)
        val generatedFormat = SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM yyyy  hh:mm a", Locale.US)

        val weekRangeLabel =
            "For the Week: ${weekHeaderDateFormat.format(mondayCal.time)} \u2013 ${weekHeaderDateFormat.format(sundayCal.time)}"

        // Merge recentDailyLogs with currentLog (deduplicated by id) for this employee
        val combinedEmployeeLogs = (listOf(currentLog) + recentDailyLogs)
            .distinctBy { it.id.ifBlank { "${it.loggedDateKey}_${it.eventType}_${it.loggedTimeLabel}" } }
            .filter { item ->
                (employee.id.isNotBlank() && item.employeeId == employee.id) ||
                    (employee.employeeCode.isNotBlank() && item.employeeCode == employee.employeeCode)
            }

        val logsByDate = combinedEmployeeLogs.groupBy { it.loggedDateKey }
        val rows = mutableListOf<WeeklyDayRow>()
        var totalWorkingDays = 0
        var totalDutyMinutes = 0

        val cursor = mondayCal.clone() as Calendar
        for (i in 0 until 7) {
            val dayKey = dateKeyFormat.format(cursor.time)
            val dayDateLabel = rowDayDateFormat.format(cursor.time)
            val dayLogs = logsByDate[dayKey].orEmpty()

            val checkInLogs = dayLogs.filter { it.eventType == AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue }
            val checkOutLogs = dayLogs.filter { it.eventType == AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT.wireValue }

            val firstCheckIn = checkInLogs
                .minByOrNull { parseTimeLabelMinutes(it.loggedTimeLabel) ?: Int.MAX_VALUE }
            val lastCheckOut = checkOutLogs
                .maxByOrNull { parseTimeLabelMinutes(it.loggedTimeLabel) ?: Int.MIN_VALUE }

            val checkInText = firstCheckIn?.loggedTimeLabel?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "--"
            val checkOutText = lastCheckOut?.loggedTimeLabel?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() } ?: "--"

            val inMinutes = firstCheckIn?.let { parseTimeLabelMinutes(it.loggedTimeLabel) }
            val outMinutes = lastCheckOut?.let { parseTimeLabelMinutes(it.loggedTimeLabel) }

            val dutyMinutes = if (inMinutes != null && outMinutes != null && outMinutes >= inMinutes) {
                outMinutes - inMinutes
            } else {
                0
            }
            val dutyHoursText = when {
                inMinutes != null && outMinutes != null && outMinutes >= inMinutes ->
                    formatDurationHoursMinutes(dutyMinutes)
                dayLogs.isNotEmpty() -> "00:00 Hrs"
                else -> "--"
            }

            val present = dayLogs.isNotEmpty()
            if (present) {
                totalWorkingDays++
                totalDutyMinutes += dutyMinutes
            }

            rows += WeeklyDayRow(
                dateKey = dayKey,
                dayDateLabel = dayDateLabel,
                checkInLabel = formatShortTimeLabel(checkInText),
                checkOutLabel = formatShortTimeLabel(checkOutText),
                dutyHoursLabel = dutyHoursText,
                isPresent = present,
                dutyMinutes = dutyMinutes
            )
            cursor.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, 1)
        }

        return WeeklyReportSummary(
            weekRangeLabel = weekRangeLabel,
            rows = rows,
            totalWorkingDays = max(totalWorkingDays, 1),
            totalDutyHoursLabel = formatDurationHoursMinutes(totalDutyMinutes),
            overallStatusLabel = "Present",
            generatedOnLabel = generatedFormat.format(generatedAt)
        )
    }

    private fun tryWriteNativeWeeklyPdf(
        outFile: File,
        employee: Employee,
        currentLog: AttendanceLog,
        weeklySummary: WeeklyReportSummary,
        registeredPhotoPath: String?,
        liveCapturePath: String?,
        hasDistinctLiveCapture: Boolean
    ): Boolean {
        return try {
            val document = PdfDocument()
            try {
                val pageInfo = PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT, 1).create()
                val page = document.startPage(pageInfo)
                val canvas = page.canvas

                // Pure white sheet background
                canvas.drawColor(Color.WHITE)

                val darkInk = Color.rgb(17, 17, 17)
                val mutedInk = Color.rgb(65, 65, 65)
                val tableHeaderFill = Color.rgb(229, 232, 236)
                val gridStrokeColor = Color.rgb(45, 45, 45)
                val greenBadgeColor = Color.rgb(22, 163, 74)

                val linePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = 1.6f
                }
                val thinDividerPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = Color.rgb(55, 55, 55)
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = 1.1f
                }
                val tableBorderPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = gridStrokeColor
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = 1.0f
                }
                val headerFillPaint = Paint().apply {
                    color = tableHeaderFill
                    style = Paint.Style.FILL
                }

                val orgTitlePaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 13.5f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                }
                val orgSubPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = mutedInk
                    textSize = 10.5f
                }
                val mainTitlePaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 18.5f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                }
                val weekSubPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = mutedInk
                    textSize = 11f
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                }
                val rightSystemPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 10f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                }
                val metaLabelPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 11.5f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                }
                val metaValuePaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 11.5f
                }
                val tableHeaderTextPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 11.5f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                }
                val cellTextPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 11f
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                }
                val cellLeftTextPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 11f
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
                }
                val statusPillTextPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = Color.WHITE
                    textSize = 10.5f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                }
                val footerBoldPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 10.5f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                }
                val footerRegularPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 10.5f
                }

                // =========================================================
                // 1. TOP HEADER BAR (Left Org | Center Title | Right Clock)
                // =========================================================
                canvas.drawText("NICH Employee Attendance 3", MARGIN_X, 42f, orgTitlePaint)
                canvas.drawText("Karachi, Pakistan", MARGIN_X, 58f, orgSubPaint)

                canvas.drawLine(252f, 24f, 252f, 66f, thinDividerPaint)

                val centerHeaderX = 436f
                canvas.drawText("WEEKLY ATTENDANCE REPORT", centerHeaderX, 44f, mainTitlePaint)
                canvas.drawText(weeklySummary.weekRangeLabel, centerHeaderX, 62f, weekSubPaint)

                canvas.drawLine(622f, 24f, 622f, 66f, thinDividerPaint)

                // Vector Clock Icon matching sample
                val clockCx = 656f
                val clockCy = 46f
                val clockRadius = 18f
                val clockPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = 2.6f
                    strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND
                }
                canvas.drawCircle(clockCx, clockCy, clockRadius, clockPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(clockCx, clockCy, clockCx, clockCy - 10f, clockPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(clockCx, clockCy, clockCx + 8f, clockCy + 5f, clockPaint)

                canvas.drawText("ATTENDANCE", 686f, 36f, rightSystemPaint)
                canvas.drawText("MANAGEMENT", 686f, 49f, rightSystemPaint)
                canvas.drawText("SYSTEM", 686f, 62f, rightSystemPaint)

                // Thick header bottom rule
                canvas.drawLine(MARGIN_X, 76f, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN_X, 76f, linePaint)

                // =========================================================
                // 2. REGISTERED PHOTO + EMPLOYEE INFO + MIDDLE LIVE CAPTURE + KPI SUMMARY
                // =========================================================
                val photoBoxTop = 88f
                val photoBoxSize = 108f
                val leftPhotoRect = RectF(
                    MARGIN_X,
                    photoBoxTop,
                    MARGIN_X + photoBoxSize,
                    photoBoxTop + photoBoxSize
                )
                drawFramedPortraitBox(
                    canvas = canvas,
                    rect = leftPhotoRect,
                    photoPath = registeredPhotoPath,
                    fallbackInitials = employee.name.trim().take(2).uppercase(Locale.US).ifEmpty { "ST" },
                    borderPaint = thinDividerPaint,
                    caption = "Employee Photo",
                    darkInk = darkInk,
                    mutedInk = mutedInk
                )

                // Left-Middle Employee Details Column
                val cleanDisplayName = BidiTextHelper.stripBidiControls(
                    BidiTextHelper.formatEmployeeHeaderTitle(employee.name, "")
                ).ifBlank { employee.name }
                val cardNo = employee.checkInCode.ifBlank {
                    employee.passcode
                }.ifBlank { employee.employeeCode }

                val employeeDetails = listOf(
                    "Employee Name" to cleanDisplayName,
                    "Employee ID" to employee.employeeCode.ifBlank { "N/A" },
                    "Department" to employee.department.ifBlank { "Operations" },
                    "Designation" to employee.roleTitle.ifBlank { "Staff" },
                    "Card No" to cardNo
                )

                val detailLabelX = 148f
                val detailColonX = 248f
                val detailValueX = 258f
                val detailMaxValueW = 162f
                var detailY = 90f
                employeeDetails.forEach { (label, value) ->
                    canvas.drawText(label, detailLabelX, detailY + 13f, metaLabelPaint)
                    canvas.drawText(":", detailColonX, detailY + 13f, metaLabelPaint)
                    drawBidiBoundedText(
                        canvas = canvas,
                        text = value,
                        x = detailValueX,
                        yTop = detailY,
                        maxWidth = detailMaxValueW,
                        maxHeight = 20f,
                        paint = metaValuePaint
                    )
                    detailY += 23f
                }

                // Divider 1 before Middle Live Capture Image
                canvas.drawLine(426f, 90f, 426f, 208f, thinDividerPaint)

                // MIDDLE: Live Captured Attendance Image (taken right when attendance is marked)
                val liveBorderPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = if (hasDistinctLiveCapture) greenBadgeColor else Color.rgb(55, 55, 55)
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = if (hasDistinctLiveCapture) 1.6f else 1.1f
                }
                val middleLiveRect = RectF(
                    438f,
                    photoBoxTop,
                    438f + photoBoxSize,
                    photoBoxTop + photoBoxSize
                )
                drawFramedPortraitBox(
                    canvas = canvas,
                    rect = middleLiveRect,
                    photoPath = liveCapturePath,
                    fallbackInitials = "LIVE",
                    borderPaint = liveBorderPaint,
                    caption = "Live Capture",
                    darkInk = darkInk,
                    mutedInk = if (hasDistinctLiveCapture) greenBadgeColor else mutedInk
                )

                // Divider 2 after Middle Live Capture Image
                canvas.drawLine(558f, 90f, 558f, 208f, thinDividerPaint)

                // Right Summary KPI Column
                val kpiLabelX = 570f
                val kpiColonX = 702f
                val kpiValueX = 714f

                canvas.drawText("Total Working Days", kpiLabelX, 114f, metaLabelPaint)
                canvas.drawText(":", kpiColonX, 114f, metaLabelPaint)
                canvas.drawText(weeklySummary.totalWorkingDays.toString(), kpiValueX, 114f, metaValuePaint)

                canvas.drawText("Total Duty Hours", kpiLabelX, 146f, metaLabelPaint)
                canvas.drawText(":", kpiColonX, 146f, metaLabelPaint)
                canvas.drawText(weeklySummary.totalDutyHoursLabel, kpiValueX, 146f, metaValuePaint)

                canvas.drawText("Attendance Status", kpiLabelX, 179f, metaLabelPaint)
                canvas.drawText(":", kpiColonX, 179f, metaLabelPaint)

                val badgeRect = RectF(kpiValueX, 162f, kpiValueX + 82f, 185f)
                val greenPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = greenBadgeColor
                    style = Paint.Style.FILL
                }
                canvas.drawRoundRect(badgeRect, 11f, 11f, greenPaint)
                canvas.drawText(
                    weeklySummary.overallStatusLabel,
                    badgeRect.centerX(),
                    badgeRect.centerY() + 4f,
                    statusPillTextPaint
                )

                // =========================================================
                // 2B. VERIFIED GPS, LOCATION ADDRESS & NTP TIME LOCK STRIP
                // =========================================================
                val securityStripTop = 212f
                val securityStripBottom = 232f
                val stripBgPaint = Paint().apply {
                    color = Color.rgb(241, 245, 249)
                    style = Paint.Style.FILL
                }
                canvas.drawRoundRect(
                    RectF(MARGIN_X, securityStripTop, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN_X, securityStripBottom),
                    5f,
                    5f,
                    stripBgPaint
                )
                canvas.drawRoundRect(
                    RectF(MARGIN_X, securityStripTop, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN_X, securityStripBottom),
                    5f,
                    5f,
                    tableBorderPaint
                )
                val gpsLabel = currentLog.gpsCoordinates.ifBlank { "Location not fixed" }
                val addressLabel = currentLog.locationAddress.ifBlank { "Offline / Address unavailable" }
                val timeAuthLabel = currentLog.timeSource.ifBlank { "NTP Network Time Locked" }
                val stripTextPaint = TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = 9.2f
                }
                val stripSummary = "GPS: $gpsLabel  |  Address: $addressLabel  |  Time: $timeAuthLabel"
                drawBidiBoundedText(
                    canvas = canvas,
                    text = stripSummary,
                    x = MARGIN_X + 8f,
                    yTop = securityStripTop + 2f,
                    maxWidth = PAGE_WIDTH - (MARGIN_X * 2f) - 16f,
                    maxHeight = 16f,
                    paint = stripTextPaint
                )

                // =========================================================
                // 3. WEEKLY 7-DAY ATTENDANCE GRID (Mon -> Sun)
                // =========================================================
                val tableLeft = MARGIN_X
                val tableRight = PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN_X
                val tableTop = 240f
                val headerRowHeight = 29f
                val dataRowHeight = 30f

                // Column boundaries summing to 786f
                val colXs = floatArrayOf(
                    tableLeft,           // 28f  (Day / Date: 238f)
                    tableLeft + 238f,    // 266f (Check In: 138f)
                    tableLeft + 376f,    // 404f (Check Out: 138f)
                    tableLeft + 514f,    // 542f (Duty Hours: 136f)
                    tableLeft + 650f,    // 678f (Status: 136f)
                    tableRight           // 814f
                )

                // Table header background
                canvas.drawRect(
                    tableLeft,
                    tableTop,
                    tableRight,
                    tableTop + headerRowHeight,
                    headerFillPaint
                )

                val headers = listOf("Day / Date", "Check In", "Check Out", "Duty Hours", "Status")
                val headerTextY = tableTop + 19f
                headers.forEachIndexed { idx, title ->
                    val centerX = (colXs[idx] + colXs[idx + 1]) / 2f
                    canvas.drawText(title, centerX, headerTextY, tableHeaderTextPaint)
                }

                // Data Rows
                val checkCirclePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = greenBadgeColor
                    style = Paint.Style.FILL
                }
                val checkMarkPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = Color.WHITE
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = 2.0f
                    strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND
                    strokeJoin = Paint.Join.ROUND
                }

                weeklySummary.rows.forEachIndexed { index, row ->
                    val rTop = tableTop + headerRowHeight + (index * dataRowHeight)
                    val rBottom = rTop + dataRowHeight
                    val textBaselineY = rTop + 20f

                    // Day / Date (left padded)
                    canvas.drawText(row.dayDateLabel, colXs[0] + 12f, textBaselineY, cellLeftTextPaint)

                    // Check In
                    canvas.drawText(
                        row.checkInLabel,
                        (colXs[1] + colXs[2]) / 2f,
                        textBaselineY,
                        cellTextPaint
                    )

                    // Check Out
                    canvas.drawText(
                        row.checkOutLabel,
                        (colXs[2] + colXs[3]) / 2f,
                        textBaselineY,
                        cellTextPaint
                    )

                    // Duty Hours
                    canvas.drawText(
                        row.dutyHoursLabel,
                        (colXs[3] + colXs[4]) / 2f,
                        textBaselineY,
                        cellTextPaint
                    )

                    // Status cell
                    val statusCenterX = (colXs[4] + colXs[5]) / 2f
                    if (row.isPresent) {
                        val iconCx = statusCenterX - 24f
                        val iconCy = rTop + (dataRowHeight / 2f)
                        canvas.drawCircle(iconCx, iconCy, 8f, checkCirclePaint)
                        val tick = Path().apply {
                            moveTo(iconCx - 3.6f, iconCy + 0.2f)
                            lineTo(iconCx - 1.0f, iconCy + 3.0f)
                            lineTo(iconCx + 3.8f, iconCy - 2.6f)
                        }
                        canvas.drawPath(tick, checkMarkPaint)
                        canvas.drawText("Present", statusCenterX + 12f, textBaselineY, cellTextPaint)
                    } else {
                        canvas.drawText("--", statusCenterX, textBaselineY, cellTextPaint)
                    }

                    // Horizontal row bottom grid line
                    canvas.drawLine(tableLeft, rBottom, tableRight, rBottom, tableBorderPaint)
                }

                val tableBottom = tableTop + headerRowHeight + (weeklySummary.rows.size * dataRowHeight)
                // Outer border & header bottom line & vertical grid dividers
                canvas.drawRect(tableLeft, tableTop, tableRight, tableBottom, tableBorderPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(tableLeft, tableTop + headerRowHeight, tableRight, tableTop + headerRowHeight, tableBorderPaint)
                for (i in 1 until colXs.size - 1) {
                    canvas.drawLine(colXs[i], tableTop, colXs[i], tableBottom, tableBorderPaint)
                }

                // =========================================================
                // 4. FOOTER RULE & SIGNATURES
                // =========================================================
                val footerRuleY = 508f
                canvas.drawLine(MARGIN_X, footerRuleY, PAGE_WIDTH - MARGIN_X, footerRuleY, linePaint)

                canvas.drawText("Report Generated On:", MARGIN_X, 534f, footerBoldPaint)
                canvas.drawText(weeklySummary.generatedOnLabel, MARGIN_X, 551f, footerRegularPaint)

                // Center HR Manager Signature Line
                val hrLineLeft = 308f
                val hrLineRight = 508f
                canvas.drawLine(hrLineLeft, 550f, hrLineRight, 550f, thinDividerPaint)
                canvas.drawText(
                    "HR Manager",
                    (hrLineLeft + hrLineRight) / 2f,
                    567f,
                    TextPaint(footerBoldPaint).apply { textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER }
                )

                // Right Authorized Signature Flourish + Line
                val authLineLeft = 594f
                val authLineRight = 794f
                val sigPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = Color.rgb(30, 64, 175)
                    style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    strokeWidth = 1.9f
                    strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND
                    strokeJoin = Paint.Join.ROUND
                }
                val sigPath = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(656f, 542f)
                    cubicTo(648f, 520f, 672f, 514f, 666f, 538f)
                    cubicTo(662f, 546f, 682f, 530f, 688f, 540f)
                    cubicTo(692f, 545f, 704f, 528f, 712f, 539f)
                    cubicTo(718f, 544f, 728f, 532f, 738f, 538f)
                }
                canvas.drawPath(sigPath, sigPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(authLineLeft, 550f, authLineRight, 550f, thinDividerPaint)
                canvas.drawText(
                    "Authorized Signature",
                    (authLineLeft + authLineRight) / 2f,
                    567f,
                    TextPaint(footerBoldPaint).apply { textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER }
                )

                document.finishPage(page)
                FileOutputStream(outFile).use { stream ->
                    document.writeTo(stream)
                }
                true
            } finally {
                runCatching { document.close() }
            }
        } catch (_: Throwable) {
            false
        }
    }

    private fun drawFramedPortraitBox(
        canvas: Canvas,
        rect: RectF,
        photoPath: String?,
        fallbackInitials: String,
        borderPaint: Paint,
        caption: String,
        darkInk: Int,
        mutedInk: Int
    ) {
        val portraitBitmap = decodeSampledBitmap(photoPath, 320)
        if (portraitBitmap != null) {
            canvas.save()
            val clipPath = Path().apply {
                addRoundRect(rect, 10f, 10f, Path.Direction.CW)
            }
            canvas.clipPath(clipPath)
            val srcRect = centerCropRect(portraitBitmap.width, portraitBitmap.height, 1f)
            canvas.drawBitmap(
                portraitBitmap,
                srcRect,
                rect,
                Paint(Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG)
            )
            portraitBitmap.recycle()
            canvas.restore()
        } else {
            val emptyPhotoBg = Paint().apply {
                color = Color.rgb(241, 245, 249)
                style = Paint.Style.FILL
            }
            canvas.drawRoundRect(rect, 10f, 10f, emptyPhotoBg)
            canvas.drawText(
                fallbackInitials,
                rect.centerX(),
                rect.centerY() + 6f,
                TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                    color = darkInk
                    textSize = if (fallbackInitials.length <= 2) 22f else 15f
                    typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                    textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
                }
            )
        }
        canvas.drawRoundRect(rect, 10f, 10f, borderPaint)
        canvas.drawText(
            caption,
            rect.centerX(),
            rect.bottom + 13f,
            TextPaint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                color = mutedInk
                textSize = 8.5f
                typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
            }
        )
    }

    private fun centerCropRect(srcW: Int, srcH: Int, targetAspect: Float): Rect {
        val srcAspect = srcW.toFloat() / srcH.toFloat().coerceAtLeast(1f)
        return if (srcAspect > targetAspect) {
            val newW = (srcH * targetAspect).toInt().coerceIn(1, srcW)
            val left = (srcW - newW) / 2
            Rect(left, 0, left + newW, srcH)
        } else {
            val newH = (srcW / targetAspect).toInt().coerceIn(1, srcH)
            val top = (srcH - newH) / 2
            Rect(0, top, srcW, top + newH)
        }
    }

    private fun parseDateKeyCalendar(dateKey: String): Calendar? {
        if (dateKey.isBlank()) return null
        return runCatching {
            val fmt = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", Locale.US).apply { isLenient = false }
            val parsed = fmt.parse(dateKey.trim()) ?: return null
            Calendar.getInstance().apply { time = parsed }
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    internal fun parseTimeLabelMinutes(timeLabel: String): Int? {
        val trimmed = timeLabel.trim()
        if (trimmed.isEmpty() || trimmed == "--") return null
        val patterns = listOf("hh:mm:ss a", "h:mm:ss a", "hh:mm a", "h:mm a", "HH:mm:ss", "HH:mm")
        for (pattern in patterns) {
            val parsed = runCatching {
                val fmt = SimpleDateFormat(pattern, Locale.US)
                fmt.parse(trimmed)
            }.getOrNull()
            if (parsed != null) {
                val cal = Calendar.getInstance().apply { time = parsed }
                return cal.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY) * 60 + cal.get(Calendar.MINUTE)
            }
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun formatShortTimeLabel(raw: String): String {
        if (raw == "--" || raw.isBlank()) return "--"
        val minutes = parseTimeLabelMinutes(raw) ?: return raw
        val cal = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
            set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, minutes / 60)
            set(Calendar.MINUTE, minutes % 60)
        }
        return SimpleDateFormat("hh:mm a", Locale.US).format(cal.time)
    }

    private fun formatDurationHoursMinutes(totalMinutes: Int): String {
        val safe = totalMinutes.coerceAtLeast(0)
        val hours = safe / 60
        val mins = safe % 60
        return String.format(Locale.US, "%02d:%02d Hrs", hours, mins)
    }

    private fun drawBidiBoundedText(
        canvas: Canvas,
        text: String,
        x: Float,
        yTop: Float,
        maxWidth: Float,
        maxHeight: Float,
        paint: TextPaint,
        maxLines: Int = 1
    ) {
        if (text.isBlank()) return
        val hasIsolates = text.indexOf('\u2066') >= 0 || text.indexOf('\u2067') >= 0
        val prepared = if (hasIsolates) text else BidiTextHelper.isolateMixedText(text)
        val widthPx = maxWidth.toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)
        val layout = StaticLayout.Builder
            .obtain(prepared, 0, prepared.length, paint, widthPx)
            .setAlignment(Layout.Alignment.ALIGN_NORMAL)
            .setTextDirection(TextDirectionHeuristics.LTR)
            .setMaxLines(maxLines)
            .setEllipsize(TextUtils.TruncateAt.END)
            .setIncludePad(false)
            .build()
        val verticalOffset = ((maxHeight - layout.height) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
        canvas.save()
        canvas.translate(x, yTop + verticalOffset)
        canvas.clipRect(0f, -2f, maxWidth, maxHeight)
        layout.draw(canvas)
        canvas.restore()
    }

    private fun decodeSampledBitmap(path: String?, reqSizePx: Int): Bitmap? {
        if (path.isNullOrBlank()) return null
        val file = File(path)
        if (!file.exists() || file.length() <= 0L) return null
        return runCatching {
            val opts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
            BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath, opts)
            var sampleSize = 1
            val maxDim = maxOf(opts.outWidth, opts.outHeight)
            while (maxDim / sampleSize > reqSizePx * 2) {
                sampleSize *= 2
            }
            val decodeOpts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = sampleSize }
            BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath, decodeOpts)
        }.getOrNull()
    }

    private fun writeFallbackPdf14(
        outFile: File,
        employee: Employee,
        currentLog: AttendanceLog,
        weeklySummary: WeeklyReportSummary,
        recipientEmail: String,
        portraitPath: String?
    ) {
        val cardNo = employee.checkInCode.ifBlank { employee.passcode }.ifBlank { employee.employeeCode }
        val gpsText = currentLog.gpsCoordinates.ifBlank { "Unavailable" }
        val addrText = currentLog.locationAddress.ifBlank { "Unavailable" }
        val timeAuth = currentLog.timeSource.ifBlank { "NTP Network Time Locked" }
        val lines = buildList {
            add("NICH EMPLOYEE ATTENDANCE 3  |  WEEKLY ATTENDANCE REPORT  |  ATTENDANCE MANAGEMENT SYSTEM")
            add(weeklySummary.weekRangeLabel)
            add("------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
            add("Employee Name: ${BidiTextHelper.stripBidiControls(employee.name)}")
            add("Employee ID: ${employee.employeeCode}   Department: ${employee.department}   Card No: $cardNo")
            add("Total Working Days: ${weeklySummary.totalWorkingDays}   Total Duty Hours: ${weeklySummary.totalDutyHoursLabel}   Status: ${weeklySummary.overallStatusLabel}")
            add("GPS Coordinates: $gpsText   |   Time Sync: $timeAuth")
            add("Location Address: $addrText")
            if (!portraitPath.isNullOrBlank()) {
                val photoFile = File(portraitPath)
                if (photoFile.exists()) {
                    add("Live Capture Photo: Embedded (${photoFile.name}, ${photoFile.length()} bytes)")
                }
            }
            add("------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
            add("Day / Date                 | Check In  | Check Out | Duty Hours | Status")
            weeklySummary.rows.forEach { row ->
                val statusStr = if (row.isPresent) "Present" else "--"
                add("${row.dayDateLabel.padEnd(26)} | ${row.checkInLabel.padEnd(9)} | ${row.checkOutLabel.padEnd(9)} | ${row.dutyHoursLabel.padEnd(10)} | $statusStr")
            }
            add("------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
            add("Report Generated On: ${weeklySummary.generatedOnLabel}   |   Recipient: $recipientEmail")
        }

        writeSimpleAsciiLinesPdf(outFile, lines)
    }

    private fun writeSimpleAsciiLinesPdf(outFile: File, lines: List<String>) {
        val maxLinesPerDoc = lines.take(28)
        val contentStream = buildString {
            append("BT\n")
            append("/F1 10 Tf\n")
            var cursorY = 555
            maxLinesPerDoc.forEachIndexed { idx, rawLine ->
                val safe = escapePdfText(rawLine)
                if (idx == 0) {
                    append("28 $cursorY Td\n")
                } else {
                    append("0 -18 Td\n")
                }
                append("($safe) Tj\n")
                cursorY -= 18
            }
            append("ET\n")
        }

        val objects = listOf(
            "1 0 obj\n<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>\nendobj\n",
            "2 0 obj\n<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>\nendobj\n",
            "3 0 obj\n<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 842 595] /Contents 4 0 R /Resources << /Font << /F1 5 0 R >> >> >>\nendobj\n",
            "4 0 obj\n<< /Length ${contentStream.toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII).size} >>\nstream\n${contentStream}endstream\nendobj\n",
            "5 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Courier >>\nendobj\n"
        )

        val pdfBuilder = StringBuilder()
        pdfBuilder.append("%PDF-1.4\n")
        val offsets = mutableListOf(0)
        objects.forEach { obj ->
            offsets += pdfBuilder.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII).size
            pdfBuilder.append(obj)
        }
        val xrefOffset = pdfBuilder.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII).size
        pdfBuilder.append("xref\n0 ${objects.size + 1}\n")
        pdfBuilder.append("0000000000 65535 f \n")
        for (i in 1..objects.size) {
            pdfBuilder.append(String.format(Locale.US, "%010d 00000 n \n", offsets[i]))
        }
        pdfBuilder.append("trailer\n<< /Size ${objects.size + 1} /Root 1 0 R >>\n")
        pdfBuilder.append("startxref\n$xrefOffset\n%%EOF\n")

        FileOutputStream(outFile).use { stream ->
            stream.write(pdfBuilder.toString().toByteArray(Charsets.US_ASCII))
        }
    }

    private fun escapePdfText(input: String): String {
        val stripped = BidiTextHelper.stripBidiControls(input)
        val ascii = buildString(stripped.length) {
            for (ch in stripped) {
                when (ch) {
                    '\\' -> append("\\\\")
                    '(' -> append("\\(")
                    ')' -> append("\\)")
                    in ' '..'~' -> append(ch)
                    '•', '—', '–' -> append('-')
                    else -> if (ch.code > 0x007F) append('?') else Unit
                }
            }
        }
        return ascii.take(120)
    }
}
