// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/report/SmtpEmailSender.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.report

import android.util.Base64
import java.io.BufferedInputStream
import java.io.BufferedOutputStream
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.Socket
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.UUID
import javax.net.ssl.SSLSocketFactory

data class SmtpConfig(
    val host: String = "smtp.gmail.com",
    val port: Int = 465,
    val useSsl: Boolean = true,
    val username: String,
    val appPassword: String,
    val connectTimeoutMs: Int = 12_000,
    val readTimeoutMs: Int = 15_000
)

fun interface SmtpSocketConnector {
    fun connect(config: SmtpConfig): Socket
}

object DefaultSmtpSocketConnector : SmtpSocketConnector {
    override fun connect(config: SmtpConfig): Socket {
        val socket = if (config.useSsl) {
            SSLSocketFactory.getDefault().createSocket()
        } else {
            Socket()
        }
        socket.soTimeout = config.readTimeoutMs
        socket.connect(InetSocketAddress(config.host, config.port), config.connectTimeoutMs)
        return socket
    }
}

class SmtpEmailSender(
    private val socketConnector: SmtpSocketConnector = DefaultSmtpSocketConnector
) {
    fun sendWithAttachment(
        config: SmtpConfig,
        toEmail: String,
        subject: String,
        plainTextBody: String,
        attachmentFile: File,
        extraAttachmentFile: File? = null
    ) {
        val cleanPassword = config.appPassword.replace(" ", "").trim()
        require(config.username.isNotBlank()) { "SMTP sender email must not be blank" }
        require(cleanPassword.isNotBlank()) { "SMTP app password must not be blank" }
        require(toEmail.isNotBlank()) { "Recipient email must not be blank" }
        require(attachmentFile.exists() && attachmentFile.length() > 0L) {
            "PDF attachment file is missing or empty"
        }

        val attachmentBytes = attachmentFile.readBytes()
        val extraBytes = extraAttachmentFile
            ?.takeIf { it.exists() && it.length() > 0L }
            ?.readBytes()
        val mimePayload = buildMimeMessage(
            fromEmail = config.username.trim(),
            toEmail = toEmail.trim(),
            subject = subject,
            plainTextBody = plainTextBody,
            attachmentName = attachmentFile.name,
            attachmentBytes = attachmentBytes,
            extraAttachmentName = extraAttachmentFile?.name,
            extraAttachmentBytes = extraBytes
        )

        socketConnector.connect(config).use { socket ->
            val input = BufferedInputStream(socket.getInputStream())
            val output = BufferedOutputStream(socket.getOutputStream())

            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 220, "SMTP greeting")

            sendLine(output, "EHLO nich-terminal")
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 250, "EHLO")

            sendLine(output, "AUTH LOGIN")
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 334, "AUTH LOGIN")

            sendLine(output, encodeBase64SingleLine(config.username.trim()))
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 334, "AUTH username")

            sendLine(output, encodeBase64SingleLine(cleanPassword))
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 235, "AUTH password")

            sendLine(output, "MAIL FROM:<${config.username.trim()}>")
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 250, "MAIL FROM")

            sendLine(output, "RCPT TO:<${toEmail.trim()}>")
            val rcptReply = readSmtpResponse(input)
            if (rcptReply.code != 250 && rcptReply.code != 251) {
                throw IOException("SMTP RCPT TO rejected (${rcptReply.code}): ${rcptReply.message}")
            }

            sendLine(output, "DATA")
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 354, "DATA")

            output.write(mimePayload.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
            output.write("\r\n.\r\n".toByteArray(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII))
            output.flush()
            expectCode(readSmtpResponse(input), 250, "DATA body")

            runCatching {
                sendLine(output, "QUIT")
                readSmtpResponse(input)
            }
        }
    }

    internal fun buildMimeMessage(
        fromEmail: String,
        toEmail: String,
        subject: String,
        plainTextBody: String,
        attachmentName: String,
        attachmentBytes: ByteArray,
        extraAttachmentName: String? = null,
        extraAttachmentBytes: ByteArray? = null
    ): String {
        val boundary = "====NICH_ATT_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "")}===="
        val encodedSubject = "=?UTF-8?B?${encodeBase64SingleLine(subject)}?="
        val encodedBody = Base64.encodeToString(
            plainTextBody.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
            Base64.CRLF
        ).trimEnd('\r', '\n')
        val encodedPdf = Base64.encodeToString(attachmentBytes, Base64.CRLF).trimEnd('\r', '\n')

        return buildString {
            append("From: NICH Employee Attendance 3 <").append(fromEmail).append(">\r\n")
            append("To: <").append(toEmail).append(">\r\n")
            append("Subject: ").append(encodedSubject).append("\r\n")
            append("MIME-Version: 1.0\r\n")
            append("Content-Type: multipart/mixed; boundary=\"").append(boundary).append("\"\r\n")
            append("\r\n")
            append("--").append(boundary).append("\r\n")
            append("Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8\r\n")
            append("Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64\r\n")
            append("\r\n")
            append(encodedBody).append("\r\n")
            append("--").append(boundary).append("\r\n")
            append("Content-Type: application/pdf; name=\"").append(attachmentName).append("\"\r\n")
            append("Content-Disposition: attachment; filename=\"").append(attachmentName).append("\"\r\n")
            append("Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64\r\n")
            append("\r\n")
            append(encodedPdf).append("\r\n")
            if (!extraAttachmentName.isNullOrBlank() && extraAttachmentBytes != null && extraAttachmentBytes.isNotEmpty()) {
                val encodedPhoto = Base64.encodeToString(extraAttachmentBytes, Base64.CRLF).trimEnd('\r', '\n')
                append("--").append(boundary).append("\r\n")
                append("Content-Type: image/jpeg; name=\"").append(extraAttachmentName).append("\"\r\n")
                append("Content-Disposition: attachment; filename=\"").append(extraAttachmentName).append("\"\r\n")
                append("Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64\r\n")
                append("\r\n")
                append(encodedPhoto).append("\r\n")
            }
            append("--").append(boundary).append("--\r\n")
        }
    }

    private fun encodeBase64SingleLine(text: String): String {
        return Base64.encodeToString(text.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), Base64.NO_WRAP)
    }

    private fun sendLine(output: BufferedOutputStream, line: String) {
        output.write(line.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
        output.write("\r\n".toByteArray(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII))
        output.flush()
    }

    private data class SmtpReply(val code: Int, val message: String)

    private fun readSmtpResponse(input: BufferedInputStream): SmtpReply {
        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
        var code = -1
        while (true) {
            val line = readAsciiLine(input) ?: throw IOException("Unexpected EOF from SMTP server")
            lines += line
            if (line.length >= 3 && line.substring(0, 3).all { it.isDigit() }) {
                code = line.substring(0, 3).toInt()
                if (line.length == 3 || line[3] == ' ') {
                    break
                }
            } else {
                break
            }
        }
        return SmtpReply(code = code, message = lines.joinToString(" | "))
    }

    private fun readAsciiLine(input: BufferedInputStream): String? {
        val buffer = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        var b = input.read()
        if (b == -1) return null
        while (b != -1) {
            if (b == '\n'.code) break
            if (b != '\r'.code) buffer.write(b)
            b = input.read()
        }
        return buffer.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name())
    }

    private fun expectCode(reply: SmtpReply, expectedCode: Int, stage: String) {
        if (reply.code != expectedCode) {
            throw IOException("SMTP $stage failed (${reply.code}): ${reply.message}")
        }
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/repository/AttendanceRepository.kt

package com.example.data.repository

import android.content.Context
import com.example.R
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import com.example.data.remote.OperationType
import com.example.data.remote.handleFirestoreError
import com.google.firebase.auth.FirebaseAuth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.DocumentSnapshot
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import com.google.firebase.firestore.Query
import com.google.firebase.firestore.snapshots
import kotlinx.coroutines.CancellationException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.catch
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.emitAll
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import java.util.UUID

class AttendanceRepository(
    private val db: FirebaseFirestore,
    private val auth: FirebaseAuth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance()
) {
    constructor(context: Context) : this(
        db = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(
            context.applicationContext.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
        ),
        auth = FirebaseAuth.getInstance()
    )

    private fun requireUserId(): String {
        return auth.currentUser?.uid
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("Terminal operator must sign in with Google before accessing Firestore.")
    }

    private fun employeesCollection(userId: String) =
        db.collection("users").document(userId).collection("employees")

    private fun attendanceLogsCollection(userId: String) =
        db.collection("users").document(userId).collection("attendanceLogs")

    fun observeEmployees(userId: String): Flow<List<Employee>> = flow {
        val col = employeesCollection(userId)
        emitAll(
            col.orderBy("employeeCode", Query.Direction.ASCENDING)
                .snapshots()
                .map { snapshot ->
                    snapshot.toObjects(
                        Employee::class.java,
                        DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                    )
                }
                .catch { error ->
                    val firestoreError = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }
                        .filterIsInstance<FirebaseFirestoreException>()
                        .firstOrNull()
                    if (firestoreError == null && error is CancellationException) throw error
                    val exceptionToLog = firestoreError ?: (error as? Exception)
                    if (exceptionToLog != null && auth.currentUser?.uid == userId) {
                        handleFirestoreError(exceptionToLog, OperationType.LIST, col.path)
                    }
                    throw error
                }
        )
    }

    fun observeAttendanceLogs(userId: String): Flow<List<AttendanceLog>> = flow {
        val col = attendanceLogsCollection(userId)
        emitAll(
            col.orderBy("createdAt", Query.Direction.DESCENDING)
                .limit(100)
                .snapshots()
                .map { snapshot ->
                    snapshot.toObjects(
                        AttendanceLog::class.java,
                        DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
                    )
                }
                .catch { error ->
                    val firestoreError = generateSequence(error) { it.cause }
                        .filterIsInstance<FirebaseFirestoreException>()
                        .firstOrNull()
                    if (firestoreError == null && error is CancellationException) throw error
                    val exceptionToLog = firestoreError ?: (error as? Exception)
                    if (exceptionToLog != null && auth.currentUser?.uid == userId) {
                        handleFirestoreError(exceptionToLog, OperationType.LIST, col.path)
                    }
                    throw error
                }
        )
    }

    suspend fun ensureStarterDirectoryIfEmpty(
        starterEmployees: List<Employee>
    ): Result<Boolean> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val col = employeesCollection(uid)
        return try {
            val existing = col.limit(1).get().await()
            if (!existing.isEmpty) {
                // Ensure Muhammad Ansar pre-registered profile is present for admin sync
                val ansar = starterEmployees.firstOrNull { it.id == "emp_muhammad_ansar" }
                if (ansar != null) {
                    val ansarSnap = col.document(ansar.id).get().await()
                    if (!ansarSnap.exists()) {
                        col.document(ansar.id).set(buildEmployeePayload(uid, ansar)).await()
                    }
                }
                return Result.success(false)
            }
            for (emp in starterEmployees) {
                col.document(emp.id).set(buildEmployeePayload(uid, emp)).await()
            }
            Result.success(true)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.CREATE, col.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun upsertEmployee(
        employee: Employee,
        overwriteIfExists: Boolean = false
    ): Result<Unit> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        require(employee.id.isNotBlank()) { "Employee ID must not be blank" }
        val docRef = employeesCollection(uid).document(employee.id)
        return try {
            val existing = docRef.get().await()
            if (!existing.exists()) {
                docRef.set(buildEmployeePayload(uid, employee)).await()
            } else if (overwriteIfExists) {
                val updates = mapOf(
                    "employeeCode" to employee.employeeCode.trim(),
                    "name" to employee.name.trim(),
                    "department" to employee.department.trim(),
                    "roleTitle" to employee.roleTitle.trim(),
                    "passcode" to employee.passcode.trim(),
                    "checkInCode" to employee.checkInCode.trim(),
                    "checkOutCode" to employee.checkOutCode.trim(),
                    "avatarKey" to employee.avatarKey,
                    "faceSignature" to employee.faceSignature.trim(),
                    "isActive" to employee.isActive,
                    "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                )
                docRef.update(updates).await()
            }
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, if (overwriteIfExists) OperationType.UPDATE else OperationType.CREATE, docRef.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    private fun buildEmployeePayload(uid: String, emp: Employee): Map<String, Any> = mapOf(
        "userId" to uid,
        "employeeCode" to emp.employeeCode.trim(),
        "name" to emp.name.trim(),
        "department" to emp.department.trim(),
        "roleTitle" to emp.roleTitle.trim(),
        "passcode" to emp.passcode.trim(),
        "checkInCode" to emp.checkInCode.trim(),
        "checkOutCode" to emp.checkOutCode.trim(),
        "avatarKey" to emp.avatarKey,
        "faceSignature" to emp.faceSignature.trim(),
        "isActive" to emp.isActive,
        "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
    )

    suspend fun createEmployee(
        employeeCode: String,
        name: String,
        department: String,
        roleTitle: String,
        passcode: String,
        avatarKey: String,
        faceSignature: String = "",
        checkInCode: String = "",
        checkOutCode: String = "",
        explicitDocId: String? = null
    ): Result<String> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docId = explicitDocId ?: "emp_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").take(16)}"
        val docRef = employeesCollection(uid).document(docId)
        val payload = mapOf(
            "userId" to uid,
            "employeeCode" to employeeCode.trim(),
            "name" to name.trim(),
            "department" to department.trim(),
            "roleTitle" to roleTitle.trim(),
            "passcode" to passcode.trim(),
            "checkInCode" to checkInCode.trim(),
            "checkOutCode" to checkOutCode.trim(),
            "avatarKey" to avatarKey,
            "faceSignature" to faceSignature.trim(),
            "isActive" to true,
            "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        return try {
            docRef.set(payload).await()
            Result.success(docId)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.CREATE, docRef.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun updateEmployee(employee: Employee): Result<Unit> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        require(employee.id.isNotBlank()) { "Employee ID must not be blank for update" }
        val docRef = employeesCollection(uid).document(employee.id)
        val updates = mapOf(
            "employeeCode" to employee.employeeCode.trim(),
            "name" to employee.name.trim(),
            "department" to employee.department.trim(),
            "roleTitle" to employee.roleTitle.trim(),
            "passcode" to employee.passcode.trim(),
            "checkInCode" to employee.checkInCode.trim(),
            "checkOutCode" to employee.checkOutCode.trim(),
            "avatarKey" to employee.avatarKey,
            "faceSignature" to employee.faceSignature.trim(),
            "isActive" to employee.isActive,
            "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        return try {
            docRef.update(updates).await()
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.UPDATE, docRef.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun deleteEmployee(employeeId: String): Result<Unit> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = employeesCollection(uid).document(employeeId)
        return try {
            docRef.delete().await()
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.DELETE, docRef.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun verifyEmployeeByPasscode(passcode: String): Result<Employee?> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val col = employeesCollection(uid)
        val normalized = passcode.trim()
        return try {
            val checkInMatch = col
                .whereEqualTo("checkInCode", normalized)
                .whereEqualTo("isActive", true)
                .limit(1)
                .get()
                .await()
                .documents
                .firstOrNull()
                ?.toObject(Employee::class.java, DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE)
            if (checkInMatch != null) return Result.success(checkInMatch)

            val checkOutMatch = col
                .whereEqualTo("checkOutCode", normalized)
                .whereEqualTo("isActive", true)
                .limit(1)
                .get()
                .await()
                .documents
                .firstOrNull()
                ?.toObject(Employee::class.java, DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE)
            if (checkOutMatch != null) return Result.success(checkOutMatch)

            val snapshot = col
                .whereEqualTo("passcode", normalized)
                .whereEqualTo("isActive", true)
                .limit(1)
                .get()
                .await()
            val match = snapshot.documents.firstOrNull()?.toObject(
                Employee::class.java,
                DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
            )
            Result.success(match)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.LIST, col.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun getEmployeeById(employeeId: String): Result<Employee?> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        val docRef = employeesCollection(uid).document(employeeId)
        return try {
            val snapshot = docRef.get().await()
            val emp = snapshot.toObject(
                Employee::class.java,
                DocumentSnapshot.ServerTimestampBehavior.ESTIMATE
            )
            Result.success(emp)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.GET, docRef.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun logAttendance(
        employee: Employee,
        eventType: AttendanceEventType,
        verificationMethod: VerificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Int,
        loggedTimeLabel: String,
        loggedDateKey: String,
        explicitLogId: String? = null,
        gpsCoordinates: String = "",
        locationAddress: String = "",
        timeSource: String = ""
    ): Result<String> {
        val uid = requireUserId()
        require(employee.id.isNotBlank()) { "Employee ID must not be blank" }
        val logId = explicitLogId ?: "log_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").take(20)}"
        val docRef = attendanceLogsCollection(uid).document(logId)
        val payload = mapOf(
            "userId" to uid,
            "employeeId" to employee.id,
            "employeeCode" to employee.employeeCode,
            "employeeName" to employee.name,
            "department" to employee.department,
            "avatarKey" to employee.avatarKey,
            "eventType" to eventType.wireValue,
            "verificationMethod" to verificationMethod.wireValue,
            "confidenceScore" to confidenceScore.coerceIn(0, 100),
            "loggedTimeLabel" to loggedTimeLabel,
            "loggedDateKey" to loggedDateKey,
            "gpsCoordinates" to gpsCoordinates,
            "locationAddress" to locationAddress,
            "timeSource" to timeSource,
            "createdAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
        )
        return try {
            docRef.set(payload).await()
            Result.success(logId)
        } catch (e: CancellationException) {
            throw e
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            handleFirestoreError(e, OperationType.CREATE, docRef.path)
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/time/NetworkTimeProvider.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.time

import android.os.SystemClock
import android.util.Log
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.net.DatagramPacket
import java.net.DatagramSocket
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.URL
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

data class NetworkTimeSnapshot(
    val epochMillis: Long,
    val sourceLabel: String,
    val clockDriftMs: Long,
    val tamperingDetected: Boolean,
    val isNetworkVerified: Boolean
)

interface NetworkTimeProvider {
    suspend fun syncNow(): NetworkTimeSnapshot
    fun currentSnapshot(): NetworkTimeSnapshot
    fun currentCalendar(): Calendar {
        val snap = currentSnapshot()
        return Calendar.getInstance(timeZone()).apply {
            timeInMillis = snap.epochMillis
        }
    }

    fun timeZone(): TimeZone = TimeZone.getDefault()
}

/**
 * Fallback deterministic provider using device clock only (used cleanly in unit tests or when
 * explicitly injected with a custom Calendar source).
 */
class DeviceCalendarTimeProvider(
    private val calendarSource: () -> Calendar = { Calendar.getInstance() }
) : NetworkTimeProvider {
    override suspend fun syncNow(): NetworkTimeSnapshot = currentSnapshot()

    override fun currentSnapshot(): NetworkTimeSnapshot {
        val cal = calendarSource()
        return NetworkTimeSnapshot(
            epochMillis = cal.timeInMillis,
            sourceLabel = "System Calendar Source",
            clockDriftMs = 0L,
            tamperingDetected = false,
            isNetworkVerified = false
        )
    }

    override fun currentCalendar(): Calendar = calendarSource()
}

/**
 * Tamper-resistant RFC 4330 SNTP UDP + HTTPS Date network time client anchored to monotonic
 * `SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()`.
 *
 * Once synced with an authoritative NTP server, subsequent attendance timestamps advance strictly
 * via monotonic uptime (`elapsedRealtime`), making manual phone Date & Time changes ineffective.
 */
class SntpNetworkTimeProvider(
    private val ntpHosts: List<String> = DEFAULT_NTP_HOSTS,
    private val ntpPort: Int = NTP_PORT,
    private val timeoutMs: Int = 2500,
    private val allowHttpFallback: Boolean = true,
    private val wallClockMs: () -> Long = { System.currentTimeMillis() },
    private val elapsedRealtimeMs: () -> Long = { SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() },
    private val timeZoneProvider: () -> TimeZone = { TimeZone.getDefault() }
) : NetworkTimeProvider {

    @Volatile
    private var syncedNetworkUtcMs: Long? = null

    @Volatile
    private var syncedAnchorElapsedMs: Long = 0L

    @Volatile
    private var syncedHostName: String = "Unverified Device Clock"

    @Volatile
    private var lastObservedDriftMs: Long = 0L

    override fun timeZone(): TimeZone = timeZoneProvider()

    override suspend fun syncNow(): NetworkTimeSnapshot = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val nowElapsed = elapsedRealtimeMs()
        val cachedUtc = syncedNetworkUtcMs
        if (cachedUtc != null && (nowElapsed - syncedAnchorElapsedMs) in 0L until RESYNC_TTL_MS) {
            return@withContext currentSnapshot()
        }

        for (host in ntpHosts) {
            val sntpUtc = runCatching { querySntpServer(host, ntpPort, timeoutMs) }.getOrNull()
            if (sntpUtc != null && sntpUtc > MIN_VALID_EPOCH_MS) {
                recordAuthoritativeSync(sntpUtc, "NTP Verified ($host)")
                return@withContext currentSnapshot()
            }
        }

        if (allowHttpFallback) {
            val httpUtc = runCatching { queryHttpDateFallback(timeoutMs) }.getOrNull()
            if (httpUtc != null && httpUtc > MIN_VALID_EPOCH_MS) {
                recordAuthoritativeSync(httpUtc, "HTTPS Network Time")
                return@withContext currentSnapshot()
            }
        }

        currentSnapshot()
    }

    private fun recordAuthoritativeSync(networkUtcMs: Long, sourceLabel: String) {
        val elapsedAnchor = elapsedRealtimeMs()
        val deviceNow = wallClockMs()
        syncedNetworkUtcMs = networkUtcMs
        syncedAnchorElapsedMs = elapsedAnchor
        syncedHostName = sourceLabel
        lastObservedDriftMs = networkUtcMs - deviceNow
        if (kotlin.math.abs(lastObservedDriftMs) > CLOCK_TAMPER_THRESHOLD_MS) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Device clock differs from $sourceLabel by ${lastObservedDriftMs}ms; enforcing network time.")
        }
    }

    override fun currentSnapshot(): NetworkTimeSnapshot {
        val anchorUtc = syncedNetworkUtcMs
        if (anchorUtc != null) {
            val elapsedDelta = (elapsedRealtimeMs() - syncedAnchorElapsedMs).coerceAtLeast(0L)
            val authoritativeNow = anchorUtc + elapsedDelta
            val currentDrift = authoritativeNow - wallClockMs()
            val tampered = kotlin.math.abs(currentDrift) > CLOCK_TAMPER_THRESHOLD_MS
            val label = if (tampered) {
                "$syncedHostName • Clock Tamper Blocked"
            } else {
                syncedHostName
            }
            return NetworkTimeSnapshot(
                epochMillis = authoritativeNow,
                sourceLabel = label,
                clockDriftMs = currentDrift,
                tamperingDetected = tampered,
                isNetworkVerified = true
            )
        }
        return NetworkTimeSnapshot(
            epochMillis = wallClockMs(),
            sourceLabel = "Pending NTP Sync (Device Clock)",
            clockDriftMs = 0L,
            tamperingDetected = false,
            isNetworkVerified = false
        )
    }

    internal fun querySntpServer(host: String, port: Int = NTP_PORT, socketTimeoutMs: Int = 2500): Long? {
        DatagramSocket().use { socket ->
            socket.soTimeout = socketTimeoutMs
            val address = InetAddress.getByName(host)
            val buffer = ByteArray(NTP_PACKET_SIZE)
            buffer[0] = NTP_MODE_CLIENT_VERSION_3

            val requestTime = wallClockMs()
            val requestTicks = elapsedRealtimeMs()
            writeTimeStamp(buffer, TRANSMIT_TIME_OFFSET, requestTime)

            val request = DatagramPacket(buffer, buffer.size, address, port)
            socket.send(request)

            val response = DatagramPacket(buffer, buffer.size)
            socket.receive(response)

            val responseTicks = elapsedRealtimeMs()
            val responseTime = requestTime + (responseTicks - requestTicks).coerceAtLeast(0L)

            val originateTime = readTimeStamp(buffer, ORIGINATE_TIME_OFFSET)
            val receiveTime = readTimeStamp(buffer, RECEIVE_TIME_OFFSET)
            val transmitTime = readTimeStamp(buffer, TRANSMIT_TIME_OFFSET)

            if (transmitTime <= 0L) return null

            val roundTripOffset = if (originateTime > 0L && receiveTime > 0L) {
                ((receiveTime - originateTime) + (transmitTime - responseTime)) / 2L
            } else {
                transmitTime - responseTime
            }
            return responseTime + roundTripOffset
        }
    }

    private fun queryHttpDateFallback(timeoutMs: Int): Long? {
        val url = URL("https://clients3.google.com/generate_204")
        val conn = (url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection).apply {
            requestMethod = "HEAD"
            connectTimeout = timeoutMs
            readTimeout = timeoutMs
            useCaches = false
        }
        return try {
            conn.connect()
            val headerDate = conn.date
            if (headerDate > MIN_VALID_EPOCH_MS) {
                headerDate
            } else {
                val rawDate = conn.getHeaderField("Date")
                if (!rawDate.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    SimpleDateFormat("EEE, dd MMM yyyy HH:mm:ss zzz", Locale.US)
                        .parse(rawDate)
                        ?.time
                } else {
                    null
                }
            }
        } finally {
            conn.disconnect()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "SntpNetworkTime"
        const val NTP_PORT = 123
        private const val NTP_PACKET_SIZE = 48
        private const val NTP_MODE_CLIENT_VERSION_3: Byte = 0x1B
        private const val ORIGINATE_TIME_OFFSET = 24
        private const val RECEIVE_TIME_OFFSET = 32
        private const val TRANSMIT_TIME_OFFSET = 40
        private const val OFFSET_1900_TO_1970_SECONDS = ((365L * 70L) + 17L) * 24L * 60L * 60L
        private const val MIN_VALID_EPOCH_MS = 1_700_000_000_000L // Late 2023 sanity floor
        private const val RESYNC_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000L
        const val CLOCK_TAMPER_THRESHOLD_MS = 120_000L // 2 minutes drift triggers tamper alert

        val DEFAULT_NTP_HOSTS = listOf(
            "time.google.com",
            "pool.ntp.org",
            "time.nist.gov"
        )

        internal fun read32Unsigned(buffer: ByteArray, offset: Int): Long {
            val b0 = buffer[offset].toLong() and 0xFFL
            val b1 = buffer[offset + 1].toLong() and 0xFFL
            val b2 = buffer[offset + 2].toLong() and 0xFFL
            val b3 = buffer[offset + 3].toLong() and 0xFFL
            return (b0 shl 24) or (b1 shl 16) or (b2 shl 8) or b3
        }

        internal fun readTimeStamp(buffer: ByteArray, offset: Int): Long {
            val seconds = read32Unsigned(buffer, offset)
            val fraction = read32Unsigned(buffer, offset + 4)
            if (seconds == 0L && fraction == 0L) return 0L
            return ((seconds - OFFSET_1900_TO_1970_SECONDS) * 1000L) +
                ((fraction * 1000L) / 0x1_0000_0000L)
        }

        internal fun writeTimeStamp(buffer: ByteArray, offset: Int, timeMs: Long) {
            if (timeMs <= 0L) {
                for (i in 0 until 8) buffer[offset + i] = 0
                return
            }
            var seconds = timeMs / 1000L
            val milliseconds = timeMs - seconds * 1000L
            seconds += OFFSET_1900_TO_1970_SECONDS

            var off = offset
            buffer[off++] = (seconds shr 24).toByte()
            buffer[off++] = (seconds shr 16).toByte()
            buffer[off++] = (seconds shr 8).toByte()
            buffer[off++] = seconds.toByte()

            val fraction = milliseconds * 0x1_0000_0000L / 1000L
            buffer[off++] = (fraction shr 24).toByte()
            buffer[off++] = (fraction shr 16).toByte()
            buffer[off++] = (fraction shr 8).toByte()
            buffer[off] = fraction.toByte()
        }
    }
}

fun avatarDrawableForKey(avatarKey: String): Int? = when (avatarKey) {
    "avatar_ansar" -> R.drawable.emp_muhammad_ansar
    "avatar_1" -> R.drawable.img_emp_avatar_1_1794571380629
    "avatar_2" -> R.drawable.img_emp_avatar_2_1794571380766
    "avatar_3" -> R.drawable.img_emp_avatar_3_1794571381051
    else -> null
}

@Composable
fun NichOfficialLogoBadge(
    size: Dp = 56.dp,
    cornerRadius: Dp = 14.dp,
    glowBrush: Brush = Brush.linearGradient(listOf(NeonCyan, NeonBlue, NeonGreen)),
    contentDescription: String? = "NICH Employee Attendance 3 official logo",
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(cornerRadius)
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .size(size)
            .background(
                brush = Brush.radialGradient(
                    listOf(
                        NeonCyan.copy(alpha = 0.28f),
                        NeonBlue.copy(alpha = 0.16f),
                        Color.Transparent
                    )
                ),
                shape = shape
            )
            .clip(shape)
            .border(width = 1.5.dp, brush = glowBrush, shape = shape)
            .background(ObsidianVoid),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Image(
            painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.nich_official_logo),
            contentDescription = contentDescription,
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(size)
                .clip(shape),
            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun EmployeeAvatar(
    name: String,
    avatarKey: String,
    size: Dp = 56.dp,
    ringColor: Color = NeonCyan
) {
    val drawableRes = avatarDrawableForKey(avatarKey)
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .size(size)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(ObsidianElevated)
            .border(2.dp, ringColor, CircleShape),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        if (drawableRes != null) {
            Image(
                painter = painterResource(id = drawableRes),
                contentDescription = "$name profile picture",
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(size)
                    .clip(CircleShape),
                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
            )
        } else {
            val initials = name.trim()
                .split(" ")
                .filter { it.isNotBlank() }
                .take(2)
                .joinToString("") { it.first().uppercase() }
                .ifEmpty { "NE" }
            Text(
                text = initials,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                color = ringColor,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun RealTimeStatusBanner(
    text: String,
    accentColor: Color,
    isPulsing: Boolean,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val animatedColor by animateColorAsState(targetValue = accentColor, label = "bannerColor")
    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "pulseTransition")
    val pulseAlpha by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.35f,
        targetValue = 1.0f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(durationMillis = 800),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "pulseAlpha"
    )
    val dotAlpha = if (isPulsing) pulseAlpha else 1f

    Surface(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .border(
                width = 1.dp,
                brush = Brush.horizontalGradient(
                    listOf(animatedColor.copy(alpha = 0.7f), animatedColor.copy(alpha = 0.2f))
                ),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
            ),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
        color = animatedColor.copy(alpha = 0.10f)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(10.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(animatedColor.copy(alpha = dotAlpha))
            )
            Text(
                text = text,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                color = animatedColor,
                maxLines = 2,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
            )
        }
    }
}

import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.util.concurrent.Executors
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.hypot
import kotlin.math.sin

private val ScannerElectricBlue = Color(0xFF29B6F6)
private val ScannerDeepBlue = Color(0xFF1E88E5)
private val ScannerNodeBlue = Color(0xFF80D8FF)

@OptIn(ExperimentalGetImage::class)
@Composable
fun BiometricCameraScanner(
    livenessSteps: List<LivenessDirection>,
    currentLivenessStepIndex: Int,
    onCameraInitialized: () -> Unit,
    onCameraInitFailed: (String) -> Unit,
    onFrameAnalyzed: (FaceAnalysisFrame) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val appContext = context.applicationContext
    val lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current

    val activeDirection = livenessSteps.getOrElse(currentLivenessStepIndex) {
        LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY
    }

    val latestCameraInit by rememberUpdatedState(onCameraInitialized)
    val latestCameraFail by rememberUpdatedState(onCameraInitFailed)
    val latestFrameAnalyzed by rememberUpdatedState(onFrameAnalyzed)

    val activeDirectionRef = remember { AtomicReference(activeDirection) }
    activeDirectionRef.set(activeDirection)
    val isFrontCameraRef = remember { AtomicBoolean(true) }
    val lastSelfieWriteMs = remember { AtomicLong(0L) }
    val lastSelfiePathRef = remember { AtomicReference<String?>(null) }

    var hasCameraPermission by remember {
        mutableStateOf(
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.CAMERA) ==
                PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
        )
    }

    val permissionLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()
    ) { granted ->
        hasCameraPermission = granted
    }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        if (!hasCameraPermission) {
            permissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.CAMERA)
        }
    }

    val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "standaloneTerminalScan")
    val sweepFraction by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.05f,
        targetValue = 0.95f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(durationMillis = 1900, easing = LinearEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "scanSweep"
    )
    val meshPulse by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
        initialValue = 0.42f,
        targetValue = 0.88f,
        animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
            animation = tween(durationMillis = 1400, easing = LinearEasing),
            repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
        ),
        label = "meshPulse"
    )

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(ObsidianVoid)
    ) {
        if (hasCameraPermission) {
            val previewView = remember(context) {
                PreviewView(context).apply {
                    implementationMode = PreviewView.ImplementationMode.COMPATIBLE
                    scaleType = PreviewView.ScaleType.FILL_CENTER
                    keepScreenOn = true
                }
            }
            val cameraExecutor = remember { Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor() }
            val faceDetector = remember {
                val options = FaceDetectorOptions.Builder()
                    .setPerformanceMode(FaceDetectorOptions.PERFORMANCE_MODE_ACCURATE)
                    .setLandmarkMode(FaceDetectorOptions.LANDMARK_MODE_ALL)
                    .setContourMode(FaceDetectorOptions.CONTOUR_MODE_ALL)
                    .setClassificationMode(FaceDetectorOptions.CLASSIFICATION_MODE_ALL)
                    .setMinFaceSize(0.18f)
                    .build()
                FaceDetection.getClient(options)
            }

            AndroidView(
                factory = { previewView },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
            )

            DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner, hasCameraPermission) {
                var isDisposed = false
                var boundProvider: ProcessCameraProvider? = null
                val cameraProviderFuture = ProcessCameraProvider.getInstance(context)

                cameraProviderFuture.addListener({
                    if (isDisposed) return@addListener
                    try {
                        val cameraProvider = cameraProviderFuture.get()
                        boundProvider = cameraProvider

                        val preview = Preview.Builder().build().also {
                            it.surfaceProvider = previewView.surfaceProvider
                        }

                        val imageAnalyzer = ImageAnalysis.Builder()
                            .setBackpressureStrategy(ImageAnalysis.STRATEGY_KEEP_ONLY_LATEST)
                            .build()
                            .also { analysis ->
                                analysis.setAnalyzer(cameraExecutor) { imageProxy ->
                                    if (isDisposed) {
                                        imageProxy.close()
                                        return@setAnalyzer
                                    }
                                    val mediaImage = imageProxy.image
                                    if (mediaImage != null) {
                                        val stepSnapshot = activeDirectionRef.get()
                                        val frontCam = isFrontCameraRef.get()
                                        val rotationDeg = imageProxy.imageInfo.rotationDegrees

                                        if (stepSnapshot == LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY || lastSelfiePathRef.get() == null) {
                                            val nowMs = System.currentTimeMillis()
                                            if (lastSelfiePathRef.get() == null || nowMs - lastSelfieWriteMs.get() >= 80L) {
                                                val capturedPath = captureRotatedSelfieFile(
                                                    context = appContext,
                                                    imageProxy = imageProxy,
                                                    isFrontCamera = frontCam,
                                                    rotationDegrees = rotationDeg,
                                                    maxKeepCount = 8
                                                )
                                                if (capturedPath != null) {
                                                    lastSelfiePathRef.set(capturedPath)
                                                    lastSelfieWriteMs.set(nowMs)
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }

                                        val inputImage = InputImage.fromMediaImage(mediaImage, rotationDeg)
                                        val selfieForFrame = lastSelfiePathRef.get()

                                        faceDetector.process(inputImage)
                                            .addOnSuccessListener { faces ->
                                                if (!isDisposed) {
                                                    val primaryFace = faces.maxByOrNull {
                                                        it.boundingBox.width() * it.boundingBox.height()
                                                    }
                                                    if (primaryFace != null) {
                                                        latestFrameAnalyzed(
                                                            analyzeFaceBiometrics(
                                                                face = primaryFace,
                                                                targetDirection = activeDirectionRef.get(),
                                                                isFrontCamera = frontCam,
                                                                capturedSelfiePath = selfieForFrame,
                                                                frameWidth = inputImage.width,
                                                                frameHeight = inputImage.height
                                                            )
                                                        )
                                                    } else {
                                                        latestFrameAnalyzed(
                                                            FaceAnalysisFrame(
                                                                faceDetected = false,
                                                                isFrontCamera = frontCam
                                                            )
                                                        )
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                            .addOnFailureListener {
                                                if (!isDisposed) {
                                                    latestFrameAnalyzed(
                                                        FaceAnalysisFrame(
                                                            faceDetected = false,
                                                            isFrontCamera = frontCam
                                                        )
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }
                                            .addOnCompleteListener {
                                                imageProxy.close()
                                            }
                                    } else {
                                        imageProxy.close()
                                    }
                                }
                            }

                        val hasFront = cameraProvider.hasCamera(CameraSelector.DEFAULT_FRONT_CAMERA)
                        val hasBack = cameraProvider.hasCamera(CameraSelector.DEFAULT_BACK_CAMERA)
                        val selector = when {
                            hasFront -> {
                                isFrontCameraRef.set(true)
                                CameraSelector.DEFAULT_FRONT_CAMERA
                            }
                            hasBack -> {
                                isFrontCameraRef.set(false)
                                CameraSelector.DEFAULT_BACK_CAMERA
                            }
                            else -> null
                        }

                        if (selector == null) {
                            latestCameraFail("No physical camera sensor detected.")
                            return@addListener
                        }

                        cameraProvider.unbindAll()
                        cameraProvider.bindToLifecycle(
                            lifecycleOwner,
                            selector,
                            preview,
                            imageAnalyzer
                        )
                        if (!isDisposed) {
                            latestCameraInit()
                        }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        Log.w("BiometricCamera", "CameraX initialization error: ${e.message}", e)
                        if (!isDisposed) {
                            latestCameraFail(
                                e.localizedMessage ?: "CameraX hardware initialization failed."
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }, ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(context))

                onDispose {
                    isDisposed = true
                    runCatching { boundProvider?.unbindAll() }
                    runCatching { faceDetector.close() }
                    runCatching { cameraExecutor.shutdown() }
                }
            }
        } else {
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(ObsidianVoid)
                    .padding(28.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Rounded.CameraAlt,
                    contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.camera_permission_required),
                    tint = ScannerElectricBlue,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(48.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.camera_permission_required),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                    color = TextSecondary,
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                Button(
                    onClick = { permissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.CAMERA) },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                        containerColor = ScannerElectricBlue,
                        contentColor = ObsidianVoid
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier.minimumInteractiveComponentSize()
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.grant_camera_permission),
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Central Precision Blue Boundary-Box + Continuous 3D Blue Geometric Scanning Overlay
        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            drawStandaloneTerminalScannerOverlay(
                sweepFraction = sweepFraction,
                meshPulse = meshPulse
            )
        }

        // Only visible branding element: NICH Employee Attendance 3 app icon cleanly in bottom corner
        NichOfficialLogoBadge(
            size = 54.dp,
            cornerRadius = 14.dp,
            glowBrush = Brush.linearGradient(
                listOf(NeonCyan, ScannerElectricBlue, NeonBlue)
            ),
            contentDescription = stringResource(R.string.app_name),
            modifier = Modifier
                .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                .navigationBarsPadding()
                .padding(end = 20.dp, bottom = 20.dp)
        )
    }
}

private fun DrawScope.drawStandaloneTerminalScannerOverlay(
    sweepFraction: Float,
    meshPulse: Float
) {
    val w = size.width
    val h = size.height
    val boxW = minOf(w * 0.72f, h * 0.44f)
    val boxH = minOf(boxW * 1.24f, h * 0.62f)
    val left = (w - boxW) / 2f
    val top = (h - boxH) / 2f
    val right = left + boxW
    val bottom = top + boxH
    val cx = w / 2f
    val cy = h / 2f

    // 1. Subtle holographic radial aura framing the central scanner zone
    drawCircle(
        brush = Brush.radialGradient(
            colors = listOf(
                ScannerElectricBlue.copy(alpha = 0.08f * meshPulse),
                Color.Transparent
            ),
            center = Offset(cx, cy),
            radius = maxOf(boxW, boxH) * 0.75f
        ),
        radius = maxOf(boxW, boxH) * 0.75f,
        center = Offset(cx, cy)
    )

    // 2. Continuous Blue Geometric 3D Facial Contour Mesh Overlay
    val rx = boxW * 0.36f
    val ry = boxH * 0.39f
    val meshColorBright = NeonCyan.copy(alpha = 0.56f * meshPulse)
    val meshColorMid = ScannerElectricBlue.copy(alpha = 0.42f * meshPulse)
    val meshColorSoft = ScannerDeepBlue.copy(alpha = 0.28f * meshPulse)
    val strokeThin = 1.1.dp.toPx()
    val strokeMed = 1.5.dp.toPx()

    // Oval boundary rings (3D concentric depth contour)
    drawOval(
        color = meshColorMid,
        topLeft = Offset(cx - rx, cy - ry),
        size = Size(rx * 2f, ry * 2f),
        style = Stroke(width = strokeMed)
    )
    drawOval(
        color = meshColorSoft,
        topLeft = Offset(cx - rx * 0.74f, cy - ry * 0.74f),
        size = Size(rx * 1.48f, ry * 1.48f),
        style = Stroke(width = strokeThin)
    )

    // Longitude 3D facial meridian arcs
    val meridianFactors = listOf(-0.68f, -0.36f, 0f, 0.36f, 0.68f)
    meridianFactors.forEach { factor ->
        val path = Path().apply {
            val steps = 28
            for (i in 0..steps) {
                val t = -1f + (2f * i / steps.toFloat())
                val angle = t * (Math.PI / 2.0)
                val xCurve = cos(angle).toFloat()
                val yOffset = sin(angle).toFloat() * ry
                val px = cx + (factor * rx * xCurve)
                val py = cy + yOffset
                if (i == 0) moveTo(px, py) else lineTo(px, py)
            }
        }
        drawPath(
            path = path,
            color = if (factor == 0f) meshColorBright else meshColorSoft,
            style = Stroke(width = if (factor == 0f) strokeMed else strokeThin)
        )
    }

    // Latitude contour cross-ribs (forehead, brow, eye, cheekbone, lip, jawline)
    val latitudeNormalizedY = listOf(-0.66f, -0.36f, -0.10f, 0.18f, 0.44f, 0.70f)
    latitudeNormalizedY.forEach { ny ->
        val halfSpanX = rx * cos(ny * (Math.PI / 2.0)).toFloat().coerceAtLeast(0.18f)
        val archLift = ry * 0.08f * ny
        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(cx - halfSpanX, cy + ny * ry)
            quadraticTo(cx, cy + ny * ry + archLift, cx + halfSpanX, cy + ny * ry)
        }
        drawPath(
            path = path,
            color = meshColorMid,
            style = Stroke(width = strokeThin)
        )
    }

    // Triangulated geometric wireframe points (brow, eyes, nose bridge, mouth, jaw)
    val pTop = Offset(cx, cy - ry * 0.88f)
    val pBrowL = Offset(cx - rx * 0.56f, cy - ry * 0.38f)
    val pBrowC = Offset(cx, cy - ry * 0.30f)
    val pBrowR = Offset(cx + rx * 0.56f, cy - ry * 0.38f)
    val pEyeL = Offset(cx - rx * 0.42f, cy - ry * 0.14f)
    val pEyeR = Offset(cx + rx * 0.42f, cy - ry * 0.14f)
    val pNoseTip = Offset(cx, cy + ry * 0.16f)
    val pCheekL = Offset(cx - rx * 0.72f, cy + ry * 0.12f)
    val pCheekR = Offset(cx + rx * 0.72f, cy + ry * 0.12f)
    val pMouthL = Offset(cx - rx * 0.34f, cy + ry * 0.45f)
    val pMouthC = Offset(cx, cy + ry * 0.48f)
    val pMouthR = Offset(cx + rx * 0.34f, cy + ry * 0.45f)
    val pChin = Offset(cx, cy + ry * 0.90f)

    val wireframePath = Path().apply {
        moveTo(pTop.x, pTop.y)
        lineTo(pBrowL.x, pBrowL.y)
        lineTo(pEyeL.x, pEyeL.y)
        lineTo(pBrowC.x, pBrowC.y)
        lineTo(pEyeR.x, pEyeR.y)
        lineTo(pBrowR.x, pBrowR.y)
        lineTo(pTop.x, pTop.y)

        moveTo(pBrowL.x, pBrowL.y)
        lineTo(pCheekL.x, pCheekL.y)
        lineTo(pEyeL.x, pEyeL.y)
        lineTo(pNoseTip.x, pNoseTip.y)
        lineTo(pEyeR.x, pEyeR.y)
        lineTo(pCheekR.x, pCheekR.y)
        lineTo(pBrowR.x, pBrowR.y)

        moveTo(pCheekL.x, pCheekL.y)
        lineTo(pMouthL.x, pMouthL.y)
        lineTo(pNoseTip.x, pNoseTip.y)
        lineTo(pMouthR.x, pMouthR.y)
        lineTo(pCheekR.x, pCheekR.y)

        moveTo(pMouthL.x, pMouthL.y)
        lineTo(pMouthC.x, pMouthC.y)
        lineTo(pMouthR.x, pMouthR.y)
        lineTo(pChin.x, pChin.y)
        lineTo(pMouthL.x, pMouthL.y)
    }
    drawPath(
        path = wireframePath,
        color = meshColorBright,
        style = Stroke(width = strokeThin, cap = StrokeCap.Round, join = StrokeJoin.Round)
    )

    val nodes = listOf(
        pTop, pBrowL, pBrowC, pBrowR,
        pEyeL, pEyeR, pNoseTip,
        pCheekL, pCheekR, pMouthL, pMouthC, pMouthR, pChin
    )
    nodes.forEach { node ->
        drawCircle(
            color = ScannerNodeBlue.copy(alpha = 0.85f * meshPulse),
            radius = 2.6.dp.toPx(),
            center = node
        )
    }

    // 3. Continuous volumetric blue scanning sweep plane inside the boundary box
    val beamY = top + (boxH * sweepFraction)
    drawRect(
        brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
            colors = listOf(
                Color.Transparent,
                ScannerElectricBlue.copy(alpha = 0.24f),
                Color.Transparent
            ),
            startY = beamY - 26.dp.toPx(),
            endY = beamY + 26.dp.toPx()
        ),
        topLeft = Offset(left + 6.dp.toPx(), (beamY - 26.dp.toPx()).coerceAtLeast(top)),
        size = Size(boxW - 12.dp.toPx(), 52.dp.toPx())
    )
    drawLine(
        brush = Brush.horizontalGradient(
            listOf(Color.Transparent, NeonCyan, ScannerElectricBlue, NeonCyan, Color.Transparent)
        ),
        start = Offset(left + 4.dp.toPx(), beamY),
        end = Offset(right - 4.dp.toPx(), beamY),
        strokeWidth = 2.2.dp.toPx(),
        cap = StrokeCap.Round
    )

    // 4. Central Precision Blue Corner Boundary-Box (L-Brackets like physical EZFace terminal)
    val cornerLen = 36.dp.toPx()
    val glowStrokeW = 7.dp.toPx()
    val sharpStrokeW = 3.2.dp.toPx()

    val corners = listOf(
        Offset(left, top + cornerLen) to Offset(left, top) to Offset(left + cornerLen, top),
        Offset(right - cornerLen, top) to Offset(right, top) to Offset(right, top + cornerLen),
        Offset(left, bottom - cornerLen) to Offset(left, bottom) to Offset(left + cornerLen, bottom),
        Offset(right - cornerLen, bottom) to Offset(right, bottom) to Offset(right, bottom - cornerLen)
    )
    corners.forEach { (edgePair, endPt) ->
        val (startPt, cornerPt) = edgePair
        val bracketPath = Path().apply {
            moveTo(startPt.x, startPt.y)
            lineTo(cornerPt.x, cornerPt.y)
            lineTo(endPt.x, endPt.y)
        }
        drawPath(
            path = bracketPath,
            color = ScannerElectricBlue.copy(alpha = 0.30f),
            style = Stroke(width = glowStrokeW, cap = StrokeCap.Square, join = StrokeJoin.Miter)
        )
        drawPath(
            path = bracketPath,
            color = NeonCyan,
            style = Stroke(width = sharpStrokeW, cap = StrokeCap.Square, join = StrokeJoin.Miter)
        )
    }
}

internal fun captureRotatedSelfieFile(
    context: Context,
    imageProxy: ImageProxy,
    isFrontCamera: Boolean,
    rotationDegrees: Int,
    targetDirName: String = "live_selfies",
    filePrefix: String = "selfie",
    maxKeepCount: Int = 4
): String? {
    var rawBitmap: Bitmap? = null
    var transformedBitmap: Bitmap? = null
    var scaledBitmap: Bitmap? = null
    return try {
        rawBitmap = imageProxy.toBitmap()
        val matrix = Matrix().apply {
            if (rotationDegrees != 0) {
                postRotate(rotationDegrees.toFloat())
            }
            if (isFrontCamera) {
                postScale(-1f, 1f)
            }
        }
        transformedBitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(
            rawBitmap,
            0,
            0,
            rawBitmap.width,
            rawBitmap.height,
            matrix,
            true
        )

        val maxDim = 480
        val finalBitmap = if (transformedBitmap.width > maxDim) {
            val aspectHeight = ((transformedBitmap.height.toFloat() / transformedBitmap.width.toFloat()) * maxDim)
                .toInt()
                .coerceAtLeast(1)
            scaledBitmap = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(transformedBitmap, maxDim, aspectHeight, true)
            scaledBitmap
        } else {
            transformedBitmap
        }

        val dir = File(context.cacheDir, targetDirName).apply {
            if (!exists()) mkdirs()
        }
        if (maxKeepCount > 0) {
            dir.listFiles()?.sortedByDescending { it.lastModified() }?.drop(maxKeepCount)?.forEach { old ->
                runCatching { old.delete() }
            }
        }
        val outFile = File(dir, "${filePrefix}_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.jpg")
        FileOutputStream(outFile).use { stream ->
            finalBitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 90, stream)
        }
        outFile.absolutePath
    } catch (e: Throwable) {
        Log.w("BiometricCamera", "Live selfie snapshot skipped: ${e.message}")
        null
    } finally {
        if (scaledBitmap != null && scaledBitmap !== transformedBitmap) {
            runCatching { scaledBitmap.recycle() }
        }
        if (transformedBitmap != null && transformedBitmap !== rawBitmap) {
            runCatching { transformedBitmap.recycle() }
        }
        if (rawBitmap != null) {
            runCatching { rawBitmap.recycle() }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Extracts a 16-dimensional scale- and lighting-invariant facial geometry & contour vector
 * covering inter-ocular spacing, eye aspect ratios, eyebrow arch lift, nose bridge & base width,
 * philtrum, mouth width & lip thickness, cheekbone span, gonial jaw taper, chin drop, and symmetry.
 */
internal fun analyzeFaceBiometrics(
    face: Face,
    targetDirection: LivenessDirection = LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY,
    isFrontCamera: Boolean = true,
    capturedSelfiePath: String? = null,
    frameWidth: Int = 0,
    frameHeight: Int = 0
): FaceAnalysisFrame {
    val box: Rect = face.boundingBox
    val boxW = box.width().toFloat().coerceAtLeast(1f)
    val boxH = box.height().toFloat().coerceAtLeast(1f)
    val centerXNorm = if (frameWidth > 0) (box.exactCenterX() / frameWidth.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f) else null
    val centerYNorm = if (frameHeight > 0) (box.exactCenterY() / frameHeight.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f) else null

    val leftEye = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.LEFT_EYE)?.position
    val rightEye = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.RIGHT_EYE)?.position
    val nose = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.NOSE_BASE)?.position
    val mouthLeft = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.MOUTH_LEFT)?.position
    val mouthRight = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.MOUTH_RIGHT)?.position
    val mouthBottom = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.MOUTH_BOTTOM)?.position
    val leftCheek = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.LEFT_CHEEK)?.position
    val rightCheek = face.getLandmark(FaceLandmark.RIGHT_CHEEK)?.position

    val faceContour = face.getContour(FaceContour.FACE)?.points.orEmpty()
    val leftEyeContour = face.getContour(FaceContour.LEFT_EYE)?.points.orEmpty()
    val rightEyeContour = face.getContour(FaceContour.RIGHT_EYE)?.points.orEmpty()
    val leftBrowContour = face.getContour(FaceContour.LEFT_EYEBROW_TOP)?.points.orEmpty()
    val rightBrowContour = face.getContour(FaceContour.RIGHT_EYEBROW_TOP)?.points.orEmpty()
    val noseBottomContour = face.getContour(FaceContour.NOSE_BOTTOM)?.points.orEmpty()
    val upperLipTop = face.getContour(FaceContour.UPPER_LIP_TOP)?.points.orEmpty()
    val upperLipBottom = face.getContour(FaceContour.UPPER_LIP_BOTTOM)?.points.orEmpty()

    val eyesLocked = leftEye != null && rightEye != null
    val noseMustacheLocked = nose != null && mouthLeft != null && mouthRight != null
    val jawBeardLocked = faceContour.size >= 19 || (mouthBottom != null && (leftCheek != null || rightCheek != null))

    val yaw = face.headEulerAngleY
    val pitch = face.headEulerAngleX
    val roll = face.headEulerAngleZ

    val leftOpenProb = face.leftEyeOpenProbability
    val rightOpenProb = face.rightEyeOpenProbability
    val smilingProb = face.smilingProbability
    val livenessOpenEyes = (leftOpenProb == null || leftOpenProb >= 0.25f) &&
        (rightOpenProb == null || rightOpenProb >= 0.25f)

    if (targetDirection != LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY) {
        val directionMatched = LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
            direction = targetDirection,
            headYawDeg = yaw,
            headPitchDeg = pitch,
            isFrontCamera = isFrontCamera
        )
        return FaceAnalysisFrame(
            faceDetected = true,
            eyesLocked = eyesLocked,
            noseMustacheLocked = noseMustacheLocked,
            jawBeardLocked = jawBeardLocked,
            signature = null,
            livenessOpenEyes = livenessOpenEyes,
            headPoseAligned = directionMatched,
            headYawDeg = yaw,
            headPitchDeg = pitch,
            headRollDeg = roll,
            leftEyeOpenProbability = leftOpenProb,
            rightEyeOpenProbability = rightOpenProb,
            smilingProbability = smilingProb,
            faceCenterXNorm = centerXNorm,
            faceCenterYNorm = centerYNorm,
            isFrontCamera = isFrontCamera,
            selfieImagePath = null,
            qualityIssue = if (directionMatched) null else targetDirection.instruction
        )
    }

    val headPoseAligned = abs(yaw) <= 25f && abs(pitch) <= 25f && abs(roll) <= 25f

    if (!eyesLocked || nose == null) {
        return FaceAnalysisFrame(
            faceDetected = true,
            eyesLocked = eyesLocked,
            noseMustacheLocked = noseMustacheLocked,
            jawBeardLocked = jawBeardLocked,
            signature = null,
            livenessOpenEyes = livenessOpenEyes,
            headPoseAligned = headPoseAligned,
            headYawDeg = yaw,
            headPitchDeg = pitch,
            headRollDeg = roll,
            leftEyeOpenProbability = leftOpenProb,
            rightEyeOpenProbability = rightOpenProb,
            smilingProbability = smilingProb,
            faceCenterXNorm = centerXNorm,
            faceCenterYNorm = centerYNorm,
            isFrontCamera = isFrontCamera,
            selfieImagePath = capturedSelfiePath,
            qualityIssue = "Look straight into the camera so both eyes and face are clearly visible"
        )
    }

    val lEye = leftEye!!
    val rEye = rightEye!!
    val eyeDist = hypot(rEye.x - lEye.x, rEye.y - lEye.y).coerceAtLeast(1f)
    val eyeMidX = (lEye.x + rEye.x) / 2f
    val eyeMidY = (lEye.y + rEye.y) / 2f

    val mLeftX = mouthLeft?.x ?: (box.left + boxW * 0.34f)
    val mLeftY = mouthLeft?.y ?: (box.top + boxH * 0.74f)
    val mRightX = mouthRight?.x ?: (box.left + boxW * 0.66f)
    val mRightY = mouthRight?.y ?: (box.top + boxH * 0.74f)

    val mouthDist = hypot(mRightX - mLeftX, mRightY - mLeftY)
    val noseDrop = hypot(nose.x - eyeMidX, nose.y - eyeMidY)
    val mouthCenterX = (mLeftX + mRightX) / 2f
    val mouthCenterY = (mLeftY + mRightY) / 2f

    val upperLipMid = if (upperLipTop.isNotEmpty()) upperLipTop[upperLipTop.size / 2] else null
    val lowerUpperLipMid = if (upperLipBottom.isNotEmpty()) upperLipBottom[upperLipBottom.size / 2] else null
    val philtrumDist = if (upperLipMid != null) {
        hypot(upperLipMid.x - nose.x, upperLipMid.y - nose.y)
    } else {
        hypot(mouthCenterX - nose.x, mouthCenterY - nose.y) * 0.65f
    }

    val chinPoint = if (faceContour.size >= 19) faceContour.maxByOrNull { it.y } else null
    val jawChinDrop = when {
        chinPoint != null -> abs(chinPoint.y - mouthCenterY)
        mouthBottom != null -> abs(box.bottom.toFloat() - mouthBottom.y)
        else -> abs(box.bottom.toFloat() - mouthCenterY)
    }

    val cheekSpan = when {
        faceContour.size >= 30 -> hypot(
            faceContour[6].x - faceContour[30].x,
            faceContour[6].y - faceContour[30].y
        ).coerceAtLeast(1f)
        leftCheek != null && rightCheek != null -> hypot(
            rightCheek.x - leftCheek.x,
            rightCheek.y - leftCheek.y
        ).coerceAtLeast(1f)
        else -> boxW * 0.84f
    }

    val jawWidthToCheek = if (faceContour.size >= 30) {
        val lowerJawSpan = hypot(
            faceContour[12].x - faceContour[24].x,
            faceContour[12].y - faceContour[24].y
        )
        (lowerJawSpan / cheekSpan).coerceIn(0.35f, 1.15f)
    } else {
        0.72f
    }

    val noseToLeftEye = hypot(nose.x - lEye.x, nose.y - lEye.y).coerceAtLeast(1f)
    val noseToRightEye = hypot(nose.x - rEye.x, nose.y - rEye.y).coerceAtLeast(1f)
    val symmetryRatio = (minOf(noseToLeftEye, noseToRightEye) / maxOf(noseToLeftEye, noseToRightEye))
        .coerceIn(0.5f, 1.0f)

    // Extra 8 high-discrimination facial structure & contour features (total = 16 dims)
    val contourMinX = faceContour.minOfOrNull { it.x } ?: box.left.toFloat()
    val contourMaxX = faceContour.maxOfOrNull { it.x } ?: box.right.toFloat()
    val contourMinY = faceContour.minOfOrNull { it.y } ?: box.top.toFloat()
    val contourMaxY = faceContour.maxOfOrNull { it.y } ?: box.bottom.toFloat()
    val contourAspect = ((contourMaxX - contourMinX).coerceAtLeast(1f) /
        (contourMaxY - contourMinY).coerceAtLeast(1f)).coerceIn(0.45f, 1.35f)

    val leftEyeAspect = computeEyeContourAspect(leftEyeContour)
    val rightEyeAspect = computeEyeContourAspect(rightEyeContour)

    val noseBaseSpan = if (noseBottomContour.size >= 3) {
        hypot(
            noseBottomContour.last().x - noseBottomContour.first().x,
            noseBottomContour.last().y - noseBottomContour.first().y
        )
    } else {
        eyeDist * 0.56f
    }

    val chinTaperSpan = if (faceContour.size >= 22) {
        hypot(faceContour[15].x - faceContour[21].x, faceContour[15].y - faceContour[21].y)
    } else {
        cheekSpan * 0.45f
    }

    val browLift = if (leftBrowContour.isNotEmpty() && rightBrowContour.isNotEmpty()) {
        val leftBrowMidY = leftBrowContour[leftBrowContour.size / 2].y
        val rightBrowMidY = rightBrowContour[rightBrowContour.size / 2].y
        ((lEye.y - leftBrowMidY) + (rEye.y - rightBrowMidY)) / 2f
    } else {
        eyeDist * 0.28f
    }

    val upperLipThickness = if (upperLipMid != null && lowerUpperLipMid != null) {
        hypot(lowerUpperLipMid.x - upperLipMid.x, lowerUpperLipMid.y - upperLipMid.y)
    } else {
        mouthDist * 0.14f
    }

    val features16 = listOf(
        (eyeDist / boxW).coerceIn(0.10f, 0.90f),
        (noseDrop / eyeDist).coerceIn(0.10f, 1.50f),
        (mouthDist / eyeDist).coerceIn(0.20f, 1.60f),
        (boxW / boxH).coerceIn(0.40f, 1.50f),
        (philtrumDist / eyeDist).coerceIn(0.05f, 0.90f),
        (jawChinDrop / eyeDist).coerceIn(0.20f, 1.60f),
        jawWidthToCheek,
        symmetryRatio,
        contourAspect,
        leftEyeAspect,
        rightEyeAspect,
        (noseBaseSpan / noseDrop.coerceAtLeast(1f)).coerceIn(0.20f, 1.40f),
        (cheekSpan / eyeDist).coerceIn(1.20f, 3.00f),
        (chinTaperSpan / cheekSpan).coerceIn(0.20f, 0.95f),
        (browLift / eyeDist).coerceIn(0.05f, 0.80f),
        (upperLipThickness / mouthDist.coerceAtLeast(1f)).coerceIn(0.03f, 0.45f)
    )

    val signature = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(features16)

    return FaceAnalysisFrame(
        faceDetected = true,
        eyesLocked = eyesLocked,
        noseMustacheLocked = noseMustacheLocked,
        jawBeardLocked = jawBeardLocked,
        signature = signature,
        livenessOpenEyes = livenessOpenEyes,
        headPoseAligned = headPoseAligned,
        headYawDeg = yaw,
        headPitchDeg = pitch,
        headRollDeg = roll,
        leftEyeOpenProbability = leftOpenProb,
        rightEyeOpenProbability = rightOpenProb,
        smilingProbability = smilingProb,
        faceCenterXNorm = centerXNorm,
        faceCenterYNorm = centerYNorm,
        isFrontCamera = isFrontCamera,
        selfieImagePath = capturedSelfiePath,
        qualityIssue = if (headPoseAligned) null else "Face camera directly (keep head level)"
    )
}

private fun computeEyeContourAspect(points: List<android.graphics.PointF>): Float {
    if (points.size < 6) return 0.34f
    val minX = points.minOf { it.x }
    val maxX = points.maxOf { it.x }
    val minY = points.minOf { it.y }
    val maxY = points.maxOf { it.y }
    val width = (maxX - minX).coerceAtLeast(1f)
    val height = (maxY - minY).coerceAtLeast(0.5f)
    return (height / width).coerceIn(0.10f, 0.90f)
}

internal fun extractFaceSignature(face: Face): String? = analyzeFaceBiometrics(face).signature

package com.example.ui.screens
