// FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/data/local/OfflineAttendanceStoreTest.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.local

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class OfflineAttendanceStoreTest {

    private lateinit var context: Context

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
        context.getSharedPreferences("nich_offline_attendance", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .clear()
            .commit()
    }

    @Test
    fun initialSeed_includesMuhammadAnsarWithDualCodes() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val ansar = store.employees.value.firstOrNull { it.id == OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID }

        assertNotNull(ansar)
        assertEquals("147", ansar?.checkInCode)
        assertEquals("369", ansar?.checkOutCode)
        assertEquals("avatar_ansar", ansar?.avatarKey)

        val checkInMatch = store.verifyCode("147", AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, checkInMatch?.eventType)
        assertEquals(OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID, checkInMatch?.employee?.id)

        val checkOutMatch = store.verifyCode("369", AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, checkOutMatch?.eventType)
        assertEquals(OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID, checkOutMatch?.employee?.id)
    }

    @Test
    fun offlineAttendanceAndEmployeeEdits_surviveRemoteMergeUntilSynced() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val ansar = store.employees.value.first { it.id == OfflineAttendanceStore.MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID }

        val localLog = store.recordLocalAttendance(
            employee = ansar,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.PASSCODE,
            confidenceScore = 100,
            loggedTimeLabel = "09:15:00 AM",
            loggedDateKey = "2026-11-13",
            synced = false
        )
        assertTrue(localLog.id in store.pendingSyncLogIds.value)

        val updatedSig = "0.4100:0.4600:0.6200:0.7400"
        store.updateEmployeeFaceSignature(ansar, updatedSig)
        assertTrue(ansar.id in store.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value)

        // Simulate stale cloud snapshot arriving before pending queue flushes
        val staleRemoteEmployee = ansar.copy(faceSignature = "0.1111:0.2222:0.3333:0.4444")
        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(listOf(staleRemoteEmployee))
        val afterMerge = store.employees.value.first { it.id == ansar.id }
        assertEquals(updatedSig, afterMerge.faceSignature)

        // Simulate empty/older cloud logs arriving while offline log is pending
        store.mergeRemoteLogs(emptyList())
        assertTrue(store.logs.value.any { it.id == localLog.id })

        // Verify persistence across store recreation
        val reloaded = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        assertTrue(localLog.id in reloaded.pendingSyncLogIds.value)
        assertTrue(ansar.id in reloaded.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value)

        reloaded.markLogSynced(localLog.id)
        reloaded.markEmployeeSynced(ansar.id)
        assertFalse(localLog.id in reloaded.pendingSyncLogIds.value)
        assertFalse(ansar.id in reloaded.pendingSyncEmployeeIds.value)
    }

    @Test
    fun offlineDelete_preventsRemoteResurrectionUntilDeleteSynced() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val target = Employee(
            id = "emp_temp_delete",
            employeeCode = "NICH-8888",
            name = "Temp Staff",
            department = "Security",
            roleTitle = "Officer",
            passcode = "8888",
            isActive = true
        )
        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(listOf(target))
        assertTrue(store.employees.value.any { it.id == target.id })

        store.deleteEmployee(target.id, queueCloudDelete = true)
        assertTrue(target.id in store.pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value)
        assertFalse(store.employees.value.any { it.id == target.id })

        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(listOf(target))
        assertFalse(store.employees.value.any { it.id == target.id })

        store.markEmployeeDeleteSynced(target.id)
        assertFalse(target.id in store.pendingDeleteEmployeeIds.value)
    }

    @Test
    fun registerEmployeesBatch_persistsMultipleNamesPhotosAndEmbeddingsLocally() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        assertFalse(store.hasRegisteredFaceProfiles())

        val tempPhoto1 = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "temp_reg_1.jpg")
        val tempPhoto2 = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "temp_reg_2.jpg")
        tempPhoto1.writeBytes(byteArrayOf(0xFF.toByte(), 0xD8.toByte(), 0xFF.toByte(), 0xD9.toByte()))
        tempPhoto2.writeBytes(byteArrayOf(0xFF.toByte(), 0xD8.toByte(), 0xFF.toByte(), 0xD9.toByte()))

        try {
            val saved = store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Ayesha Khan",
                        department = "Diagnostics",
                        photoPath = tempPhoto1.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = "0.4200:0.4800:0.6200:0.7400:0.2700:0.7500:0.7100:0.9600"
                    ),
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Zainab Tariq",
                        department = "Radiology",
                        photoPath = tempPhoto2.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = "0.3400:0.3800:0.5100:0.8600:0.1900:0.5900:0.8800:0.9100"
                    )
                )
            )
            assertEquals(2, saved.size)
            assertTrue(store.hasRegisteredFaceProfiles())

            val reloaded = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
            assertTrue(reloaded.hasRegisteredFaceProfiles())
            val ayesha = reloaded.employees.value.firstOrNull { it.name == "Ayesha Khan" }
            val zainab = reloaded.employees.value.firstOrNull { it.name == "Zainab Tariq" }
            assertNotNull(ayesha)
            assertNotNull(zainab)
            assertTrue(ayesha!!.localPhotoPath.isNotBlank())
            assertTrue(java.io.File(ayesha.localPhotoPath).exists())
            assertTrue(zainab!!.localPhotoPath.isNotBlank())
            assertTrue(java.io.File(zainab.localPhotoPath).exists())
        } finally {
            tempPhoto1.delete()
            tempPhoto2.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun pendingCheckOutLock_persistsLivePhotoGpsNtpAndPdfAcrossStoreRecreation_andClearsOnlyOnUnlock() {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        assertFalse(store.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
        assertNull(store.pendingCheckOutLock.value)

        store.savePendingCheckOutLock(
            PendingCheckOutLock(
                logId = "log_checkout_locked_1",
                employeeId = "emp_bilal",
                employeeCode = "NICH-4001",
                employeeName = "Bilal Ahmed",
                department = "Engineering",
                verificationMethod = "FACE_SCAN_1",
                confidenceScore = 95,
                loggedTimeLabel = "05:45:00 PM",
                loggedDateKey = "2026-11-17",
                gpsCoordinates = "24.860734, 67.001136 (±8m)",
                locationAddress = "NICH Campus, Karachi",
                timeSource = "NTP Verified (time.google.com)",
                liveSelfiePath = "/tmp/selfie_checkout.jpg",
                pdfReportPath = ""
            )
        )
        store.updatePendingCheckOutPdfPath("log_checkout_locked_1", "/tmp/report_checkout.pdf")

        // Simulate app process kill & cold relaunch with a fresh OfflineAttendanceStore instance
        val restartedStore = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        assertTrue(restartedStore.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
        val restored = restartedStore.pendingCheckOutLock.value
        assertNotNull(restored)
        assertEquals("log_checkout_locked_1", restored!!.logId)
        assertEquals("Bilal Ahmed", restored.employeeName)
        assertEquals("24.860734, 67.001136 (±8m)", restored.gpsCoordinates)
        assertEquals("NICH Campus, Karachi", restored.locationAddress)
        assertEquals("NTP Verified (time.google.com)", restored.timeSource)
        assertEquals("/tmp/selfie_checkout.jpg", restored.liveSelfiePath)
        assertEquals("/tmp/report_checkout.pdf", restored.pdfReportPath)
        assertTrue("log_checkout_locked_1" in restartedStore.pendingEmailLogIds.value)

        // Unlock only after confirmation clear
        restartedStore.clearPendingCheckOutLock("log_checkout_locked_1")
        val unlockedStore = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        assertFalse(unlockedStore.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
        assertNull(unlockedStore.pendingCheckOutLock.value)
        assertFalse("log_checkout_locked_1" in unlockedStore.pendingEmailLogIds.value)
    }
}

package com.example.data.model

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class AttendanceLogicTest {

    @Test
    fun determineEventType_morningHours_returnsCheckIn() {
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(8))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(9))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(11))
    }

    @Test
    fun determineEventType_noonAndAfternoonHours_returnsCheckOut() {
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(12))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(14))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(18))
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(23))
    }

    @Test
    fun faceBiometricMatcher_enforcesFiftyPercentThresholdAndSeparatesMultipleEmployees() {
        val employeeA = Employee(
            id = "emp_a",
            name = "Bilal Ahmed",
            faceSignature = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(
                listOf(
                    0.4200f, 0.4800f, 0.6200f, 0.7400f,
                    0.2700f, 0.7500f, 0.7100f, 0.9600f,
                    0.7800f, 0.3400f, 0.3400f, 0.5600f,
                    2.1000f, 0.4500f, 0.2900f, 0.1400f
                )
            ),
            isActive = true
        )
        val employeeB = Employee(
            id = "emp_b",
            name = "Dr. Ananya Rao",
            faceSignature = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(
                listOf(
                    0.3400f, 0.3800f, 0.5100f, 0.8600f,
                    0.1900f, 0.5900f, 0.8800f, 0.9100f,
                    0.8900f, 0.4400f, 0.4500f, 0.7200f,
                    2.5500f, 0.6200f, 0.3900f, 0.2100f
                )
            ),
            isActive = true
        )
        val roster = listOf(employeeA, employeeB)

        val scanForA = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(
            listOf(
                0.4220f, 0.4820f, 0.6180f, 0.7420f,
                0.2710f, 0.7490f, 0.7120f, 0.9580f,
                0.7820f, 0.3420f, 0.3390f, 0.5620f,
                2.1040f, 0.4520f, 0.2910f, 0.1410f
            )
        )
        val matchA = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(scanForA, roster)
        assertNotNull(matchA)
        assertEquals("emp_a", matchA?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals("Bilal Ahmed", matchA?.employee?.name)
        assertEquals(true, (matchA?.confidencePercent ?: 0) >= 50)

        // Lighting/angle-shifted scan (~61%) must still pass cleanly at >= 50%
        val partialScanForA = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(
            listOf(
                0.5800f, 0.6400f, 0.7800f, 0.9000f,
                0.4300f, 0.9100f, 0.8700f, 1.1200f,
                0.9400f, 0.5000f, 0.5000f, 0.7200f,
                1.8500f, 0.6100f, 0.4500f, 0.3000f
            )
        )
        val partialMatchA = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(partialScanForA, roster)
        assertNotNull(partialMatchA)
        assertEquals("emp_a", partialMatchA?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(true, (partialMatchA?.confidencePercent ?: 0) in 50..89)

        val scanForB = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(
            listOf(
                0.3410f, 0.3820f, 0.5080f, 0.8580f,
                0.1910f, 0.5920f, 0.8780f, 0.9120f,
                0.8880f, 0.4410f, 0.4480f, 0.7190f,
                2.5480f, 0.6180f, 0.3910f, 0.2090f
            )
        )
        val matchB = FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(scanForB, roster)
        assertNotNull(matchB)
        assertEquals("emp_b", matchB?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals("Dr. Ananya Rao", matchB?.employee?.name)
        assertEquals(true, (matchB?.confidencePercent ?: 0) >= 50)

        // Unrecognized stranger (< 50%) must still be rejected cleanly (no single-name fallback)
        val strangerScan = FaceBiometricMatcher.encodeVectorSignature(
            listOf(
                0.6100f, 0.7900f, 0.9500f, 0.5200f,
                0.5500f, 1.1500f, 0.4800f, 0.7200f,
                0.5600f, 0.1900f, 0.2000f, 0.9500f,
                1.4500f, 0.2600f, 0.5800f, 0.3400f
            )
        )
        assertNull(FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(strangerScan, roster))
        assertNull(FaceBiometricMatcher.matchBestEmployee(scanForA, emptyList()))
    }

    @Test
    fun passcodeResolver_resolvesDedicatedCheckInAndCheckOutCodes() {
        val employees = listOf(
            Employee(
                id = "emp_muhammad_ansar",
                employeeCode = "NICH-0147",
                name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
                passcode = "147",
                checkInCode = "147",
                checkOutCode = "369",
                isActive = true
            ),
            Employee(
                id = "emp_nich_1042",
                employeeCode = "NICH-1042",
                name = "Dr. Ananya Rao",
                passcode = "1042",
                isActive = true
            )
        )

        val checkIn = PasscodeResolver.resolve("147", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        assertNotNull(checkIn)
        assertEquals("emp_muhammad_ansar", checkIn?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, checkIn?.eventType)

        val checkOut = PasscodeResolver.resolve("369", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN)
        assertNotNull(checkOut)
        assertEquals("emp_muhammad_ansar", checkOut?.employee?.id)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, checkOut?.eventType)

        val fallbackTime = PasscodeResolver.resolve("1042", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT)
        assertNotNull(fallbackTime)
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, fallbackTime?.eventType)

        assertNull(PasscodeResolver.resolve("9999", employees, AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN))
    }

    @Test
    fun livenessEvaluator_validatesDirectionalHeadPosesAndSequence() {
        val sequence = LivenessDirection.buildChallengeSequence(seed = 42L)
        assertEquals(3, sequence.size)
        assertEquals(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY, sequence.last())
        assertEquals(true, sequence[0] != sequence[1])

        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT,
                headYawDeg = 14f,
                headPitchDeg = 0f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            false,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.TURN_LEFT,
                headYawDeg = -14f,
                headPitchDeg = 0f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.TURN_RIGHT,
                headYawDeg = -13f,
                headPitchDeg = 0f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.LOOK_UP,
                headYawDeg = 0f,
                headPitchDeg = 12f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.LOOK_DOWN,
                headYawDeg = 0f,
                headPitchDeg = -11f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
        assertEquals(
            true,
            LivenessEvaluator.isDirectionSatisfied(
                direction = LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY,
                headYawDeg = 3f,
                headPitchDeg = -2f,
                isFrontCamera = true
            )
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun bidiTextHelper_isolatesMixedEnglishUrduWithoutReversingParentheses() {
        val formatted = BidiTextHelper.formatEmployeeHeaderTitle(
            rawName = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
            employeeCode = "NICH-0147"
        )
        assertEquals(true, formatted.contains("\u2067محمد انصار\u2069"))
        assertEquals("Muhammad Ansar / محمد انصار - NICH-0147", BidiTextHelper.stripBidiControls(formatted))
    }
}

package com.example.data.report

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceLog
import com.example.data.model.Employee
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config
import java.io.BufferedReader
import java.io.InputStreamReader
import java.io.OutputStreamWriter
import java.net.InetAddress
import java.net.ServerSocket
import java.net.Socket
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [36])
class AttendanceReportTest {

    @Test
    fun generateReportPdf_createsValidPdfFile() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val employee = Employee(
            id = "emp_muhammad_ansar",
            employeeCode = "NICH-0147",
            name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
            department = "Executive Operations",
            roleTitle = "Authorized Staff",
            passcode = "147",
            checkInCode = "147",
            checkOutCode = "369"
        )
        val log = AttendanceLog(
            id = "log_pdf_test_001",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            department = employee.department,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.PASSCODE.wireValue,
            confidenceScore = 100L,
            loggedTimeLabel = "09:10:00 AM",
            loggedDateKey = "2026-11-15"
        )

        val selfieFile = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "unit_test_selfie.jpg")
        val bmp = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(64, 64, android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        java.io.FileOutputStream(selfieFile).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(android.graphics.Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()

        val pdfFile = AttendancePdfGenerator.generateReportPdf(
            context = context,
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.PASSCODE,
            confidenceScore = 100,
            syncedToCloud = false,
            recentDailyLogs = listOf(log),
            recipientEmail = AttendanceEmailReporter.TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL,
            liveSelfiePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
        )

        try {
            assertTrue(pdfFile.exists())
            assertTrue(pdfFile.length() > 200L)
            val rawBytes = pdfFile.readBytes()
            val header = rawBytes.copyOfRange(0, 5).toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
            assertEquals("%PDF-", header)
            val pdfContentText = rawBytes.toString(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
            assertTrue(pdfContentText.contains("unit_test_selfie.jpg") || rawBytes.size > 1500)
        } finally {
            pdfFile.delete()
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun buildWeeklySummary_aggregatesMondayToSundayDutyHoursAccurately() {
        val employee = Employee(
            id = "emp_ali",
            employeeCode = "EMP-001",
            name = "Ali Raza",
            department = "Operations",
            roleTitle = "Staff",
            checkInCode = "1001"
        )
        val monIn = AttendanceLog(
            id = "mon_in",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue,
            loggedTimeLabel = "09:00:00 AM",
            loggedDateKey = "2025-11-10"
        )
        val monOut = AttendanceLog(
            id = "mon_out",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT.wireValue,
            loggedTimeLabel = "05:00:00 PM",
            loggedDateKey = "2025-11-10"
        )
        val sunOut = AttendanceLog(
            id = "sun_out",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT.wireValue,
            loggedTimeLabel = "05:10:00 PM",
            loggedDateKey = "2025-11-16"
        )
        val sunIn = AttendanceLog(
            id = "sun_in",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue,
            loggedTimeLabel = "09:10:00 AM",
            loggedDateKey = "2025-11-16"
        )

        val summary = AttendancePdfGenerator.buildWeeklySummary(
            employee = employee,
            currentLog = sunOut,
            recentDailyLogs = listOf(monIn, monOut, sunIn, sunOut)
        )

        assertEquals(7, summary.rows.size)
        assertEquals("Monday, 10 Nov 2025", summary.rows.first().dayDateLabel)
        assertEquals("Sunday, 16 Nov 2025", summary.rows.last().dayDateLabel)
        assertEquals("09:00 AM", summary.rows.first().checkInLabel)
        assertEquals("05:00 PM", summary.rows.first().checkOutLabel)
        assertEquals("08:00 Hrs", summary.rows.first().dutyHoursLabel)
        assertEquals(true, summary.rows.first().isPresent)
        assertEquals(false, summary.rows[1].isPresent)
        assertEquals(2, summary.totalWorkingDays)
        assertEquals("16:00 Hrs", summary.totalDutyHoursLabel)
        assertEquals("Present", summary.overallStatusLabel)
        assertTrue(summary.weekRangeLabel.contains("10 Nov 2025"))
        assertTrue(summary.weekRangeLabel.contains("16 Nov 2025"))
    }

    @Test
    fun smtpEmailSender_executesCompleteMimeAttachmentHandshake() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val employee = Employee(
            id = "emp_ansar",
            employeeCode = "NICH-0147",
            name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
            department = "Executive Operations",
            roleTitle = "Authorized Staff"
        )
        val log = AttendanceLog(
            id = "log_smtp_test_001",
            employeeId = employee.id,
            employeeCode = employee.employeeCode,
            employeeName = employee.name,
            department = employee.department,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT.wireValue,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1.wireValue,
            confidenceScore = 97L,
            loggedTimeLabel = "05:30:00 PM",
            loggedDateKey = "2026-11-15"
        )
        val pdfFile = AttendancePdfGenerator.generateReportPdf(
            context = context,
            employee = employee,
            log = log,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1,
            confidenceScore = 97,
            syncedToCloud = true,
            recentDailyLogs = listOf(log),
            recipientEmail = AttendanceEmailReporter.TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL
        )

        val selfieFile = java.io.File(context.cacheDir, "smtp_unit_selfie.jpg")
        val bmp = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(48, 48, android.graphics.Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        java.io.FileOutputStream(selfieFile).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(android.graphics.Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()

        val serverSocket = ServerSocket(0, 1, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val receivedCommands = mutableListOf<String>()
        val dataPayload = StringBuilder()
        val serverDone = CountDownLatch(1)

        val serverThread = Thread {
            serverSocket.use { srv ->
                srv.accept().use { client ->
                    val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(client.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                    val writer = OutputStreamWriter(client.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)

                    fun reply(msg: String) {
                        writer.write("$msg\r\n")
                        writer.flush()
                    }

                    reply("220 localhost ESMTP ready")
                    var inData = false
                    while (true) {
                        val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                        if (inData) {
                            if (line == ".") {
                                inData = false
                                reply("250 2.0.0 OK queued")
                            } else {
                                dataPayload.append(line).append('\n')
                            }
                        } else {
                            receivedCommands += line
                            when {
                                line.startsWith("EHLO") -> reply("250-localhost\r\n250 AUTH LOGIN")
                                line == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 VXNlcm5hbWU6")
                                receivedCommands.count { it == "AUTH LOGIN" } == 1 &&
                                    receivedCommands.size >= 3 &&
                                    receivedCommands[receivedCommands.size - 2] == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 UGFzc3dvcmQ6")
                                line.startsWith("MAIL FROM:") -> reply("250 2.1.0 OK")
                                line.startsWith("RCPT TO:") -> reply("250 2.1.5 OK")
                                line == "DATA" -> {
                                    reply("354 End data with <CR><LF>.<CR><LF>")
                                    inData = true
                                }
                                line == "QUIT" -> {
                                    reply("221 Bye")
                                    break
                                }
                                else -> reply("235 2.7.0 Authentication successful")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            serverDone.countDown()
        }
        serverThread.start()

        try {
            val sender = SmtpEmailSender { cfg ->
                Socket(cfg.host, cfg.port)
            }
            sender.sendWithAttachment(
                config = SmtpConfig(
                    host = "127.0.0.1",
                    port = serverSocket.localPort,
                    useSsl = false,
                    username = "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com",
                    appPassword = "abcd efgh ijkl mnop"
                ),
                toEmail = AttendanceEmailReporter.TARGET_RECIPIENT_EMAIL,
                subject = "NICH Attendance 3 • CHECK-OUT • محمد انصار",
                plainTextBody = "Attendance verified for NICH-0147.",
                attachmentFile = pdfFile,
                extraAttachmentFile = selfieFile
            )

            assertTrue(serverDone.await(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS))
            assertTrue(receivedCommands.any { it == "MAIL FROM:<Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com>" })
            assertTrue(receivedCommands.any { it == "RCPT TO:<Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com>" })
            val rawMime = dataPayload.toString()
            assertTrue(rawMime.contains("Content-Type: application/pdf; name=\"${pdfFile.name}\""))
            assertTrue(rawMime.contains("Content-Type: image/jpeg; name=\"${selfieFile.name}\""))
            assertTrue(rawMime.contains("Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64"))
        } finally {
            pdfFile.delete()
            selfieFile.delete()
            runCatching { serverSocket.close() }
        }
    }
}

// FILE: app/src/test/java/com/example/data/repository/AttendanceRepositoryRuleTest.kt
======================================================================
package com.example.data.repository

import com.example.base.FirestoreEmulatorTestBase
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceEventType
import com.example.data.model.AttendanceTimeRules
import com.example.data.model.VerificationMethod
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestoreException
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.runBlocking
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await
import kotlinx.coroutines.withTimeout
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Assert.fail
import org.junit.Test
import java.util.UUID

class AttendanceRepositoryRuleTest : FirestoreEmulatorTestBase() {

    @Test
    fun createEmployeeAndVerifyPasscode_authenticatedOwner_succeeds() = runBlocking {
        val uid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        val repository = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)
        val uniquePasscode = (100..999).random().toString()
        val uniqueOutCode = (100..999).random().toString()
        val uniqueCode = "NICH-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(6)}"

        val createResult = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.createEmployee(
                employeeCode = uniqueCode,
                name = "محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)",
                department = "Executive Operations",
                roleTitle = "Authorized Staff",
                passcode = uniquePasscode,
                avatarKey = "avatar_ansar",
                faceSignature = "0.4350:0.4800:0.6400:0.7500",
                checkInCode = uniquePasscode,
                checkOutCode = uniqueOutCode
            )
        }
        assertTrue(createResult.isSuccess)
        val employeeId = createResult.getOrThrow()
        assertTrue(employeeId.isNotBlank())

        val verifiedIn = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.verifyEmployeeByPasscode(uniquePasscode)
        }
        assertTrue(verifiedIn.isSuccess)
        assertEquals("محمد انصار (Muhammad Ansar)", verifiedIn.getOrThrow()?.name)

        val verifiedOut = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.verifyEmployeeByPasscode(uniqueOutCode)
        }
        assertTrue(verifiedOut.isSuccess)
        assertEquals(employeeId, verifiedOut.getOrThrow()?.id)

        val emitted = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.observeEmployees(uid).first { list -> list.any { it.id == employeeId } }
        }
        assertTrue(emitted.any { it.id == employeeId })
    }

    @Test
    fun logAttendance_validEmployee_createsImmutableLog() = runBlocking {
        val uid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        val repository = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)

        val employeeId = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.createEmployee(
                employeeCode = "NICH-${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(6)}",
                name = "Marcus Chen",
                department = "Neural Systems",
                roleTitle = "Principal Architect",
                passcode = (1000..9999).random().toString(),
                avatarKey = "avatar_2"
            ).getOrThrow()
        }
        val employee = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.getEmployeeById(employeeId).getOrThrow()!!
        }

        val logResult = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.logAttendance(
                employee = employee,
                eventType = AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(9),
                verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_2,
                confidenceScore = 95,
                loggedTimeLabel = "09:30:00 AM",
                loggedDateKey = "2026-11-13"
            )
        }
        assertTrue(logResult.isSuccess)
        val logId = logResult.getOrThrow()

        val logs = withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            repository.observeAttendanceLogs(uid).first { list -> list.any { it.id == logId } }
        }
        val createdLog = logs.first { it.id == logId }
        assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN.wireValue, createdLog.eventType)
        assertEquals(VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_2.wireValue, createdLog.verificationMethod)
    }

    @Test
    fun getEmployeeById_crossUserAccess_failsWithPermissionDenied() = runBlocking {
        val aliceUid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        val aliceRepo = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)
        val employeeId = withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            aliceRepo.createEmployee(
                employeeCode = "NICH-CROSS",
                name = "Elena Vance",
                department = "Operations",
                roleTitle = "Director",
                passcode = "3095",
                avatarKey = "avatar_3"
            ).getOrThrow()
        }

        val bobUid = signInTestUser(BOB_EMAIL)
        check(bobUid != aliceUid)

        try {
            withTimeout(DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                firestore.collection("users").document(aliceUid)
                    .collection("employees").document(employeeId)
                    .get()
                    .await()
            }
            fail("Expected PERMISSION_DENIED for cross-user read")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val fsEx = e.unwrapFirestoreException()
            assertNotNull("Expected FirebaseFirestoreException, got $e", fsEx)
            assertEquals(FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.PERMISSION_DENIED, fsEx?.code)
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun observeEmployees_unauthenticatedUser_failsWithPermissionDenied() = runBlocking {
        val aliceUid = signInTestUser(ALICE_EMAIL)
        auth.signOut()
        val repository = AttendanceRepository(firestore, auth)

        try {
            withTimeout(FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS) {
                repository.observeEmployees(aliceUid).first()
            }
            fail("Expected PERMISSION_DENIED for unauthenticated observeEmployees")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            val fsEx = e.unwrapFirestoreException()
            assertNotNull("Expected nested FirebaseFirestoreException, got $e", fsEx)
            assertEquals(FirebaseFirestoreException.Code.PERMISSION_DENIED, fsEx?.code)
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val ALICE_EMAIL = "alice.nich@test.com"
        const val BOB_EMAIL = "bob.nich@test.com"
        const val DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000L
        const val FLOW_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000L
    }
}

class AttendanceViewModelStrictTest {

    @get:Rule
    val composeRule = createComposeRule()

    private val testDispatcher = StandardTestDispatcher()
    private lateinit var context: Context

    private val vectorEmployeeA =
        "0.4200:0.4800:0.6200:0.7400:0.2700:0.7500:0.7100:0.9600:0.7800:0.3400:0.3400:0.5600:2.1000:0.4500:0.2900:0.1400"
    private val vectorEmployeeB =
        "0.3400:0.3800:0.5100:0.8600:0.1900:0.5900:0.8800:0.9100:0.8900:0.4400:0.4500:0.7200:2.5500:0.6200:0.3900:0.2100"
    private val vectorEmployeeAPartial50Plus =
        "0.5800:0.6400:0.7800:0.9000:0.4300:0.9100:0.8700:1.1200:0.9400:0.5000:0.5000:0.7200:1.8500:0.6100:0.4500:0.3000"
    private val vectorStranger =
        "0.6100:0.7900:0.9500:0.5200:0.5500:1.1500:0.4800:0.7200:0.5600:0.1900:0.2000:0.9500:1.4500:0.2600:0.5800:0.3400"

    private fun morningCheckInCalendar(): Calendar = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        clear()
        set(2026, Calendar.NOVEMBER, 14, 9, 15, 0)
    }

    private fun afternoonCheckOutCalendar(): Calendar = Calendar.getInstance().apply {
        clear()
        set(2026, Calendar.NOVEMBER, 14, 17, 30, 0)
    }

    private fun createTempPortrait(name: String): File {
        val file = File(context.cacheDir, name)
        val bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(48, 48, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        FileOutputStream(file).use { stream ->
            bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 85, stream)
        }
        bmp.recycle()
        return file
    }

    private fun feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
        viewModel: AttendanceViewModel,
        signature: String,
        selfiePath: String? = null
    ) {
        val eyeSequence = listOf(0.88f, 0.14f, 0.90f)
        eyeSequence.forEach { eyeProb ->
            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(
                FaceAnalysisFrame(
                    faceDetected = true,
                    eyesLocked = true,
                    headPoseAligned = true,
                    leftEyeOpenProbability = eyeProb,
                    rightEyeOpenProbability = eyeProb,
                    smilingProbability = 0.12f,
                    faceCenterXNorm = 0.50f,
                    signature = signature,
                    selfieImagePath = selfiePath
                )
            )
        }
    }

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        Dispatchers.setMain(testDispatcher)
        context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
        context.getSharedPreferences("nich_offline_attendance", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .clear()
            .commit()
    }

    @After
    fun tearDown() {
        Dispatchers.resetMain()
    }

    @Test
    fun terminalStartsScanningImmediatelyAndRemainsLiveInContinuousKioskMode() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val vmStore = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelStore()
        val viewModel = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider(
            vmStore,
            object : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider.Factory {
                @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                override fun <T : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel> create(modelClass: Class<T>): T {
                    return AttendanceViewModel(
                        offlineStore = store,
                        autoInitBackgroundJobs = true,
                        continuousKioskMode = true
                    ) as T
                }
            }
        )[AttendanceViewModel::class.java]

        try {
            runCurrent()

            val initialPhase = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(initialPhase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            assertEquals(false, (initialPhase as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)

            // Fresh install opens local registration flow cleanly before profiles are registered
            assertTrue(viewModel.showRegistrationScreen.value)

            advanceTimeBy(10_000L)
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)

            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue((viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)

            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(FaceAnalysisFrame(faceDetected = false))
            advanceTimeBy(20_000L)
            runCurrent()
            val standby = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(standby is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            assertTrue((standby as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning).cameraReady)
        } finally {
            vmStore.clear()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun checkIn_doesNotSendEmailAndAutoResetsToScanningAfterConfirmation() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val dispatchCount = AtomicInteger(0)
        val smtpSender = SmtpEmailSender { _ ->
            dispatchCount.incrementAndGet()
            throw IllegalStateException("Check-In must never dispatch email")
        }
        val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
            context = context,
            smtpSender = smtpSender,
            senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
            appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
        )
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val photo = createTempPortrait("checkin_a.jpg")
        try {
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = photo.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )
            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                emailReporter = reporter,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { morningCheckInCalendar() },
                continuousKioskMode = true,
                sameEmployeeCooldownMs = 0L
            )
            runCurrent()

            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan()
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorEmployeeA,
                selfiePath = photo.absolutePath
            )
            runCurrent()

            val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(verified is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)
            verified as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN, verified.eventType)
            assertEquals("Bilal Ahmed", verified.employee.name)
            assertTrue(verified.confidenceScore >= 50)
            assertEquals(0, dispatchCount.get())

            advanceTimeBy(2_600L)
            runCurrent()
            val returned = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(returned is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            assertEquals(0, dispatchCount.get())
        } finally {
            photo.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun faceMatching_dynamicallyMatchesDistinctLocalEmployeesAndRejectsStrangers() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val photoA = createTempPortrait("reg_a.jpg")
        val photoB = createTempPortrait("reg_b.jpg")
        try {
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = photoA.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    ),
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Dr. Ananya Rao",
                        department = "Security",
                        photoPath = photoB.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeB
                    )
                )
            )

            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { morningCheckInCalendar() },
                sameEmployeeCooldownMs = 0L
            )
            runCurrent()

            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            // Stranger (< 50%) must NOT verify or fall back to any employee
            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorStranger
            )
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)

            // Scanning Employee B matches ONLY Dr. Ananya Rao with her photo
            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorEmployeeB
            )
            runCurrent()

            val verifiedB = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            assertEquals("Dr. Ananya Rao", verifiedB.employee.name)
            assertTrue(verifiedB.employee.localPhotoPath.isNotBlank())
            assertTrue(File(verifiedB.employee.localPhotoPath).exists())

            viewModel.resetToReady()
            runCurrent()

            // Partial lighting/angle scan (~61%) for Employee A marks Bilal Ahmed seamlessly at >= 50%
            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorEmployeeAPartial50Plus
            )
            runCurrent()

            val verifiedA = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            assertEquals("Bilal Ahmed", verifiedA.employee.name)
            assertTrue(verifiedA.confidenceScore in 50..89)
            assertTrue(verifiedA.employee.localPhotoPath.isNotBlank())
            assertTrue(File(verifiedA.employee.localPhotoPath).exists())
        } finally {
            photoA.delete()
            photoB.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun checkOut_automaticallyTriggersSmtpTlsEmailWithPdfAndPhotoToNich() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val dispatchCount = AtomicInteger(0)
        val capturedRecipients = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val capturedMimePayloads = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val serverSocket = ServerSocket(0, 4, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val serverThread = Thread {
            try {
                while (!serverSocket.isClosed) {
                    val client = serverSocket.accept()
                    val reader = BufferedReader(
                        InputStreamReader(client.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                    )
                    val writer = OutputStreamWriter(client.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
                    fun reply(msg: String) {
                        writer.write("$msg\r\n")
                        writer.flush()
                    }
                    reply("220 localhost ESMTP ready")
                    var inData = false
                    var sentAuthUser = false
                    val payloadBuilder = StringBuilder()
                    while (true) {
                        val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                        if (inData) {
                            if (line == ".") {
                                inData = false
                                capturedMimePayloads.add(payloadBuilder.toString())
                                dispatchCount.incrementAndGet()
                                reply("250 2.0.0 OK queued")
                            } else {
                                payloadBuilder.append(line).append('\n')
                            }
                        } else {
                            when {
                                line.startsWith("EHLO") -> reply("250-localhost\r\n250 AUTH LOGIN")
                                line == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 VXNlcm5hbWU6")
                                !sentAuthUser && line != "AUTH LOGIN" -> {
                                    sentAuthUser = true
                                    reply("334 UGFzc3dvcmQ6")
                                }
                                line.startsWith("MAIL FROM:") -> reply("250 2.1.0 OK")
                                line.startsWith("RCPT TO:") -> {
                                    capturedRecipients.add(line)
                                    reply("250 2.1.5 OK")
                                }
                                line == "DATA" -> {
                                    reply("354 End data with <CR><LF>.<CR><LF>")
                                    inData = true
                                }
                                line == "QUIT" -> {
                                    reply("221 Bye")
                                    break
                                }
                                else -> reply("235 2.7.0 OK")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    client.close()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                // Server closed cleanly
            }
        }
        serverThread.isDaemon = true
        serverThread.start()

        val selfieFile = createTempPortrait("checkout_live_selfie.jpg")
        try {
            val smtpSender = SmtpEmailSender { _ ->
                Socket("127.0.0.1", serverSocket.localPort)
            }
            val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
                context = context,
                smtpSender = smtpSender,
                senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
                appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
            )
            val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = selfieFile.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )
            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                emailReporter = reporter,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { afternoonCheckOutCalendar() },
                continuousKioskMode = true
            )
            runCurrent()

            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorEmployeeA,
                selfiePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
            )
            runCurrent()

            val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, verified.eventType)

            // Wait for automatic Check-Out SMTP/TLS dispatch to finish without requiring an extra click
            val deadlineMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while (dispatchCount.get() < 1 && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadlineMs) {
                Thread.sleep(25L)
                runCurrent()
            }
            assertEquals(1, dispatchCount.get())
            assertTrue(capturedRecipients.any { it.contains("Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com", ignoreCase = true) })
            val mime = capturedMimePayloads.single()
            assertTrue(mime.contains("Content-Type: application/pdf;"))
            assertTrue(mime.contains("Content-Type: image/jpeg;"))
            assertEquals(
                EmailReportStatus.SENT,
                (viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified).emailReportStatus
            )
        } finally {
            runCatching { serverSocket.close() }
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun registrationUi_addsMultipleDynamicEmployeeFormsAboveSubmitButton() {
        composeRule.setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                EmployeeRegistrationScreen(
                    savedEmployees = emptyList(),
                    onRegisterBatch = {},
                    onDeleteEmployee = {},
                    onNavigateToScanner = {}
                )
