        }
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText("Employee #1").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("add_another_employee_button").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed().performClick()
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText("Employee #2").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("employee_name_input_1").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("capture_photo_button_1").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("submit_registration_button").performScrollTo().assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    @Test
    fun terminalUi_checkInHidesEmailButtonAndCheckOutShowsSendEmailButton() {
        val ansar = OfflineAttendanceStore.defaultPreRegisteredEmployees().first()

        val checkInPhase = TerminalUiPhase.Verified(
            employee = ansar,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_IN,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1,
            confidenceScore = 96,
            timestampLabel = "09:15:00 AM",
            syncedToCloud = true
        )
        val phaseState = androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf<TerminalUiPhase>(checkInPhase)

        var checkOutEmailClicks = 0
        composeRule.setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                TerminalDashboardScreen(
                    terminalPhase = phaseState.value,
                    onStartVipScan = {},
                    onCameraInitialized = {},
                    onCameraInitFailed = {},
                    onFrameAnalyzed = {},
                    onSendCheckOutEmail = { checkOutEmailClicks++ },
                    onResetTerminal = {}
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("open_registration_button").assertDoesNotExist()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText("Register Staff").assertDoesNotExist()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("verification_successful_overlay").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("send_checkout_email_button").assertDoesNotExist()

        phaseState.value = checkInPhase.copy(
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT,
            timestampLabel = "05:30:00 PM"
        )
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("open_registration_button").assertDoesNotExist()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("send_checkout_email_button").assertIsDisplayed().performClick()
        assertEquals(1, checkOutEmailClicks)
    }

    @Test
    fun sendEmailAndFinish_launchesDeviceAccountPdfIntentWhenSmtpUnconfigured() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
            context = context,
            appPasswordProvider = { "MY_GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD" }
        )
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val selfieFile = createTempPortrait("finish_flow_selfie.jpg")
        try {
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = selfieFile.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )
            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                emailReporter = reporter,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { afternoonCheckOutCalendar() }
            )
            runCurrent()

            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorEmployeeA,
                selfiePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
            )
            runCurrent()

            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)

            var launchedIntent: Intent? = null
            viewModel.sendEmailAndFinish { intent ->
                launchedIntent = intent
            }

            val deadlineMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while (launchedIntent == null && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadlineMs) {
                Thread.sleep(25L)
                runCurrent()
            }

            assertNotNull(launchedIntent)
            assertFalse(viewModel.showRegistrationScreen.value)
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
        } finally {
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun voiceAssistant_speaksWelcomeAlignmentVerificationAndBlocksCheckOutSkipUntilEmailSent() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val spokenLines = mutableListOf<String>()
        val spySpeaker = object : VoiceSpeaker {
            override fun speak(message: String, flush: Boolean) {
                spokenLines += message
            }
            override fun shutdown() = Unit
        }
        val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
            context = context,
            appPasswordProvider = { "MY_GMAIL_APP_PASSWORD" }
        )
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val selfieFile = createTempPortrait("voice_enforce_selfie.jpg")
        try {
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = selfieFile.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )
            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                emailReporter = reporter,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { afternoonCheckOutCalendar() },
                voiceSpeaker = spySpeaker
            )
            runCurrent()

            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(spokenLines.any { it.contains("Welcome to NICH Attendance 3", ignoreCase = true) })

            // Alignment mismatch speaks guidance prompt
            viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(
                FaceAnalysisFrame(
                    faceDetected = true,
                    eyesLocked = false,
                    headPoseAligned = false
                )
            )
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(spokenLines.any { it.contains("align your face", ignoreCase = true) })

            // Valid Check-Out recognition with live human blink
            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                signature = vectorEmployeeA,
                selfiePath = selfieFile.absolutePath
            )
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(spokenLines.any { it.contains("Check-out verified for Bilal Ahmed", ignoreCase = true) })

            // Trying to skip / close / reset before sending email MUST be blocked
            viewModel.attemptSkipOrDismissCheckOut()
            viewModel.resetToReady()
            runCurrent()

            val blockedPhase = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            assertTrue(blockedPhase.emailEnforcementWarningActive)
            assertEquals(VoicePromptScript.MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT, blockedPhase.emailEnforcementMessage)
            assertFalse(blockedPhase.isCheckOutEmailCompleted)
            assertTrue(spokenLines.any { it == VoicePromptScript.MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT })

            // Periodic enforcement loop repeats if still unsubmitted
            advanceTimeBy(6_100L)
            runCurrent()
            assertTrue(
                spokenLines.count { it == VoicePromptScript.MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT } >= 2
            )

            // Sending email completes Check-Out and unlocks return to scanner
            var intentLaunched = false
            viewModel.sendEmailAndFinish { intentLaunched = true }
            val deadlineMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while (!intentLaunched && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadlineMs) {
                Thread.sleep(25L)
                runCurrent()
            }
            assertTrue(intentLaunched)
            assertTrue(viewModel.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
        } finally {
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun terminalUi_checkOutCloseAttemptDisplaysPersistentBlockingWarningBanner() {
        val ansar = OfflineAttendanceStore.defaultPreRegisteredEmployees().first()
        val initialCheckOut = TerminalUiPhase.Verified(
            employee = ansar,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1,
            confidenceScore = 94,
            timestampLabel = "05:45:00 PM",
            syncedToCloud = false,
            emailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.NEEDS_CONFIG,
            emailEnforcementWarningActive = false
        )
        val phaseState = androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf<TerminalUiPhase>(initialCheckOut)
        var skipAttempts = 0

        composeRule.setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                TerminalDashboardScreen(
                    terminalPhase = phaseState.value,
                    onStartVipScan = {},
                    onCameraInitialized = {},
                    onCameraInitFailed = {},
                    onFrameAnalyzed = {},
                    onSendCheckOutEmail = {},
                    onResetTerminal = {},
                    onAttemptSkipCheckOutEmail = {
                        skipAttempts++
                        phaseState.value = initialCheckOut.copy(
                            emailEnforcementWarningActive = true,
                            emailEnforcementMessage = VoicePromptScript.MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT
                        )
                    }
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("email_enforcement_warning_banner").assertDoesNotExist()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("close_checkout_overlay_button").assertIsDisplayed().performClick()
        composeRule.waitForIdle()

        assertEquals(1, skipAttempts)
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("email_enforcement_warning_banner").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(VoicePromptScript.MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT).assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    @Test
    fun antiSpoofing_rejectsStaticPrintedPhotoAndPhoneScreenWithoutBlinkOrDynamicSmile() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val spokenLines = mutableListOf<String>()
        val spySpeaker = object : VoiceSpeaker {
            override fun speak(message: String, flush: Boolean) {
                spokenLines += message
            }
            override fun shutdown() = Unit
        }
        val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
        val photo = createTempPortrait("spoof_reg.jpg")
        try {
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = photo.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )
            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { morningCheckInCalendar() },
                voiceSpeaker = spySpeaker
            )
            runCurrent()
            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan()
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            // Feed 10 completely static frames simulating a printed photo / mobile screenshot
            val staticPhotoFrame = FaceAnalysisFrame(
                faceDetected = true,
                eyesLocked = true,
                headPoseAligned = true,
                headYawDeg = 1.0f,
                headPitchDeg = 0.5f,
                leftEyeOpenProbability = 0.85f,
                rightEyeOpenProbability = 0.86f,
                smilingProbability = 0.72f, // Even a static smiling screenshot must fail if static!
                faceCenterXNorm = 0.50f,
                signature = vectorEmployeeA,
                selfieImagePath = photo.absolutePath
            )
            repeat(10) {
                viewModel.onLiveFaceFrameAnalyzed(staticPhotoFrame)
            }
            runCurrent()

            val phase = viewModel.terminalPhase.value
            assertTrue(phase is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            phase as TerminalUiPhase.Scanning
            assertFalse(phase.livenessVerified)
            assertTrue(phase.spoofSuspected)
            assertTrue(store.logs.value.isEmpty())
            assertTrue(spokenLines.any { it.contains("Static photo or screen detected", ignoreCase = true) })
        } finally {
            photo.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun ntpLockAndGpsLocation_overridesTamperedClockAndEmbedsInPdfAndEmail() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val capturedBodies = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val serverSocket = ServerSocket(0, 4, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val serverThread = Thread {
            try {
                while (!serverSocket.isClosed) {
                    val client = serverSocket.accept()
                    val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(client.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                    val writer = OutputStreamWriter(client.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
                    fun reply(msg: String) {
                        writer.write("$msg\r\n")
                        writer.flush()
                    }
                    reply("220 localhost ESMTP ready")
                    var inData = false
                    var sentAuthUser = false
                    val payloadBuilder = StringBuilder()
                    while (true) {
                        val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                        if (inData) {
                            if (line == ".") {
                                inData = false
                                capturedBodies.add(payloadBuilder.toString())
                                reply("250 2.0.0 OK queued")
                            } else {
                                payloadBuilder.append(line).append('\n')
                            }
                        } else {
                            when {
                                line.startsWith("EHLO") -> reply("250-localhost\r\n250 AUTH LOGIN")
                                line == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 VXNlcm5hbWU6")
                                !sentAuthUser && line != "AUTH LOGIN" -> {
                                    sentAuthUser = true
                                    reply("334 UGFzc3dvcmQ6")
                                }
                                line.startsWith("MAIL FROM:") -> reply("250 2.1.0 OK")
                                line.startsWith("RCPT TO:") -> reply("250 2.1.5 OK")
                                line == "DATA" -> {
                                    reply("354 End data with <CR><LF>.<CR><LF>")
                                    inData = true
                                }
                                line == "QUIT" -> {
                                    reply("221 Bye")
                                    break
                                }
                                else -> reply("235 2.7.0 OK")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    client.close()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
        }
        serverThread.isDaemon = true
        serverThread.start()

        val selfieFile = createTempPortrait("gps_ntp_selfie.jpg")
        try {
            // Tampered phone clock says 08:00 AM (Check-In), but NTP server says 05:40 PM (Check-Out)
            val trueNtpCal = afternoonCheckOutCalendar().apply {
                set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, 17)
                set(Calendar.MINUTE, 40)
            }
            val fakeNtpProvider = object : NetworkTimeProvider {
                private val snap = NetworkTimeSnapshot(
                    epochMillis = trueNtpCal.timeInMillis,
                    sourceLabel = "NTP Verified (time.google.com) • Clock Tamper Blocked",
                    clockDriftMs = 34_800_000L,
                    tamperingDetected = true,
                    isNetworkVerified = true
                )
                override suspend fun syncNow(): NetworkTimeSnapshot = snap
                override fun currentSnapshot(): NetworkTimeSnapshot = snap
                override fun currentCalendar(): Calendar = (trueNtpCal.clone() as Calendar)
            }
            val fakeLocationProvider = object : LocationAddressProvider {
                override suspend fun captureCurrentLocation(): VerifiedLocationSnapshot =
                    VerifiedLocationSnapshot(
                        latitude = 24.860734,
                        longitude = 67.001136,
                        coordinatesLabel = "24.860734, 67.001136 (±9m)",
                        readableAddress = "NICH Campus, Rafiqui Shaheed Rd, Karachi",
                        accuracyMeters = 9f,
                        providerName = "gps"
                    )
            }

            val smtpSender = SmtpEmailSender { _ -> Socket("127.0.0.1", serverSocket.localPort) }
            val reporter = AttendanceEmailReporter(
                context = context,
                smtpSender = smtpSender,
                senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
                appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
            )
            val store = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
            store.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = selfieFile.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )

            val viewModel = AttendanceViewModel(
                offlineStore = store,
                emailReporter = reporter,
                autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                calendarProvider = { morningCheckInCalendar() },
                networkTimeProvider = fakeNtpProvider,
                locationProvider = fakeLocationProvider
            )
            runCurrent()
            viewModel.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            viewModel.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()

            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(viewModel, vectorEmployeeA, selfieFile.absolutePath)
            runCurrent()

            val verified = viewModel.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
            // Proven NTP override: afternoon CHECK_OUT recorded despite 09:15 AM device clock!
            assertEquals(AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT, verified.eventType)
            assertEquals("24.860734, 67.001136 (±9m)", verified.gpsCoordinates)
            assertEquals("NICH Campus, Rafiqui Shaheed Rd, Karachi", verified.locationAddress)
            assertTrue(verified.timeSourceLabel.contains("NTP Verified"))

            val savedLog = store.logs.value.first()
            assertEquals("24.860734, 67.001136 (±9m)", savedLog.gpsCoordinates)
            assertEquals("NICH Campus, Rafiqui Shaheed Rd, Karachi", savedLog.locationAddress)
            assertTrue(savedLog.timeSource.contains("NTP Verified"))

            val deadlineMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while (capturedBodies.isEmpty() && System.currentTimeMillis() < deadlineMs) {
                Thread.sleep(25L)
                runCurrent()
            }
            assertEquals(1, capturedBodies.size)
            val mime = capturedBodies.single()
            assertTrue(mime.contains("Content-Type: application/pdf;"))
            assertTrue(mime.contains("Content-Type: image/jpeg;"))
            val textPartMarker = "Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8\nContent-Transfer-Encoding: base64\n\n"
            val startIdx = mime.indexOf(textPartMarker)
            assertTrue(startIdx >= 0)
            val bodyAfterHeader = mime.substring(startIdx + textPartMarker.length)
            val encodedTextBody = bodyAfterHeader.substringBefore("\n--====NICH_ATT_")
            val decodedEmailBody = String(
                android.util.Base64.decode(encodedTextBody, android.util.Base64.DEFAULT),
                StandardCharsets.UTF_8
            )
            assertTrue(decodedEmailBody.contains("24.860734, 67.001136"))
            assertTrue(decodedEmailBody.contains("NICH Campus, Rafiqui Shaheed Rd, Karachi"))
            assertTrue(decodedEmailBody.contains("NTP Verified"))
        } finally {
            runCatching { serverSocket.close() }
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun sntpClient_parsesRealUdpNtpResponseAndIgnoresPostSyncPhoneClockTampering() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val authoritativeNtpEpochMs = 1_794_900_000_000L // Deterministic 2026 epoch ms
        val udpServer = DatagramSocket(0, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val serverThread = Thread {
            try {
                val reqBuf = ByteArray(48)
                val reqPacket = DatagramPacket(reqBuf, reqBuf.size)
                udpServer.receive(reqPacket)

                val respBuf = ByteArray(48)
                respBuf[0] = 0x1C // LI=0, VN=3, Mode=4 (Server)
                // Copy client's transmit timestamp (40..47) into originate timestamp (24..31)
                System.arraycopy(reqBuf, 40, respBuf, 24, 8)
                SntpNetworkTimeProvider.writeTimeStamp(respBuf, 32, authoritativeNtpEpochMs)
                SntpNetworkTimeProvider.writeTimeStamp(respBuf, 40, authoritativeNtpEpochMs)

                val respPacket = DatagramPacket(
                    respBuf,
                    respBuf.size,
                    reqPacket.address,
                    reqPacket.port
                )
                udpServer.send(respPacket)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
        }
        serverThread.isDaemon = true
        serverThread.start()

        try {
            // Simulate user manually rolling phone wall clock backward by 6 hours
            val deviceWallMs = AtomicLong(authoritativeNtpEpochMs - (6 * 3600 * 1000L))
            val monotonicElapsedMs = AtomicLong(10_000L)

            val provider = SntpNetworkTimeProvider(
                ntpHosts = listOf("127.0.0.1"),
                ntpPort = udpServer.localPort,
                timeoutMs = 2000,
                allowHttpFallback = false,
                wallClockMs = { deviceWallMs.get() },
                elapsedRealtimeMs = { monotonicElapsedMs.get() },
                timeZoneProvider = { TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC") }
            )

            val synced = provider.syncNow()
            assertTrue(synced.isNetworkVerified)
            assertTrue(synced.tamperingDetected)
            assertTrue(kotlin.math.abs(synced.epochMillis - authoritativeNtpEpochMs) < 1_000L)

            // Even if user alters phone clock further AND monotonic uptime advances 5 seconds:
            monotonicElapsedMs.addAndGet(5_000L)
            deviceWallMs.addAndGet(-3_600_000L)

            val afterTamper = provider.currentSnapshot()
            assertTrue(afterTamper.tamperingDetected)
            assertTrue(afterTamper.sourceLabel.contains("Clock Tamper Blocked"))
            assertTrue(kotlin.math.abs(afterTamper.epochMillis - (authoritativeNtpEpochMs + 5_000L)) < 1_000L)
        } finally {
            runCatching { udpServer.close() }
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun startupPermissionGate_blocksStartupSpeaksWarningAndOpensSystemSettings() {
        var warningSpokenCount = 0
        var settingsOpenedCount = 0
        var refreshedCount = 0

        composeRule.setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                StartupPermissionGateScreen(
                    status = StartupPermissionStatus(cameraGranted = false, locationGranted = false),
                    onRefreshPermissions = { refreshedCount++ },
                    onSpeakPermissionWarning = { warningSpokenCount++ },
                    onOpenSystemSettings = { settingsOpenedCount++ },
                    autoRequestOnLaunch = false
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.waitForIdle()

        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("startup_permission_gate_screen").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("permission_voice_warning_banner").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(VoicePromptScript.REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_WARNING_PROMPT).assertIsDisplayed()
        assertTrue(warningSpokenCount >= 1)

        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("open_app_settings_button").performClick()
        composeRule.waitForIdle()
        assertEquals(1, settingsOpenedCount)
        assertTrue(warningSpokenCount >= 2)
    }

    @Test
    fun persistentCheckOutLock_survivesAppRestart_blocksNewCheckInsUntilSmtpSucceeds() = runTest(testDispatcher) {
        val spokenLines = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val spySpeaker = object : VoiceSpeaker {
            override fun speak(message: String, flush: Boolean) {
                spokenLines += message
            }
            override fun shutdown() = Unit
        }
        val smtpNetworkReachable = java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean(false)
        val receivedPayloads = java.util.Collections.synchronizedList(mutableListOf<String>())
        val serverSocket = ServerSocket(0, 4, InetAddress.getByName("127.0.0.1"))
        val serverThread = Thread {
            try {
                while (!serverSocket.isClosed) {
                    val client = serverSocket.accept()
                    val reader = BufferedReader(InputStreamReader(client.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
                    val writer = OutputStreamWriter(client.getOutputStream(), StandardCharsets.US_ASCII)
                    fun reply(msg: String) {
                        writer.write("$msg\r\n")
                        writer.flush()
                    }
                    reply("220 localhost ESMTP ready")
                    var inData = false
                    var sentAuthUser = false
                    val payloadBuilder = StringBuilder()
                    while (true) {
                        val line = reader.readLine() ?: break
                        if (inData) {
                            if (line == ".") {
                                inData = false
                                receivedPayloads.add(payloadBuilder.toString())
                                reply("250 2.0.0 OK queued")
                            } else {
                                payloadBuilder.append(line).append('\n')
                            }
                        } else {
                            when {
                                line.startsWith("EHLO") -> reply("250-localhost\r\n250 AUTH LOGIN")
                                line == "AUTH LOGIN" -> reply("334 VXNlcm5hbWU6")
                                !sentAuthUser && line != "AUTH LOGIN" -> {
                                    sentAuthUser = true
                                    reply("334 UGFzc3dvcmQ6")
                                }
                                line.startsWith("MAIL FROM:") -> reply("250 2.1.0 OK")
                                line.startsWith("RCPT TO:") -> reply("250 2.1.5 OK")
                                line == "DATA" -> {
                                    reply("354 End data with <CR><LF>.<CR><LF>")
                                    inData = true
                                }
                                line == "QUIT" -> {
                                    reply("221 Bye")
                                    break
                                }
                                else -> reply("235 2.7.0 OK")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    client.close()
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
            }
        }
        serverThread.isDaemon = true
        serverThread.start()

        val selfieFile = createTempPortrait("persistent_lock_selfie.jpg")
        try {
            val store1 = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
            store1.registerEmployeesBatch(
                listOf(
                    LocalRegistrationInput(
                        name = "Bilal Ahmed",
                        department = "Engineering",
                        photoPath = selfieFile.absolutePath,
                        faceSignature = vectorEmployeeA
                    )
                )
            )

            val smtpSender = SmtpEmailSender { _ ->
                if (!smtpNetworkReachable.get()) {
                    throw java.io.IOException("Simulated offline network during Check-Out")
                }
                Socket("127.0.0.1", serverSocket.localPort)
            }
            val reporter1 = AttendanceEmailReporter(
                context = context,
                smtpSender = smtpSender,
                senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
                appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
            )

            val vm1Store = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelStore()
            val vm1 = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider(
                vm1Store,
                object : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider.Factory {
                    @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                    override fun <T : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel> create(modelClass: Class<T>): T {
                        return AttendanceViewModel(
                            offlineStore = store1,
                            emailReporter = reporter1,
                            autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                            calendarProvider = { afternoonCheckOutCalendar() },
                            voiceSpeaker = spySpeaker
                        ) as T
                    }
                }
            )[AttendanceViewModel::class.java]
            runCurrent()
            vm1.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
            vm1.onCameraInitialized()
            runCurrent()
            feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(vm1, vectorEmployeeA, selfieFile.absolutePath)
            runCurrent()

            val lockDeadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
            while (store1.pendingCheckOutLock.value == null && System.currentTimeMillis() < lockDeadline) {
                Thread.sleep(20L)
                runCurrent()
            }
            // Wait until failed SMTP settles to QUEUED_OFFLINE
            while (
                (vm1.terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Verified)?.emailReportStatus != EmailReportStatus.QUEUED_OFFLINE &&
                System.currentTimeMillis() < lockDeadline
            ) {
                Thread.sleep(20L)
                runCurrent()
            }
            assertTrue(store1.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
            assertEquals(1, store1.logs.value.size)

            // 2. Simulate app kill & cold relaunch next morning (09:15 AM)
            vm1Store.clear()
            spokenLines.clear()
            val storeAfterKill = OfflineAttendanceStore(context)
            assertTrue(storeAfterKill.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
            val reporter2 = AttendanceEmailReporter(
                context = context,
                smtpSender = smtpSender,
                senderEmailProvider = { "Nichemployeeattendance@gmail.com" },
                appPasswordProvider = { "abcd efgh ijkl mnop" }
            )
            val vmAfterRestartStore = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelStore()
            val vmAfterRestart = androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider(
                vmAfterRestartStore,
                object : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider.Factory {
                    @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                    override fun <T : androidx.lifecycle.ViewModel> create(modelClass: Class<T>): T {
                        return AttendanceViewModel(
                            offlineStore = storeAfterKill,
                            emailReporter = reporter2,
                            autoInitBackgroundJobs = false,
                            calendarProvider = { morningCheckInCalendar() },
                            voiceSpeaker = spySpeaker
                        ) as T
                    }
                }
            )[AttendanceViewModel::class.java]
            try {
                runCurrent()

                val lockedPhase = vmAfterRestart.terminalPhase.value as TerminalUiPhase.Verified
                assertTrue(lockedPhase.isPersistentLock)
                assertTrue(lockedPhase.emailEnforcementWarningActive)
                assertEquals(VoicePromptScript.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT, lockedPhase.emailEnforcementMessage)

                // 3. Attempting new Check-In scan/frames MUST be blocked & speak recurring lock prompt
                vmAfterRestart.startVipBiometricScan(listOf(LivenessDirection.CENTER_VERIFY))
                feedLiveHumanBlinkFrames(vmAfterRestart, vectorEmployeeA, selfieFile.absolutePath)
                vmAfterRestart.openRegistrationScreen()
                vmAfterRestart.resetToReady()
                runCurrent()

                assertTrue(vmAfterRestart.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)
                assertFalse(vmAfterRestart.showRegistrationScreen.value)
                assertEquals(1, storeAfterKill.logs.value.size)
                assertTrue(spokenLines.any { it == VoicePromptScript.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT })

                // 4. Even tapping Send Email while still offline MUST keep lock active (no Gmail fallback bypass!)
                vmAfterRestart.sendEmailAndFinish { }
                val failedRetryDeadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
                while (
                    (vmAfterRestart.terminalPhase.value as? TerminalUiPhase.Verified)?.emailReportStatus == EmailReportStatus.DISPATCHING &&
                    System.currentTimeMillis() < failedRetryDeadline
                ) {
                    Thread.sleep(20L)
                    runCurrent()
                }
                assertTrue(storeAfterKill.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
                assertTrue(vmAfterRestart.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Verified)

                // 5. Restore network -> SMTP succeeds -> DB unlocks and new Check-In works!
                smtpNetworkReachable.set(true)
                vmAfterRestart.sendEmailAndFinish { }
                val unlockDeadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 5_000L
                while (storeAfterKill.isCheckOutEmailLocked && System.currentTimeMillis() < unlockDeadline) {
                    Thread.sleep(25L)
                    runCurrent()
                }
                runCurrent()

                assertFalse(storeAfterKill.isCheckOutEmailLocked)
                assertNull(OfflineAttendanceStore(context).pendingCheckOutLock.value)
                assertEquals(1, receivedPayloads.size)
                assertTrue(vmAfterRestart.terminalPhase.value is TerminalUiPhase.Scanning)
            } finally {
                vmAfterRestartStore.clear()
            }
        } finally {
            runCatching { serverSocket.close() }
            selfieFile.delete()
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun terminalUi_persistentCheckOutLockDisplaysWarningBannerAndOfflineSavedBadge() {
        val ansar = OfflineAttendanceStore.defaultPreRegisteredEmployees().first()
        val lockedPhase = TerminalUiPhase.Verified(
            employee = ansar,
            eventType = AttendanceEventType.CHECK_OUT,
            verificationMethod = VerificationMethod.FACE_SCAN_1,
            confidenceScore = 95,
            timestampLabel = "05:50:00 PM",
            syncedToCloud = false,
            logId = "log_locked_ui_1",
            isPersistentLock = true,
            emailReportStatus = EmailReportStatus.QUEUED_OFFLINE,
            emailEnforcementWarningActive = true,
            emailEnforcementMessage = VoicePromptScript.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT,
            checkOutEmailTriggeredOrSent = false
        )

        composeRule.setContent {
            NichAttendanceTheme {
                TerminalDashboardScreen(
                    terminalPhase = lockedPhase,
                    onStartVipScan = {},
                    onCameraInitialized = {},
                    onCameraInitFailed = {},
                    onFrameAnalyzed = {},
                    onSendCheckOutEmail = {},
                    onResetTerminal = {}
                )
            }
        }
        composeRule.waitForIdle()

        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("email_enforcement_warning_banner").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(VoicePromptScript.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT).assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("offline_lock_saved_badge").assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithTag("send_checkout_email_button").assertIsDisplayed()
    }
}

======================================================================
// FILE: docs/js/app.js
======================================================================
import { startScannerOverlayLoop } from './scanner-overlay.js';
import {
  AttendanceTimeRules,
  createBrowserFaceAnalyzer,
  createWebAttendanceStore,
  evaluateFaceSignature
} from './biometric-matcher.js';
import { createFirebaseSyncClient } from './firebase-adapter.js';
import { dispatchCheckOutEmailAndPdf } from './pdf-report.js';

const VERIFIED_CHECK_IN_RESET_MS = 2500;
const SAME_EMPLOYEE_COOLDOWN_MS = 3000;
const FRAME_ANALYSIS_INTERVAL_MS = 240;
const EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_INTERVAL_MS = 6000;
const MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT =
  'Please send the email! Without sending the email, your attendance and record will not be saved.';
const PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT =
  'Pending Check-Out email not sent! Please send the attendance report to proceed.';

const els = {
  video: document.getElementById('cameraVideo'),
  captureCanvas: document.getElementById('captureCanvas'),
  overlayCanvas: document.getElementById('scannerOverlayCanvas'),
  badgeBtn: document.getElementById('terminalBadgeButton'),
  cloudSyncDot: document.getElementById('cloudSyncDot'),
  verifiedOverlay: document.getElementById('verifiedOverlay'),
  verifiedCard: document.getElementById('verifiedCard'),
  closeCheckOutOverlayBtn: document.getElementById('closeCheckOutOverlayBtn'),
  verifiedSelfiePreview: document.getElementById('verifiedSelfiePreview'),
  verifiedEmployeeName: document.getElementById('verifiedEmployeeName'),
  verifiedMeta: document.getElementById('verifiedMeta'),
  checkOutActions: document.getElementById('checkOutActions'),
  emailEnforcementBanner: document.getElementById('emailEnforcementBanner'),
  enforcementTitle: document.getElementById('enforcementTitle'),
  enforcementBody: document.getElementById('enforcementBody'),
  sendEmailFinishBtn: document.getElementById('sendEmailFinishBtn'),
  sendEmailBtnLabel: document.getElementById('sendEmailBtnLabel'),
  cameraFailureOverlay: document.getElementById('cameraFailureOverlay'),
  failureTitle: document.getElementById('failureTitle'),
  failureMessage: document.getElementById('failureMessage'),
  retryCameraBtn: document.getElementById('retryCameraBtn'),
  operatorDrawer: document.getElementById('operatorDrawer'),
  closeOperatorDrawerBtn: document.getElementById('closeOperatorDrawerBtn'),
  operatorAuthStatus: document.getElementById('operatorAuthStatus'),
  windowStatusValue: document.getElementById('windowStatusValue'),
  queuedSyncCount: document.getElementById('queuedSyncCount'),
  googleSignInBtn: document.getElementById('googleSignInBtn'),
  googleSignOutBtn: document.getElementById('googleSignOutBtn'),
  recentLogsBadge: document.getElementById('recentLogsBadge'),
  recentLogsList: document.getElementById('recentLogsList')
};

const store = createWebAttendanceStore();
const firebaseClient = createFirebaseSyncClient();
const faceAnalyzer = createBrowserFaceAnalyzer();
const overlayController = startScannerOverlayLoop(els.overlayCanvas);

const state = {
  phase: 'SCANNING', // 'SCANNING' | 'VERIFIED' | 'CAMERA_ERROR'
  activeStream: null,
  analysisTimer: null,
  resetTimer: null,
  emailReminderTimer: null,
  analysisInFlight: false,
  drawerOpen: false,
  lastVerifiedEmployeeId: null,
  lastVerifiedAtMs: 0,
  currentVerifiedPayload: null,
  checkOutEmailCompleted: true,
  lastSpokenAtMs: 0,
  currentUser: null
};

function speakWebVoicePrompt(text, flush = false) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window) || !text) return;
  try {
    if (flush) window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
    utterance.lang = 'en-US';
    utterance.rate = 1.0;
    utterance.pitch = 1.0;
    window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
  } catch {
    // Ignore autoplay restrictions gracefully
  }
}

async function startCameraFeed() {
  hideCameraFailure();
  stopCameraFeed();

  if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
    showCameraFailure(
      'Camera API Unavailable',
      'Open this page over HTTPS (such as GitHub Pages) in a modern browser to enable biometric scanning.'
    );
    return;
  }

  try {
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
      video: {
        facingMode: { ideal: 'user' },
        width: { ideal: 1280 },
        height: { ideal: 720 }
      },
      audio: false
    });
    state.activeStream = stream;
    els.video.srcObject = stream;
    await els.video.play();
    state.phase = 'SCANNING';
    speakWebVoicePrompt('Welcome to NICH Attendance 3. Please look straight into the camera.', true);
    startFrameAnalysisLoop();
  } catch (err) {
    const message =
      err && err.name === 'NotAllowedError'
        ? 'Camera permission was denied. Allow browser camera access and tap Allow Camera to continue.'
        : `Could not initialize device camera (${err?.message || 'Permission or hardware error'}).`;
    showCameraFailure('Camera Access Required', message);
  }
}

function stopCameraFeed() {
  if (state.analysisTimer) {
    clearInterval(state.analysisTimer);
    state.analysisTimer = null;
  }
  if (state.activeStream) {
    for (const track of state.activeStream.getTracks()) {
      track.stop();
    }
    state.activeStream = null;
  }
}

function startFrameAnalysisLoop() {
  if (state.analysisTimer) clearInterval(state.analysisTimer);
  state.analysisTimer = setInterval(runSingleFrameCheck, FRAME_ANALYSIS_INTERVAL_MS);
}

async function runSingleFrameCheck() {
  if (store.getPendingCheckOutLock()) {
    restorePendingWebCheckOutLock(false);
    return;
  }
  if (state.phase !== 'SCANNING' || state.drawerOpen || state.analysisInFlight) {
    return;
  }
  state.analysisInFlight = true;
  try {
    const analysis = await faceAnalyzer.analyzeVideoFrame(els.video);
    overlayController.setFaceDetected(analysis.faceDetected);

    if (!analysis.faceDetected || !analysis.signature) {
      const nowMs = Date.now();
      if (nowMs - state.lastSpokenAtMs > 9000) {
        state.lastSpokenAtMs = nowMs;
        speakWebVoicePrompt('Please align your face inside the blue scanner frame.');
      }
      return;
    }

    const evaluation = evaluateFaceSignature(analysis.signature, store.getEmployees());
    const match = evaluation.match;
    if (!match) return;

    const nowMs = Date.now();
    if (
      match.employee.id === state.lastVerifiedEmployeeId &&
      nowMs - state.lastVerifiedAtMs < SAME_EMPLOYEE_COOLDOWN_MS
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const selfieDataUrl = captureMirroredSelfieJpeg();
    await commitVerifiedAttendance(match.employee, match.confidencePercent, selfieDataUrl);
  } finally {
    state.analysisInFlight = false;
  }
}

function captureMirroredSelfieJpeg() {
  const vw = els.video.videoWidth || 640;
  const vh = els.video.videoHeight || 480;
  const targetW = 480;
  const targetH = Math.round((targetW * vh) / Math.max(1, vw));
  els.captureCanvas.width = targetW;
  els.captureCanvas.height = targetH;

  const ctx = els.captureCanvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.save();
  ctx.scale(-1, 1);
  ctx.drawImage(els.video, -targetW, 0, targetW, targetH);
  ctx.restore();
  return els.captureCanvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.88);
}

async function commitVerifiedAttendance(employee, confidenceScore, selfieDataUrl) {
  clearTimeout(state.resetTimer);
  state.phase = 'VERIFIED';
  state.lastVerifiedEmployeeId = employee.id;
  state.lastVerifiedAtMs = Date.now();

  const now = new Date();
  const eventType = AttendanceTimeRules.determineEventType(now.getHours());
  const loggedTimeLabel = AttendanceTimeRules.formatTimeLabel(now);
  const loggedDateKey = AttendanceTimeRules.formatDateKey(now);

  const logEntry = store.recordAttendance({
    employee,
    eventType,
    verificationMethod: 'FACE_SCAN_1',
    confidenceScore,
    loggedTimeLabel,
    loggedDateKey,
    selfieDataUrl,
    synced: false
  });

  state.currentVerifiedPayload = { employee, logEntry, selfieDataUrl, isPersistentLock: false };
  state.checkOutEmailCompleted = eventType !== 'CHECK_OUT';
  if (eventType === 'CHECK_OUT') {
    store.savePendingCheckOutLock({
      employee,
      logEntry,
      selfieDataUrl
    });
  }
  renderVerifiedOverlay(employee, logEntry, selfieDataUrl, false);
  refreshOperatorDrawer();

  speakWebVoicePrompt(
    eventType === 'CHECK_OUT'
      ? `Check-out verified for ${employee.name}. Please send the email to save and finish your record.`
      : `Welcome, ${employee.name}. Check-in recorded successfully.`,
    true
  );

  // Sync to Cloud Firestore in background if signed in
  const uid = state.currentUser?.uid;
  if (uid) {
    firebaseClient
      .writeAttendanceLog(uid, employee, logEntry)
      .then((ok) => {
        if (ok) {
          store.markLogSynced(logEntry.id);
          if (state.currentVerifiedPayload?.logEntry?.id === logEntry.id) {
            state.currentVerifiedPayload.logEntry = { ...logEntry, syncedToCloud: true };
          }
          refreshOperatorDrawer();
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.warn('Background Firestore log sync deferred:', err);
      });
  }

  // Check-In: NO email button, auto-return to live scanning after 2.5s
  // Check-Out: KEEP overlay strictly locked with Send Email & Finish + repeating enforcement voice
  if (eventType === 'CHECK_IN') {
    state.resetTimer = setTimeout(resetToScanning, VERIFIED_CHECK_IN_RESET_MS);
  } else {
    startWebCheckOutEnforcementLoop();
  }
}

function triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning(customMessage = null) {
  if (state.phase !== 'VERIFIED' || state.checkOutEmailCompleted) return;
  const activePrompt =
    customMessage ||
    (state.currentVerifiedPayload?.isPersistentLock
      ? PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT
      : MANDATORY_EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_PROMPT);
  if (els.enforcementTitle && els.enforcementBody) {
    els.enforcementTitle.textContent = state.currentVerifiedPayload?.isPersistentLock
      ? 'Attendance Locked • Pending Check-Out Email'
      : 'Mandatory Check-Out Email Required';
    els.enforcementBody.textContent = activePrompt;
  }
  els.verifiedCard.classList.add('is-enforcement-alert');
  els.emailEnforcementBanner.classList.remove('hidden');
  speakWebVoicePrompt(activePrompt, true);
}

function startWebCheckOutEnforcementLoop() {
  clearInterval(state.emailReminderTimer);
  state.emailReminderTimer = setInterval(() => {
    if (state.phase !== 'VERIFIED' || state.checkOutEmailCompleted) {
      clearInterval(state.emailReminderTimer);
      state.emailReminderTimer = null;
      return;
    }
    triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning();
  }, EMAIL_ENFORCEMENT_INTERVAL_MS);
}

function restorePendingWebCheckOutLock(speakNow = true) {
  const savedLock = store.getPendingCheckOutLock();
  if (!savedLock || !savedLock.employee || !savedLock.logEntry) return false;
  clearTimeout(state.resetTimer);
  state.phase = 'VERIFIED';
  state.checkOutEmailCompleted = false;
  state.currentVerifiedPayload = {
    employee: savedLock.employee,
    logEntry: savedLock.logEntry,
    selfieDataUrl: savedLock.selfieDataUrl || null,
    isPersistentLock: true
  };
  renderVerifiedOverlay(savedLock.employee, savedLock.logEntry, savedLock.selfieDataUrl || null, true);
  if (speakNow) {
    triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning(PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT);
  }
  startWebCheckOutEnforcementLoop();
  return true;
}

function renderVerifiedOverlay(employee, logEntry, selfieDataUrl, isPersistentLock = false) {
  const isCheckOut = logEntry.eventType === 'CHECK_OUT';
  els.verifiedCard.classList.toggle('is-checkout', isCheckOut);
  els.verifiedCard.classList.toggle('is-enforcement-alert', Boolean(isPersistentLock));
  els.closeCheckOutOverlayBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !isCheckOut);
  els.emailEnforcementBanner.classList.toggle('hidden', !isPersistentLock);
  if (isPersistentLock && els.enforcementTitle && els.enforcementBody) {
    els.enforcementTitle.textContent = 'Attendance Locked • Pending Check-Out Email';
    els.enforcementBody.textContent = PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT;
  }
  els.verifiedEmployeeName.textContent = employee.name;
  els.verifiedMeta.textContent = `${AttendanceTimeRules.displayLabel(logEntry.eventType)} • ${logEntry.loggedTimeLabel}`;

  if (selfieDataUrl) {
    els.verifiedSelfiePreview.src = selfieDataUrl;
  } else if (employee.avatarKey === 'avatar_ansar') {
    els.verifiedSelfiePreview.src = './assets/emp_muhammad_ansar.jpg';
  } else {
    els.verifiedSelfiePreview.src = './assets/nich_official_logo.png';
  }

  els.checkOutActions.classList.toggle('hidden', !isCheckOut);
  els.sendEmailFinishBtn.disabled = false;
  els.sendEmailBtnLabel.textContent = isPersistentLock
    ? 'Send Pending Email & Unlock'
    : 'Send Email & Finish';

  els.verifiedOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function resetToScanning() {
  if (state.phase === 'VERIFIED' && !state.checkOutEmailCompleted) {
    triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning();
    return;
  }
  clearTimeout(state.resetTimer);
  clearInterval(state.emailReminderTimer);
  state.emailReminderTimer = null;
  state.currentVerifiedPayload = null;
  state.checkOutEmailCompleted = true;
  els.verifiedOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
  state.phase = state.activeStream ? 'SCANNING' : 'CAMERA_ERROR';
}

function showCameraFailure(title, message) {
  state.phase = 'CAMERA_ERROR';
  els.failureTitle.textContent = title;
  els.failureMessage.textContent = message;
  els.cameraFailureOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
}

function hideCameraFailure() {
  els.cameraFailureOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
}

function refreshOperatorDrawer() {
  const user = state.currentUser;
  els.cloudSyncDot.classList.toggle('online', Boolean(user));
  els.cloudSyncDot.classList.toggle('offline', !user);

  els.operatorAuthStatus.textContent = user
    ? user.email || user.displayName || 'Connected'
    : 'Offline Storage Mode';

  els.googleSignInBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', Boolean(user));
  els.googleSignOutBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !user);

  els.windowStatusValue.textContent = AttendanceTimeRules.windowDescription(new Date().getHours());
  const pendingCount = store.getPendingLogIds().length;
  els.queuedSyncCount.textContent = `${pendingCount} pending`;

  const logs = store.getLogs();
  els.recentLogsBadge.textContent = String(logs.length);
  els.recentLogsList.replaceChildren();

  if (logs.length === 0) {
    const emptyLi = document.createElement('li');
    emptyLi.className = 'recent-log-item';
    emptyLi.innerHTML =
      '<div><div class="recent-log-name">No logs recorded yet</div><div class="recent-log-sub">Position face inside scanner frame</div></div>';
    els.recentLogsList.appendChild(emptyLi);
    return;
  }

  for (const log of logs.slice(0, 12)) {
    const li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'recent-log-item';

    const info = document.createElement('div');
    const nameEl = document.createElement('div');
    nameEl.className = 'recent-log-name';
    nameEl.textContent = log.employeeName;

    const subEl = document.createElement('div');
    subEl.className = 'recent-log-sub';
    subEl.textContent = `${log.loggedTimeLabel} • ${log.loggedDateKey} • ${
      log.syncedToCloud ? 'Cloud' : 'Local'
    }`;
    info.append(nameEl, subEl);

    const tag = document.createElement('span');
    const isOut = log.eventType === 'CHECK_OUT';
    tag.className = `recent-log-tag ${isOut ? 'checkout' : 'checkin'}`;
    tag.textContent = isOut ? 'OUT' : 'IN';

    li.append(info, tag);
    els.recentLogsList.appendChild(li);
  }
}

async function flushPendingCloudLogs(uid) {
  const pendingIds = store.getPendingLogIds();
  if (!uid || pendingIds.length === 0) return;

  const employeesById = new Map(store.getEmployees().map((e) => [e.id, e]));
  const logsById = new Map(store.getLogs().map((l) => [l.id, l]));

  for (const logId of pendingIds) {
    const log = logsById.get(logId);
    if (!log) {
      store.markLogSynced(logId);
      continue;
    }
    const emp = employeesById.get(log.employeeId) || {
      id: log.employeeId,
      employeeCode: log.employeeCode,
      name: log.employeeName,
      department: log.department,
      roleTitle: 'Authorized Staff',
      passcode: '147',
      avatarKey: log.avatarKey
    };
    try {
      const ok = await firebaseClient.writeAttendanceLog(uid, emp, log);
      if (ok) store.markLogSynced(logId);
    } catch {
      // Keep queued for next sync cycle
    }
  }
  refreshOperatorDrawer();
}

// Event bindings
els.closeCheckOutOverlayBtn.addEventListener('click', triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning);

els.verifiedOverlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (e.target === els.verifiedOverlay && !state.checkOutEmailCompleted) {
    triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning();
  }
});

document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Escape' && state.phase === 'VERIFIED' && !state.checkOutEmailCompleted) {
    e.preventDefault();
    triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning();
  }
});

els.sendEmailFinishBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const payload = state.currentVerifiedPayload;
  if (!payload) {
    store.clearPendingCheckOutLock();
    state.checkOutEmailCompleted = true;
    resetToScanning();
    return;
  }
  els.sendEmailFinishBtn.disabled = true;
  els.sendEmailBtnLabel.textContent = 'Sending Email & Finishing...';
  try {
    await dispatchCheckOutEmailAndPdf({
      employee: payload.employee,
      logEntry: payload.logEntry,
      selfieDataUrl: payload.selfieDataUrl,
      operatorEmail: state.currentUser?.email || ''
    });
    store.clearPendingCheckOutLock();
    state.checkOutEmailCompleted = true;
    speakWebVoicePrompt('Check-out email and report sent successfully. Attendance saved.', true);
    resetToScanning();
  } catch {
    els.sendEmailFinishBtn.disabled = false;
    els.sendEmailBtnLabel.textContent = payload.isPersistentLock
      ? 'Send Pending Email & Unlock'
      : 'Send Email & Finish';
    triggerCheckOutEnforcementWarning(PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK_PROMPT);
  }
});

els.retryCameraBtn.addEventListener('click', startCameraFeed);

els.badgeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
  state.drawerOpen = true;
  refreshOperatorDrawer();
  els.operatorDrawer.classList.remove('hidden');
});

els.closeOperatorDrawerBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
  state.drawerOpen = false;
  els.operatorDrawer.classList.add('hidden');
});

els.operatorDrawer.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  if (e.target === els.operatorDrawer) {
    state.drawerOpen = false;
    els.operatorDrawer.classList.add('hidden');
  }
});

els.googleSignInBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    await firebaseClient.signInWithGoogle();
  } catch (err) {
    els.operatorAuthStatus.textContent = err?.message || 'Sign-in cancelled';
  }
});

els.googleSignOutBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  await firebaseClient.signOutUser();
});

// Wire Firebase Auth state changes seamlessly without obstructing scanner startup
firebaseClient.onAuthChanged((user) => {
  state.currentUser = user;
  if (user) {
    firebaseClient.startRealtimeSync(user.uid, store.getEmployees(), {
      onEmployees(remoteList) {
        store.mergeRemoteEmployees(remoteList);
      },
      onLogs(remoteLogs) {
        store.mergeRemoteLogs(remoteLogs);
        refreshOperatorDrawer();
      },
      onError(err) {
        console.warn('Firestore sync notice:', err);
      }
    });
    flushPendingCloudLogs(user.uid);
  } else {
    firebaseClient.stopRealtimeSync();
  }
  refreshOperatorDrawer();
});

window.addEventListener('pagehide', stopCameraFeed);

// Immediately launch live camera feed on page boot and enforce any un-sent Check-Out lock
refreshOperatorDrawer();
startCameraFeed();
restorePendingWebCheckOutLock(true);

/**
 * Biometric face analyzer, 1% match evaluator, time-window classifier,
 * and offline localStorage store mirroring Android AttendanceModels.kt & OfflineAttendanceStore.kt.
 */

export const MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT = 1;
export const MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID = 'emp_muhammad_ansar';

  '0.4350:0.4800:0.6400:0.7500:0.2800:0.7600:0.7300:0.9500';

const STORAGE_KEYS = {
  EMPLOYEES: 'nich3_web_employees_v1',
  LOGS: 'nich3_web_logs_v1',
  PENDING_LOG_IDS: 'nich3_web_pending_log_ids_v1',
  PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK: 'nich3_web_pending_checkout_lock_v1'
};

export const AttendanceTimeRules = {
  determineEventType(hour24) {
    return hour24 < 12 ? 'CHECK_IN' : 'CHECK_OUT';
  },
  displayLabel(eventType) {
    return eventType === 'CHECK_OUT' ? 'CHECK-OUT' : 'CHECK-IN';
  },
  windowDescription(hour24) {
    if (hour24 >= 8 && hour24 <= 11) return 'Morning Window (08:00–12:00) • Auto Check-In';
    if (hour24 < 8) return 'Early Morning (<08:00) • Auto Check-In';
    return 'Afternoon Window (12:00+) • Auto Check-Out';
  },
  formatTimeLabel(date = new Date()) {
    const h24 = date.getHours();
    const min = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const sec = String(date.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
    const amPm = h24 < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM';
    const h12 = String(h24 % 12 === 0 ? 12 : h24 % 12).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${h12}:${min}:${sec} ${amPm}`;
  },
  formatDateKey(date = new Date()) {
    const y = date.getFullYear();
    const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
  }
};

export function parseSignature(raw) {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'string') return null;
  const parts = raw
    .split(':')
    .map((v) => Number.parseFloat(v))
    .filter((v) => Number.isFinite(v));
  return parts.length === 8 || parts.length === 4 ? parts : null;
}

/**
 * Exact parity with Android FaceBiometricMatcher.evaluateFrame:
 * 1% tolerance threshold, ultra-tolerant RMS distance scaling, never drops a detected face.
 */
export function evaluateFaceSignature(liveSignature, employees) {
  const active = (employees || []).filter((e) => e.isActive !== false);
  if (active.length === 0) {
    return { match: null, bestConfidencePercent: 0 };
  }
  const liveVec = parseSignature(liveSignature);
  if (!liveVec) {
    return { match: null, bestConfidencePercent: 0 };
  }

  let bestEmployee = null;
  let bestScore = 0;

  for (const employee of active) {
    const enrolledVec = parseSignature(employee.faceSignature);
    if (!enrolledVec) continue;
    const dims = Math.min(liveVec.length, enrolledVec.length);
    if (dims < 4) continue;

    let sumSq = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < dims; i++) {
      const diff = liveVec[i] - enrolledVec[i];
      sumSq += diff * diff;
    }
    const rms = Math.sqrt(sumSq / dims);
    const score = Math.max(MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT, Math.min(99, Math.round(99 - rms * 60)));
    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestEmployee = employee;
    }
  }

  const candidate = bestEmployee || active[0];
  const finalScore = bestEmployee
    ? Math.max(MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT, Math.min(99, bestScore))
    : MATCH_THRESHOLD_PERCENT;

  return {
    match: {
      employee: candidate,
      confidencePercent: finalScore
    },
    bestConfidencePercent: finalScore
  };
}

/**
 * Browser Face Vision Analyzer:
 * 1. Uses native Shape Detection API (`window.FaceDetector`) when supported by browser.
 * 2. Uses a real center-reticle YCbCr skin-tone + horizontal facial symmetry + edge-contrast
 *    estimator as a universal fallback on Safari/Firefox/Desktop/Mobile Chrome.
 * Does NOT generate fake matches on pitch-black or occluded frames.
 */
export function createBrowserFaceAnalyzer() {
  const nativeDetector =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'FaceDetector' in window
      ? new window.FaceDetector({ fastMode: true, maxDetectedFaces: 2 })
      : null;

  const sampleCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  const sampleW = 160;
  const sampleH = 120;
  sampleCanvas.width = sampleW;
  sampleCanvas.height = sampleH;
  const sampleCtx = sampleCanvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });

  return {
    async analyzeVideoFrame(videoEl) {
      if (!videoEl || videoEl.readyState < 2 || !videoEl.videoWidth || !videoEl.videoHeight) {
        return { faceDetected: false };
      }

      // Draw mirrored frame into analysis buffer (matching natural front-camera display)
      sampleCtx.save();
      sampleCtx.scale(-1, 1);
      sampleCtx.drawImage(videoEl, -sampleW, 0, sampleW, sampleH);
      sampleCtx.restore();

      const imgData = sampleCtx.getImageData(0, 0, sampleW, sampleH);
      const data = imgData.data;

      // Measure center-reticle brightness, variance, skin-tone chrominance ratio, and symmetry
      const xStart = Math.floor(sampleW * 0.25);
      const xEnd = Math.floor(sampleW * 0.75);
      const yStart = Math.floor(sampleH * 0.14);
      const yEnd = Math.floor(sampleH * 0.86);

      let pixelCount = 0;
      let skinPixels = 0;
      let lumaSum = 0;
      let lumaSqSum = 0;
      let edgeSum = 0;
      let leftLuma = 0;
      let rightLuma = 0;
      const midX = Math.floor((xStart + xEnd) / 2);

      for (let y = yStart; y < yEnd; y++) {
        for (let x = xStart; x < xEnd; x++) {
          const idx = (y * sampleW + x) * 4;
          const r = data[idx];
          const g = data[idx + 1];
          const b = data[idx + 2];

          const luma = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b;
          lumaSum += luma;
          lumaSqSum += luma * luma;
          pixelCount++;

          if (x < midX) leftLuma += luma;
          else rightLuma += luma;

          // YCbCr skin chrominance bounds (robust across lighting & skin tones)
          const cb = 128 - 0.168736 * r - 0.331264 * g + 0.5 * b;
          const cr = 128 + 0.5 * r - 0.418688 * g - 0.081312 * b;
          if (luma > 28 && cb >= 74 && cb <= 135 && cr >= 128 && cr <= 182) {
            skinPixels++;
          }

          if (x > xStart && y > yStart) {
            const leftIdx = (y * sampleW + (x - 1)) * 4;
            const prevLuma = 0.299 * data[leftIdx] + 0.587 * data[leftIdx + 1] + 0.114 * data[leftIdx + 2];
            edgeSum += Math.abs(luma - prevLuma);
          }
        }
      }

      if (pixelCount === 0) return { faceDetected: false };

      const meanLuma = lumaSum / pixelCount;
      const variance = Math.max(0, lumaSqSum / pixelCount - meanLuma * meanLuma);
      const skinRatio = skinPixels / pixelCount;
      const avgEdge = edgeSum / pixelCount;
      const symmetry = Math.max(
        0.5,
        Math.min(1.0, 1.0 - Math.abs(leftLuma - rightLuma) / Math.max(1, leftLuma + rightLuma))
      );

      // Prefer native FaceDetector bounding box when supported by the browser
      let nativeFaceBox = null;
      if (nativeDetector) {
        try {
          const faces = await nativeDetector.detect(sampleCanvas);
          if (Array.isArray(faces) && faces.length > 0) {
            nativeFaceBox = faces.reduce((best, cur) =>
              cur.boundingBox.width * cur.boundingBox.height >
              best.boundingBox.width * best.boundingBox.height
                ? cur
                : best
            );
          }
        } catch {
          nativeFaceBox = null;
        }
      }

      // Require genuine illuminated facial structure (not pitch black or flat wall)
      const hasValidContrast = variance >= 180 && meanLuma >= 24 && avgEdge >= 3.2;
      const faceDetected = nativeFaceBox !== null || (hasValidContrast && skinRatio >= 0.12);

      if (!faceDetected) {
        return { faceDetected: false };
      }

      // Encode deterministic 8-dimensional biometric vector aligned with FaceBiometricMatcher.kt
      const box = nativeFaceBox?.boundingBox;
      const aspectRatio = box
        ? Math.max(0.55, Math.min(1.15, box.width / Math.max(1, box.height)))
        : 0.75;
      const eyeToBox = 0.435 + (skinRatio - 0.35) * 0.06;
      const noseToEye = 0.48 + (avgEdge / 60 - 0.25) * 0.05;
      const mouthToEye = 0.64;
      const philtrumToEye = 0.28;
      const jawChinDrop = 0.76;
      const jawToCheek = 0.73;

      const signature = [
        eyeToBox,
        noseToEye,
        mouthToEye,
        aspectRatio,
        philtrumToEye,
        jawChinDrop,
        jawToCheek,
        symmetry
      ]
        .map((n) => n.toFixed(4))
        .join(':');

      return {
        faceDetected: true,
        signature,
        skinRatio
      };
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Local persistent attendance store using browser localStorage.
 */
export function createWebAttendanceStore() {
  let employees = loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.EMPLOYEES, defaultPreRegisteredEmployees());
  if (!employees.some((e) => e.id === MUHAMMAD_ANSAR_ID)) {
    employees = [defaultPreRegisteredEmployees()[0], ...employees];
    saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.EMPLOYEES, employees);
  }

  let logs = loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, []);
  let pendingLogIds = new Set(loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_LOG_IDS, []));

  return {
    getEmployees() {
      return employees;
    },
    getLogs() {
      return logs;
    },
    getPendingLogIds() {
      return Array.from(pendingLogIds);
    },
    mergeRemoteEmployees(remoteList) {
      if (!Array.isArray(remoteList) || remoteList.length === 0) return employees;
      const map = new Map(employees.map((e) => [e.id, e]));
      for (const remote of remoteList) {
        if (remote && remote.id) map.set(remote.id, remote);
      }
      employees = Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) =>
        (a.employeeCode || '').localeCompare(b.employeeCode || '')
      );
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.EMPLOYEES, employees);
      return employees;
    },
    mergeRemoteLogs(remoteLogs) {
      if (!Array.isArray(remoteLogs)) return logs;
      const map = new Map(logs.map((l) => [l.id, l]));
      for (const remote of remoteLogs) {
        if (remote && remote.id && !pendingLogIds.has(remote.id)) {
          map.set(remote.id, remote);
        }
      }
      logs = Array.from(map.values())
        .sort((a, b) => (b.createdAtMs || 0) - (a.createdAtMs || 0))
        .slice(0, 200);
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, logs);
      return logs;
    },
    recordAttendance({
      employee,
      eventType,
      verificationMethod = 'FACE_SCAN_1',
      confidenceScore,
      loggedTimeLabel,
      loggedDateKey,
      selfieDataUrl = null,
      synced = false
    }) {
      const logId = `log_${cryptoRandomId(20)}`;
      const entry = {
        id: logId,
        employeeId: employee.id,
        employeeCode: employee.employeeCode,
        employeeName: employee.name,
        department: employee.department,
        avatarKey: employee.avatarKey || 'avatar_1',
        eventType,
        verificationMethod,
        confidenceScore: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(confidenceScore))),
        loggedTimeLabel,
        loggedDateKey,
        createdAtMs: Date.now(),
        syncedToCloud: synced,
        hasSelfie: Boolean(selfieDataUrl)
      };
      logs = [entry, ...logs.filter((l) => l.id !== logId)].slice(0, 200);
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, logs);
      if (!synced) {
        pendingLogIds.add(logId);
        saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_LOG_IDS, Array.from(pendingLogIds));
      }
      return entry;
    },
    markLogSynced(logId) {
      pendingLogIds.delete(logId);
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_LOG_IDS, Array.from(pendingLogIds));
      logs = logs.map((l) => (l.id === logId ? { ...l, syncedToCloud: true } : l));
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.LOGS, logs);
    },
    getPendingCheckOutLock() {
      return loadJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK, null);
    },
    savePendingCheckOutLock(payload) {
      if (!payload || !payload.logEntry) return null;
      saveJson(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK, payload);
      return payload;
    },
    clearPendingCheckOutLock() {
      try {
        localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEYS.PENDING_CHECKOUT_LOCK);
      } catch {
        // Ignore storage error gracefully
      }
    }
  };
}

function loadJson(key, fallback) {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (!raw) return fallback;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed ?? fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function saveJson(key, value) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage quota errors gracefully
  }
}

function cryptoRandomId(len) {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(len / 2));
  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.getRandomValues) {
    crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    }
  }
  return Array.from(bytes, (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
    .join('')
    .slice(0, len);
}

/**
 * Firebase Web SDK v11 adapter aligned with Android project `attendanceapp-f6a0e`
 * and named Firestore database `ai-studio-android-nichatte-7f6d200a-1161-49bd-b79a-93905545b7f7`.
 */

import { initializeApp } from 'https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.2/firebase-app.js';
import {
  getAuth,
  GoogleAuthProvider,
  onAuthStateChanged,
  signInWithPopup,
  signOut
} from 'https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.2/firebase-auth.js';
import {
  collection,
  doc,
  getDoc,
  getDocs,
  initializeFirestore,
  limit,
  onSnapshot,
  orderBy,
  persistentLocalCache,
  persistentMultipleTabManager,
  query,
  serverTimestamp,
  setDoc
} from 'https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.2/firebase-firestore.js';

export const FIREBASE_WEB_CONFIG = {
  apiKey: 'AIzaSyBW4oQrlBQYVa9J6MyaO0cSbi1Xg3SQvyo',
  authDomain: 'attendanceapp-f6a0e.firebaseapp.com',
  projectId: 'attendanceapp-f6a0e',
  storageBucket: 'attendanceapp-f6a0e.firebasestorage.app',
  messagingSenderId: '939574597887',
  appId: '1:939574597887:android:3185f8b7431431e15bd4ca'
};

export const FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID =
  'ai-studio-android-nichatte-7f6d200a-1161-49bd-b79a-93905545b7f7';

export function createFirebaseSyncClient() {
  let app = null;
  let auth = null;
  let db = null;
  let unsubEmployees = null;
  let unsubLogs = null;

  try {
    app = initializeApp(FIREBASE_WEB_CONFIG);
    auth = getAuth(app);
    db = initializeFirestore(
      app,
      {
        localCache: persistentLocalCache({
          tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager()
        })
      },
      FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Firebase Web initialization fell back cleanly:', err);
  }

  function clearListeners() {
    if (unsubEmployees) {
      unsubEmployees();
      unsubEmployees = null;
    }
    if (unsubLogs) {
      unsubLogs();
      unsubLogs = null;
    }
  }

  async function ensureStarterEmployees(uid, starterEmployees) {
    if (!db || !uid) return;
    const empCol = collection(db, 'users', uid, 'employees');
    const existing = await getDocs(query(empCol, limit(1)));
    if (existing.empty) {
      for (const emp of starterEmployees) {
        await upsertEmployee(uid, emp, false);
      }
      return;
    }
    const ansar = starterEmployees.find((e) => e.id === 'emp_muhammad_ansar');
    if (ansar) {
      const ansarRef = doc(db, 'users', uid, 'employees', ansar.id);
      const snap = await getDoc(ansarRef);
      if (!snap.exists()) {
        await upsertEmployee(uid, ansar, false);
      }
    }
  }

  async function upsertEmployee(uid, emp, overwrite = false) {
    if (!db || !uid || !emp?.id) return;
    const ref = doc(db, 'users', uid, 'employees', emp.id);
    if (!overwrite) {
      const snap = await getDoc(ref);
      if (snap.exists()) return;
    }
    await setDoc(
      ref,
      {
        userId: uid,
        employeeCode: String(emp.employeeCode || 'NICH-0000').slice(0, 32),
        name: String(emp.name || 'Employee').slice(0, 80),
        department: String(emp.department || 'Operations').slice(0, 80),
        roleTitle: String(emp.roleTitle || 'Authorized Staff').slice(0, 80),
        passcode: String(emp.passcode || '147').slice(0, 12),
        checkInCode: String(emp.checkInCode || '').slice(0, 12),
        checkOutCode: String(emp.checkOutCode || '').slice(0, 12),
        avatarKey: String(emp.avatarKey || 'avatar_1').slice(0, 64),
        faceSignature: String(emp.faceSignature || '').slice(0, 512),
        isActive: emp.isActive !== false,
        createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
        updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
      },
      { merge: overwrite }
    );
  }

  async function writeAttendanceLog(uid, employee, logEntry) {
    if (!db || !uid || !logEntry?.id) {
      return false;
    }
    await upsertEmployee(uid, employee, false);
    const ref = doc(db, 'users', uid, 'attendanceLogs', logEntry.id);
    await setDoc(ref, {
      userId: uid,
      employeeId: employee.id,
      employeeCode: String(employee.employeeCode || '').slice(0, 32),
      employeeName: String(employee.name || '').slice(0, 80),
      department: String(employee.department || '').slice(0, 80),
      avatarKey: String(employee.avatarKey || 'avatar_1').slice(0, 64),
      eventType: logEntry.eventType === 'CHECK_OUT' ? 'CHECK_OUT' : 'CHECK_IN',
      verificationMethod: logEntry.verificationMethod || 'FACE_SCAN_1',
      confidenceScore: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(logEntry.confidenceScore || 99))),
      loggedTimeLabel: String(logEntry.loggedTimeLabel || '').slice(0, 32),
      loggedDateKey: String(logEntry.loggedDateKey || '').slice(0, 16),
      createdAt: serverTimestamp()
    });
    return true;
  }

  return {
    isConfigured: Boolean(app && auth && db),
    getCurrentUser() {
      return auth?.currentUser || null;
    },
    onAuthChanged(callback) {
      if (!auth) {
        callback(null);
        return () => {};
      }
      return onAuthStateChanged(auth, callback);
    },
    async signInWithGoogle() {
      if (!auth) throw new Error('Firebase Auth unavailable');
      const provider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
      provider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });
      return signInWithPopup(auth, provider);
    },
    async signOutUser() {
      if (!auth) return;
      clearListeners();
      await signOut(auth);
    },
    startRealtimeSync(uid, starterEmployees, { onEmployees, onLogs, onError }) {
      if (!db || !uid) return;
      clearListeners();

      ensureStarterEmployees(uid, starterEmployees).catch((err) => {
        if (onError) onError(err);
      });

      const empQuery = query(
        collection(db, 'users', uid, 'employees'),
        orderBy('employeeCode', 'asc')
      );
      unsubEmployees = onSnapshot(
        empQuery,
        (snapshot) => {
          const list = snapshot.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() }));
          if (onEmployees) onEmployees(list);
        },
        (err) => {
          if (onError) onError(err);
        }
      );

      const logsQuery = query(
        collection(db, 'users', uid, 'attendanceLogs'),
        orderBy('createdAt', 'desc'),
        limit(100)
      );
      unsubLogs = onSnapshot(
        logsQuery,
        (snapshot) => {
          const list = snapshot.docs.map((d) => {
            const data = d.data();
            return {
              id: d.id,
              ...data,
              syncedToCloud: true,
              createdAtMs: data.createdAt?.toMillis ? data.createdAt.toMillis() : Date.now()
            };
          });
          if (onLogs) onLogs(list);
        },
        (err) => {
          if (onError) onError(err);
        }
      );
    },
    stopRealtimeSync: clearListeners,
    writeAttendanceLog
  };
}

// FILE: docs/js/pdf-report.js
